91. tattha na tathā mataṃ satarasaṃ, sudhan‍naṃ yaṃ mayaj‍ja paribhuttanti tathāti taina pakāraina| matanti abhimataṃ| satarasanti satarasabhaujanaṃ ‘‘satarasabhaujanaṃ nāma satapākasappiādīhi abhisaṅkhataṃ bhaujana’’nti vadanti| atha vā anaikatthau satasaddau ‘‘satasau sahassasau’’tiādīsu viya| tasmā yaṃ bhaujanaṃ anaikasūpaṃ anaikabyañ‍janaṃ, taṃ anaikarasatāya ‘‘satarasa’’nti vuc‍cati, nānārasabhaujananti atthau| sudhā aiva an‍naṃ sudhābhaujanaṃ daivānaṃ āhārau| yaṃ mayaj‍ja paribhuttanti yaṃ mayā aj‍ja anubhuttaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ mayā paribhutta’’nti ca idaṃ ‘‘satarasaṃ sudhan‍na’’nti aitthāpi yaujaitabbaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yaṃ mayā aj‍ja aitarahi niraudhasamāpattisamāpaj‍janavasaina phalasamāpattisamāpaj‍janavasaina ca ac‍cantamaiva santaṃ paṇītaṃ nibbānasukhaṃ paribhuñ‍jiyamānaṃ, taṃ yathā mataṃ abhimataṃ sambhāvitaṃ tathā rājakālai mayā paribhuttaṃ satarasabhaujanaṃ daivattabhāvai paribhuttaṃ sudhan‍nañ‍ca na mataṃ nābhimataṃ| kasmā? idañhi ariyanisaivitaṃ nirāmisaṃ kilaisānaṃ avatthubhūtaṃ, taṃ pana puthuj‍janasaivitaṃ sāmisaṃ kilaisānaṃ vatthubhūtaṃ, taṃ imassa saṅkhampi kalampi kalabhāgampi na upaitīti| idāni ‘‘yaṃ mayaj‍ja paribhutta’’nti vuttadhammaṃ daisaintau aparimitadassinā gautamaina, buddhaina sudaisitau dhammau’’ti āha| tassatthau – aparimitaṃ aparicchin‍naṃ uppādavayābhāvatau santaṃ asaṅkhatadhātuṃ sayambhūñāṇaina passī, aparimitassa anantāparimaiyyassa ñaiyyassa dassāvīti taina aparimitadassinā gautamagauttaina sammāsambuddhaina  ‘‘khayaṃ virāgaṃ amataṃ paṇīta’’nti (khu॰ pā॰ 6.4; su॰ ni॰ 227) ca ‘‘madanimmadanau pipāsavinayau’’ (a॰ ni॰ 4.34; itivu॰ 90) ‘‘sabbasaṅkhārasamathau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.140; 10.6) ca ādinā suṭṭhu daisitau dhammau, nibbānaṃ mayā aj‍ja paribhuttanti yaujanā|

paripuṇṇakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. vijayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yassāsavā  parikkhīṇāti āyasmatau vijayatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau piyadassissa bhagavatau kālai vibhavasampan‍nai kulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari parinibbutai tassa thūpassa ratanakhacitaṃ vaidikaṃ kāraitvā tattha uḷāraṃ vaidikāmahaṃ  kāraisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina anaikasatai attabhāvai maṇiaubhāsaina vicari| aivaṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbatti, vijayautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau brāhmaṇavij‍jāsu nipphattiṃ gatau tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā arañ‍ñāyatanai jhānalābhī hutvā viharantau buddhuppādaṃ sutvā uppan‍nappasādau satthu santikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā satthāraṃ vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| tassa satthā dhammaṃ daisaisi| sau dhammaṃ sutvā pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.10-14) –

‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, piyadassīnaruttamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, muttāvaidimakāsahaṃ||

‘‘maṇīhi parivāraitvā, akāsiṃ vaidimuttamaṃ|

vaidikāya mahaṃ katvā, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

maṇī dhārainti ākāsai, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sauḷasitau kappasatai, maṇippabhāsanāmakā|

chattiṃsāsiṃsu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau ‘‘yassāsavā parikkhīṇā’’ti gāthaṃ abhāsi|

92. tattha yassāsavā parikkhīṇāti yassa uttamapuggalassa kāmāsavādayau cattārau āsavā sabbasau khīṇā ariyamaggaina khaipitā| āhārai ca anissitauti yau ca āhārai taṇhādiṭṭhinissayaihi anissitau agadhitau anajjhāpan‍nau, nidassanamattaṃ, āhārasīsainaittha cattāraupi pac‍cayā gahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| pac‍cayapariyāyau vā idha āhāra-saddau vaiditabbau| ‘‘suñ‍ñatau animittau cā’’ti aittha appaṇihitavimaukkhaupi gahitauyaiva, tīṇipi caitāni nibbānassaiva nāmāni| nibbānañhi rāgādīnaṃ abhāvaina suñ‍ñaṃ, taihi vimuttañ‍cāti suñ‍ñatavimaukkhau, tathā rāgādinimittābhāvaina saṅkhāranimittābhāvaina ca animittaṃ, taihi vimuttañ‍cāti animittavimaukkhau, rāgādipaṇidhīnaṃ abhāvaina appaṇihitaṃ, taihi vimuttañ‍cāti appaṇihitau vimaukkhauti vuc‍cati| phalasamāpattivasaina taṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā viharantassa ayampi tividhau vimaukkhau yassa gaucarau, ākāsaiva sakuntānaṃ, padaṃ tassa duran‍nayanti yathā ākāsai  gacchantānaṃ sakuṇānaṃ ‘‘imasmiṃ ṭhānai pādaihi ak‍kamitvā gatā, idaṃ ṭhānaṃ uraina paharitvā gatā, idaṃ sīsaina, idaṃ pakkhaihī’’ti na sak‍kā ñātuṃ, aivamaiva aivarūpassa bhikkhunau ‘‘nirayapadādīsu iminā nāma padaina gatau’’ti ñāpaituñ‍ca na sak‍kāti|

vijayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. airakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

dukkhā kāmā airakāti āyasmatau airakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau satthu kiñ‍ci dātabbayuttakaṃ alabhantau ‘‘handāhaṃ kāyasāraṃ puñ‍ñaṃ karissāmī’’ti satthu gamanamaggaṃ saudhaitvā samaṃ akāsi| satthā taina tathākataṃ maggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji| sau tattha bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau vanditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggayha pasan‍nacittau yāva dassanupacārasamatik‍kamā buddhārammaṇaṃ pītiṃ avijahantau aṭṭhāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattau aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ sambhāvanīyassa kuṭumbiyassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, airakautissa nāmaṃ ahausi abhirūpau dassanīyau pāsādikau itikattabbatāsu paramaina vaiyyattiyaina saman‍nāgatau| tassa mātāpitarau kulaina rūpaina ācāraina vayaina kausal‍laina ca anucchavikaṃ dārikaṃ ānaitvā vivāhakammaṃ akaṃsu| sau tāya saddhiṃ saṃvāsaina gaihai vasantau pacchimabhavikattā kainacidaiva saṃvaigavatthunā saṃsārai saṃviggamānasau satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbaji, tassa satthā kammaṭṭhānaṃ adāsi| sau kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā katipayadivasātik‍kamaina uk‍kaṇṭhābhibhūtau vihāsi| atha satthā tassa cittappavattiṃ ñatvā auvādavasaina ‘‘dukkhā kāmā airakā’’ti gāthaṃ abhāsi| sau taṃ sutvā ‘‘ayuttaṃ mayā kataṃ, yauhaṃ aivarūpassa satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā taṃ vissaj‍jaintau micchāvitak‍kabahulau vihāsi’’nti saṃvaigajātau vipassanāya yuttappayuttau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.32-36) –

‘‘uttaritvāna nadikaṃ, vanaṃ gacchati cakkhumā|

tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, siddhatthaṃ varalakkhaṇaṃ||

‘‘kudālapiṭakamādāya, samaṃ katvāna taṃ pathaṃ|

satthāraṃ abhivādaitvā, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maggadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

nāmaina suppabuddhauti, nāyakau sau narissarau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahā pana hutvā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

93.

 ‘‘dukkhā  kāmā airaka, na sukhā kāmā airaka|

yau kāmai kāmayati, dukkhaṃ sau kāmayati airaka|

yau kāmai na kāmayati, dukkhaṃ sau na kāmayati airakā’’ti|| –

tamaiva bhagavatā vuttagāthaṃ pac‍cudāhāsi|

tattha dukkhā kāmāti imai vatthukāmakilaisakāmā dukkhavatthutāya vipariṇāmadukkhasaṃsāradukkhasabhāvatau ca, dukkhā dukkhamā dukkhanibbattikā| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘appassādā kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’’tiādi (pāci॰ 417; ma॰ ni॰ 1.234)| airakāti paṭhamaṃ tāva bhagavā taṃ ālapati, pacchā pana thairau attānaṃ nāmaina kathaisi| na sukhā kāmāti kāmā nāmaitai jānantassa sukhā na haunti, ajānantassa pana sukhatau upaṭṭhahanti| yathāha – ‘‘yau sukhaṃ dukkhatau adda, dukkhamaddakkhi sal‍latau’’tiādi (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.253; itivu॰ 53; thairagā॰ 986)| yau kāmai kāmayati, dukkhaṃ sau kāmayatīti yau sattau kilaisakāmaina vatthukāmai kāmayati, tassa taṃ kāmanaṃ sampati sapariḷāhatāya, āyatiṃ apāyadukkhahaitutāya ca vaṭṭadukkhahaitutāya ca dukkhaṃ| vatthukāmā pana dukkhassa vatthubhūtā| iti sau dukkhasabhāvaṃ dukkhanimittaṃ dukkhavatthuñ‍ca kāmayatīti vuttau| itaraṃ paṭipakkhavasaina tamaivatthaṃ ñāpaituṃ vuttaṃ, tasmā tassatthau vuttavipariyāyaina vaiditabbau|

airakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. maittajitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

namau  hi tassa bhagavatauti āyasmatau maittajitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? sau kira anaumadassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau sāsanai abhippasan‍nau hutvā baudhirukkhassa iṭṭhakāhi vaidikaṃ cinitvā sudhāparikammaṃ kāraisi| satthā tassa anumaudanaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, maittajītissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā arañ‍ñai viharantau buddhuppādaṃ sutvā pubbahaitunā caudiyamānau satthu santikaṃ gantvā pavattinivattiyau ārabbha pañhaṃ pucchitvā satthārā pañhai vissaj‍jitai paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.26-31) –

‘‘anaumadassīmuninau, baudhivaidimakāsahaṃ|

sudhāya piṇḍaṃ datvāna, pāṇikammaṃ akāsahaṃ||

‘‘disvā taṃ sukataṃ kammaṃ, anaumadassī naruttamau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘iminā sudhakammaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

sampattiṃ anubhautvāna, dukkhassantaṃ karissati||

‘‘pasan‍namukhavaṇṇaumhi, aikaggau susamāhitau|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

‘‘itau kappasatai āsiṃ, paripuṇṇai anūnakai|

rājā sabbaghanau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā satthāraṃ thaumaintau –

94.

 ‘‘namau hi tassa bhagavatau, sakyaputtassa sirīmatau|

taināyaṃ aggappattaina, aggadhammau sudaisitau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ  abhāsi|

tattha  namauti namak‍kārau| hīti nipātamattaṃ| tassāti yau sau bhagavā samattiṃsapāramiyau pūraitvā  sabbakilaisai bhañ‍jitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau, sakyarājassa puttauti sakyaputtau| anañ‍ñasādhāraṇāya puñ‍ñasampattiyā ca sambhāvitau uttamāya rūpakāyasiriyā dhammakāyasiriyā ca saman‍nāgatattā sirīmā, tassa bhagavatau sakyaputtassa sirīmatau namau atthu, taṃ namāmīti atthau| taināti taina bhagavatā| ayanti tassa dhammassa attanau pac‍cakkhatāya vadati| aggappattaināti aggaṃ sabbañ‍ñutaṃ, sabbaihi vā guṇaihi aggabhāvaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ pattaina| aggadhammauti aggau uttamau navavidhalaukuttarau dhammau suṭṭhu aviparītaṃ daisitau pavaiditauti|

maittajitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. cakkhupālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

andhauhaṃ hatanaittausmīti āyasmatau cakkhupālatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? saupi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bhagavati parinibbutai thūpamahai kayiramānai umāpupphaṃ gahaitvā thūpaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ mahāsuvaṇṇassa nāma kuṭumbikassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, tassa pālauti nāmamakaṃsu| mātā tassa ādhāvitvā paridhāvitvā vicaraṇakālai añ‍ñaṃ puttaṃ labhi| tassa mātāpitarau cūḷapālauti nāmaṃ katvā itaraṃ mahāpālauti vauhariṃsu| atha tai vayappattai gharabandhanaina bandhiṃsu| tasmiṃ samayai satthā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai| tattha mahāpālau jaitavanaṃ gacchantaihi upāsakaihi saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gantvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau kuṭumbabhāraṃ kaniṭṭhabhātikassaiva bhāraṃ katvā sayaṃ pabbajitvā laddhūpasampadau ācariyupajjhāyānaṃ santikai  pañ‍cavassāni vasitvā vuṭṭhavassau pavāraitvā satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā saṭṭhimattai  sahāyabhikkhū labhitvā taihi saddhiṃ bhāvanānukūlaṃ vasanaṭṭhānaṃ pariyaisantau añ‍ñataraṃ pac‍cantagāmaṃ nissāya gāmavāsikaihi upāsakaihi kāraitvā din‍nāya arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇasālāya vasantau samaṇadhammaṃ karauti| tassa akkhiraugau uppan‍nau| vaij‍jau bhaisaj‍jaṃ sampādaitvā adāsi| sau vaij‍jaina vuttavidhānaṃ na paṭipaj‍ji| tainassa raugau vaḍḍhi| sau ‘‘akkhiraugavūpasamanatau kilaisaraugavūpasamanamaiva mayhaṃ vara’’nti akkhiraugaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā vipassanāyayaiva yuttappayuttau ahausi| tassa bhāvanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaintassa apubbaṃ acarimaṃ akkhīni caiva kilaisā ca bhij‍jiṃsu| sau sukkhavipassakau arahā ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.21-25) –

‘‘nibbutai  laukamahitai, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahai|

siddhatthamhi bhagavati, mahāthūpamahau ahu||

‘‘mahai pavattamānamhi, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

umāpupphaṃ gahaitvāna, thūpamhi abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau ca navamai kappai, saumadaivasanāmakā|

pañ‍cāsītisu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

atha thairai akkhiraugaina vihārai auhīnai gāmaṃ piṇḍāya gatai bhikkhū disvā upāsakā ‘‘kasmā thairau nāgatau’’ti pucchitvā tamatthaṃ sutvā saukābhibhūtā piṇḍapātaṃ upanaitvā, ‘‘bhantai, kiñ‍ci mā cintayittha, idāni mayamaiva piṇḍapātaṃ ānaitvā upaṭṭhahissāmā’’ti tathā karaunti| bhikkhū thairassa auvādai ṭhatvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ patvā vuṭṭhavassā pavāraitvā, ‘‘satthāraṃ vandituṃ sāvatthiṃ gamissāma, bhantai’’ti āhaṃsu| thairau, ‘‘ahaṃ dubbalau acakkhukau, maggau ca saupaddavau, mayā saddhiṃ gacchantānaṃ tumhākaṃ parissayau bhavissati, tumhai paṭhamaṃ gacchatha, gantvā satthāraṃ mahāthairai ca mama vandanāya vandatha, cūḷapālassa mama  pavattiṃ kathaitvā kañ‍ci purisaṃ paisaiyyāthā’’ti āha| tai punapi yācitvā gamanaṃ alabhantā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā upāsakai āpucchitvā anuk‍kamaina jaitavanaṃ gantvā satthāraṃ mahāthairai ca tassa vandanāya vanditvā dutiyadivasai sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā  cūḷapālassa taṃ pavattiṃ vatvā taina ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, mayhaṃ bhāginaiyyau pālitau nāma, imaṃ paisissāmī’’ti vuttai, ‘‘maggau saparissayau, na sak‍kā aikaina gahaṭṭhaina gantuṃ, tasmā pabbājaitabbau’’ti taṃ pabbājaitvā paisaisuṃ| sau anuk‍kamaina thairassa santikaṃ gantvā attānaṃ tassa auraucaitvā taṃ gahaitvā āgacchantau antarāmaggai añ‍ñatarassa gāmassa sāmantā arañ‍ñaṭṭhānai aikissā kaṭṭhahāriyā gāyantiyā saddaṃ sutvā paṭibaddhacittau hutvā yaṭṭhikauṭiṃ vissaj‍jaitvā ‘‘tiṭṭhatha, bhantai, muhuttaṃ yāvāhaṃ āgacchāmī’’ti vatvā tassā santikaṃ gantvā tattha sīlavipattiṃ pāpuṇi| thairau idānimaiva itthiyā gītasaddau sutau, sāmaṇairau ca cirāyati, nūna sīlavipattiṃ pattau bhavissatī’’ti cintaisi| saupi āgantvā ‘‘gacchāma, bhantai’’ti āha| thairau ‘‘kiṃ pāpau jātausī’’ti pucchi| sāmaṇairau tuṇhī hutvā puna pucchitaupi na kathaisi| thairau ‘‘tādisaina pāpaina mayhaṃ yaṭṭhigahaṇakic‍caṃ natthi, gaccha tva’’nti vatvā puna taina ‘‘bahuparissayau  maggau, tumhai ca andhā, kathaṃ gamissathā’’ti vuttai ‘‘bāla idhaiva mai nipaj‍jitvā marantassāpi aparāparaṃ parivattaintassāpi tādisaina gamanaṃ nāma natthī’’ti imamatthaṃ dassaintau –

95.

 ‘‘andhauhaṃ hatanaittausmi, kantāraddhānapakkhandau|

sayamānaupi gacchissaṃ, na sahāyaina pāpainā’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsittha|

tattha andhauti cakkhuvikalau| hatanaittauti vinaṭṭhacakkhukau, taina ‘‘payaugavipattivasaināhaṃ upahatanaittatāya andhau, na jac‍candhabhāvainā’’ti yathāvuttaṃ andhabhāvaṃ visaisaiti| atha vā ‘‘andhau’’ti idaṃ ‘‘andhai  jiṇṇai mātāpitarau pausaitī’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 2.288) viya maṃsacakkhuvaikal‍ladīpanaṃ, ‘‘sabbaipimai paribbājakā andhā acakkhukā’’ (udā॰ 54) ‘‘andhau aikacakkhu dvicakkhū’’tiādīsu (a॰ ni॰ 3.29) viya na pañ‍ñācakkhuvaikal‍ladīpananti dassaituṃ ‘‘hatanaittausmī’’ti vuttaṃ, taina mukhyamaiva andhabhāvaṃ dassaiti| kantāraddhānapakkhandauti  kantārai vivanai dīghamaggaṃ anupaviṭṭhau, na jātikantārādigahanaṃ saṃsāraddhānaṃ paṭipan‍nauti adhippāyau| tādisañhi kantāraddhānaṃ ayaṃ thairau samatik‍kamitvā ṭhitau, sayamānaupīti sayantaupi, pādaisu avahantaisu uraina jaṇṇukāhi ca bhūmiyaṃ saṃsarantau parivattaintaupi gacchaiyyaṃ| na sahāyaina pāpaināti tādisaina pāpapuggalaina sahāyabhūtaina saddhiṃ na gacchissanti yaujanā| taṃ sutvā itarau saṃvaigajātau ‘‘bhāriyaṃ vata mayā sāhasikakammaṃ kata’’nti bāhā paggayha kandantau vanasaṇḍaṃ pakkhandau ca ahausi| atha thairassa sīlataijaina paṇḍukambalasilāsanaṃ uṇhākāraṃ dassaisi| taina sak‍kau taṃ kāraṇaṃ ñatvā thairassa santikaṃ gantvā sāvatthigāmipurisaṃ viya attānaṃ ñāpaitvā yaṭṭhikauṭiṃ gaṇhantau maggaṃ saṅkhipitvā tadahaiva sāyanhai sāvatthiyaṃ thairaṃ naitvā tattha jaitavanai cūḷapālitaina kāritāya paṇṇasālāya phalakai nisīdāpaitvā tassa sahāyavaṇṇaina thairassa āgatabhāvaṃ jānāpaitvā pak‍kāmi; cūḷapālitaupi taṃ yāvajīvaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ upaṭṭhāsīti|

cakkhupālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. khaṇḍasumanatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

aikapupphaṃ  cajitvānāti āyasmatau khaṇḍasumanatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? sau kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari parinibbutai tassa thūpassa samantatau candanavaidikāya parikkhipitvā mahantaṃ pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu uḷāraṃ sampattiṃ anubhavantau kassapassa bhagavatau kālai kuṭumbikakulai  nibbattau satthari parinibbutai kanakathūpaṃ uddissa rañ‍ñā pupphapūjāya kayiramānāya pupphāni alabhantau aikaṃ khaṇḍasumanapupphaṃ disvā mahatā mūlaina  taṃ kiṇitvā gaṇhantau caitiyai pūjaṃ karauntau uḷāraṃ pītisaumanassaṃ uppādaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā asīti vassakauṭiyau saggasukhaṃ anubhavitvā imasmiṃ buddhuppādai pāvāyaṃ mal‍larājakulai nibbatti| tassa jātakālai gaihai khaṇḍasakkharā sumanapupphāni ca uppan‍nāni ahaisuṃ| tainassa khaṇḍasumanauti nāmamakaṃsu| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bhagavati pāvāyaṃ cundassa ambavanai viharantai upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.15-20) –

‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

jalitvā aggikkhandhauva, sambuddhau parinibbutau||

‘‘nibbutai ca mahāvīrai, thūpau vitthārikau ahu|

dūratauva upaṭṭhainti, dhātugaihavaruttamai||

‘‘pasan‍nacittau, sumanau, akaṃ candanavaidikaṃ|

dissati thūpakhandhau ca, thūpānucchavikau tadā||

‘‘bhavai nibbattamānamhi, daivattai atha mānusai|

aumattaṃ mai na passāmi, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘pañ‍cadasakappasatai, itau aṭṭha janā ahuṃ|

sabbai samattanāmā tai cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau purimajātiṃ anussarantau tattha attanau sumanapupphaparic‍cāgassa saggasampattinimittakaṃ nibbānūpanissayatañ‍ca disvā udānavasaina tamatthaṃ pakāsaintau –

96.

 ‘‘aikapupphaṃ  cajitvāna, asīti vassakauṭiyau|

saggaisu paricāraitvā, saisakainamhi nibbutau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha  aikapupphanti aikaṃ kusumaṃ, taṃ pana idha sumanapupphaṃ adhippaitaṃ| cajitvānāti satthu thūpapūjākaraṇavasaina paric‍cajitvā paric‍cāgahaitu| asīti vassakauṭiyauti manussagaṇanāya vassānaṃ asīti kauṭiyau, ac‍cantasaṃyaugai caitaṃ upayaugavacanaṃ, idañ‍ca chasu kāmasaggaisu dutiyai aparāparuppattivasaina vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| tasmā saggaisūti tāvatiṃsasaṅkhātai saggalaukai, punappunaṃ uppaj‍janavasaina haittha bahuvacanaṃ| paricāraitvāti rūpādīsu ārammaṇaisu indriyāni paricāraitvā sukhaṃ anubhavitvā, daivaccharāhi vā attānaṃ paricāraitvā  upaṭṭhāpaitvā| saisakainamhi nibbutauti pupphapūjāya vasaina pavattakusalacaitanāsu bhavasampatti dāyakakammatau saisaina yaṃ tattha vivaṭṭūpanissayabhūtaṃ, taṃ sandhāya vadati| bahū hi tattha pubbāparavasaina pavattā caitanā| saisakaināti vā tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisaina aparikkhīṇaiyaiva tasmiṃ kammavipākai nibbutau amhi, kilaisaparinibbānaina parinibbutausmi| aitaina yasmiṃ attabhāvai ṭhatvā attanā arahattaṃ sacchikataṃ, saupi carimattabhāvau tassa kammavipākauti dassaiti| yādisaṃ sandhāya añ‍ñatthāpi ‘‘tassaiva kammassa vipākāvasaisainā’’ti (pārā॰ 228; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.131) vuttaṃ|

khaṇḍasumanatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. tissatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

hitvā satapalaṃ kaṃsanti āyasmatau tissatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai yānakārakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau candanakhaṇḍaina phalakaṃ katvā bhagavatau upanāmaisi, tañ‍ca bhagavā paribhuñ‍ji| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rauruvanagarai rājakulai nibbatti| sau vayappattau pitari kālaṅkatai raj‍jai patiṭṭhitau bimbisārarañ‍ñau adiṭṭhasahāyau hutvā tassa maṇimuttāvatthādīni paṇṇākārāni paisaisi| tassa rājā bimbisārau puñ‍ñavantataṃ  sutvā paṭipābhataṃ paisaintau cittapaṭai buddhacaritaṃ  suvaṇṇapaṭṭai ca paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ likhāpaitvā paisaisi| sau taṃ disvā purimabuddhaisu katādhikāratāya pacchimabhavikatāya ca cittapaṭai dassaintaṃ buddhacaritaṃ suvaṇṇapaṭṭakai likhitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādak‍kamañ‍ca aulaukaitvā  pavattinivattiyau sal‍lakkhaitvā sāsanak‍kamaṃ hadayai ṭhapaitvā sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau ‘‘diṭṭhau mayā bhagavatau vaisau, sāsanak‍kamau ca aikapadaisaina ñātau, bahudukkhā kāmā bahupāyāsā, kiṃ dāni mayhaṃ gharāvāsainā’’ti raj‍jaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaintau bhagavantaṃ uddissa pabbajitvā mattikāpattaṃ gahaitvā rājā puk‍kusāti viya mahājanassa paridaivantassaiva nagaratau nikkhamitvā anuk‍kamaina rājagahaṃ gantvā tattha sappasauṇḍikapabbhārai viharantaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| satthā dhammaṃ daisaisi| sau dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā vipassanāya kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā yuttappayuttau viharantau vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.37-42) –

‘‘yānakārau purai āsiṃ, dārukammai susikkhitau|

candanaṃ phalakaṃ katvā, adāsiṃ laukabandhunau||

‘‘pabhāsati idaṃ byamhaṃ, suvaṇṇassa sunimmitaṃ|

hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, dibbayānaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ||

‘‘pāsādā sivikā caiva, nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ|

akkhubbhaṃ ratanaṃ mayhaṃ, phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, phalakaṃ yamahaṃ dadiṃ|

duggati nābhijānāmi, phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, caturau nimmitāvhayā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā udānavasaina attanau paṭipattiṃ kathaintau –

97.

 ‘‘hitvā  satapalaṃ kaṃsaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ satarājikaṃ|

aggahiṃ mattikāpattaṃ, idaṃ dutiyābhisaicana’’nti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha hitvāti paric‍cajitvā| satapalanti sataṃ palāni yassa, taṃ satapalaparimāṇaṃ| kaṃsanti thālaṃ| sauvaṇṇanti suvaṇṇamayaṃ| satarājikanti bhittivicittatāya ca anaikarūparājicittatāya ca anaikalaikhāyuttaṃ| aggahiṃ mattikāpattanti aivarūpai mahārahai bhājanai pubbai  bhuñ‍jitvā buddhānaṃ auvādaṃ karauntau ‘‘idānāhaṃ taṃ chaḍḍaitvā mattikāmayapattaṃ aggahaisiṃ, ahau, sādhu, mayā kataṃ ariyavataṃ anuṭhita’’nti bhājanakittanāpadaisaina raj‍japaric‍cāgaṃ pabbaj‍jūpagamanañ‍ca anumaudantau vadati| taināha ‘‘idaṃ dutiyābhisaicana’’nti| paṭhamaṃ raj‍jābhisaicanaṃ upādāya idaṃ pabbaj‍jūpagamanaṃ mama dutiyaṃ abhisaicanaṃ| tañhi rāgādīhi saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ sāsaṅkaṃ saparisaṅkaṃ kammaṃ anatthasañhitaṃ dukkhapaṭibaddhaṃ nihīnaṃ, idaṃ pana taṃvipariyāyatau uttamaṃ paṇītanti adhippāyau|

tissatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. abhayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

rūpaṃ disvā sati muṭṭhāti āyasmatau abhayatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? saupi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau sumaidhassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ sumaidhaṃ bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau saḷalapupphaihi pūjamakāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivaisu nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā abhayauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau haitusampattiyā caudiyamānau aikadivasaṃ vihāraṃ gatau satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā katapubbakic‍cau vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau viharati| athassa aikadivasaṃ gāmaṃ  piṇḍāya paviṭṭhassa alaṅkatapaṭiyattaṃ mātugāmaṃ disvā ayaunisaumanasikāravasaina tassa rūpaṃ ārabbha chandarāgau uppaj‍ji, sau vihāraṃ pavisitvā ‘‘satiṃ vissaj‍jitvā aulaukaintassa rūpārammaṇai mayhaṃ kilaisau uppan‍nau, ayuttaṃ mayā kata’’nti attanau cittaṃ niggaṇhantau tāvadaiva vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.43-47) –

‘‘sumaidhau  nāma nāmaina, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikamanubrūhantau, ajjhaugahi mahāvanaṃ||

‘‘saḷalaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, ganthitvāna vaṭaṃsakaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sammukhā laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘ūnavīsai  kappasatai, sauḷasāsuṃ sunimmitā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau kilaisuppattinidassanaina ‘‘kilaisai anuvattaintassa vaṭṭadukkhatau natthaiva sīsukkhipanaṃ| ahaṃ pana tai nānuvatti’’nti dassaintau –

98.

 ‘‘rūpaṃ disvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati|

tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā, bhavamūlaupagāminau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha rūpanti raj‍janīyaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ, taṃ panaittha itthirūpaṃ adhippaitaṃ| disvāti cakkhunā disvā, cakkhudvārānusāraina nimittānubyañ‍janasal‍lakkhaṇavasaina taṃ gahaitvā, tassa tathāgahaṇahaitūti atthau| sati muṭṭhāti asubhasabhāvai kāyai ‘‘asubha’’ntvaiva pavattanasati naṭṭhā| yathā pana rūpaṃ disvā sati naṭṭhā, taṃ dassaintau āha ‘‘piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau’’ti| yathāupaṭṭhitaṃ ārammaṇaṃ ‘‘subhaṃ sukha’’ntiādinā piyanimittaṃ katvā  ayaunisaumanasikāraina manasikarautau sati muṭṭhāti yaujanā| tathā bhūtauva sārattacittau vaidaitīti suṭṭhu rattacittau hutvā taṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ anubhavati abhinandati, abhinandantau pana tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati ajjhausāya taṃ ārammaṇaṃ gilitvā pariniṭṭhapaitvā vattati caiva, aivaṃbhūtassa ca tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā bhavamūlaupagāminauti bhavassa saṃsārassa mūlabhāvaṃ kāraṇabhāvaṃ upagamanasabhāvā kāmāsavādayau cattāraupi āsavā tassa puggalassa uparūpari vaḍḍhantiyaiva, na hāyanti| mayhaṃ pana paṭisaṅkhānai ṭhatvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā sac‍cāni paṭivijjhantassa maggapaṭipāṭiyā tai cattāraupi āsavā anavasaisatau pahīnā parikkhīṇāti adhippāyau|

abhayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. uttiyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

saddaṃ  sutvā sati muṭṭhāti āyasmatau uttiyatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau sumaidhassa bhagavatau kālai  kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau gaunakādiatthataṃ sauttaracchadaṃ buddhārahaṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ gandhakuṭiyaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kapilavatthusmiṃ sakyarājakulai nibbatti, tassa uttiyauti nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau satthu ñātisamāgamai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau aikadivasaṃ nāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhau antarāmaggai mātugāmassa gītasaddaṃ sutvā ayaunisaumanasikāravasaina tattha chandarāgai uppan‍nai paṭisaṅkhānabalaina taṃ vikkhambhaitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau divāṭṭhānai nisīditvā tāvadaiva vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.15.48-52) –

‘‘sumaidhassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

pal‍laṅkau hi mayā din‍nau, sauttarasapacchadau||

‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nau, pal‍laṅkau āsi sau tadā|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, nibbattati sadā mama||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, pal‍laṅkamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pal‍laṅkassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘vīsakappasahassamhi, suvaṇṇābhā tayau janā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau kilaisuppattinidassanaina ‘‘kilaisai ajigucchantassa natthi vaṭṭadukkhatau sīsukkhipanaṃ, ahaṃ pana tai jigucchimaivā’’ti dassaintau –

99.

 ‘‘saddaṃ sutvā sati muṭṭhā, piyaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau|

sārattacittau vaidaiti, tañ‍ca ajjhausa tiṭṭhati|

tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā, saṃsāra upagāminau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha saddanti raj‍janīyaṃ saddārammaṇaṃ, saṃsāraupagāminauti –

‘‘khandhānañ‍ca paṭipāṭi, dhātuāyatanāna ca|

abbaucchin‍naṃ vattamānā, saṃsārauti pavuc‍catī’’ti|| –

aivaṃ  vuttasaṃsāravaṭṭakāraṇaṃ hutvā upagamaintīti saṃsāraupagāminau, ‘‘saṃsārūpagāminau’’ti vā pāṭhau| saisaṃ anantaragāthāya vuttanayamaiva|

uttiyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. (dutiya) daivasabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

sammappadhānasampan‍nauti  āyasmatau daivasabhatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinantau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ sikhiṃ bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau bandhujīvakapupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kapilavatthusmiṃ sakyarājakulai nibbatti, tassa daivasabhauti nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau cumbaṭakalahavūpasamanatthaṃ satthari āgatai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau saraṇaisu patiṭṭhitau puna nigraudhārāmai satthari viharantai satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā katapubbakic‍cau vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.1-6) –

‘‘candaṃva vimalaṃ suddhaṃ, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ|

nandībhavaparikkhīṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ laukai visattikaṃ||

‘‘nibbāpayantaṃ janataṃ, tiṇṇaṃ tārayataṃ varaṃ|

muniṃ vanamhi jhāyantaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

‘‘bandhujīvakapupphāni, lagaitvā suttakainahaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau sattamakai kappai, manujindau mahāyasau|

samantacakkhunāmāsi, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā attanā adhigataṃ vimuttisukhaṃ nissāya uppan‍napītisaumanassau udānavasaina –

100.

 ‘‘sammappadhānasampan‍nau, satipaṭṭhānagaucarau|

vimuttikusumasañchan‍nau, parinibbissatyanāsavau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha sammappadhānasampan‍nauti sampan‍nacatubbidhasammappadhānau, taihi kattabbakic‍caṃ sampādaitvā ṭhitauti atthau| satipaṭṭhānagaucarauti kāyānupassanādayau satipaṭṭhānā gaucarau  pavattiṭṭhānaṃ aitassāti satipaṭṭhānagaucarau, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu patiṭṭhitacittauti atthau| guṇasaubhaina paramasugandhā vimuttiyaiva kusumāni, taihi sabbasau sammadaiva sañchan‍nau vibhūsitau alaṅkatauti vimuttikusumasañchan‍nau| parinibbissatyanāsavauti aivaṃ sammā paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu nacirassaiva anāsavau hutvā parinibbissati saupādisaisāya anupādisaisāya ca nibbānadhātuyāti atthau| idamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausi|

(dutiya) daivasabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

dasamavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

11. aikādasamavaggau

1. bailaṭṭhānikatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

hitvā  gihittaṃ anavausitattautiādikā āyasmatau bailaṭṭhānikatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau itau aikatiṃsai kappai vaissabhussa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vayappattau brāhmaṇasippaisu nipphattiṃ gantvā gharāvāsaṃ pahāya isipabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā isīhi parivutau vicarantau aikadivasaṃ vaissabhuṃ bhagavantaṃ disvā pītisaumanassajātau satthu ñāṇasampattiṃ nissāya pasan‍namānasau ñāṇaṃ uddissa pupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā bailaṭṭhānikauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ  pattau satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā kausalaraṭṭhai arañ‍ñai viharantau alasau kāyadaḷhibahulau pharusavācau ahausi, samaṇadhammai cittaṃ na uppādaisi| atha naṃ bhagavā ñāṇaparipākaṃ aulaukaitvā –

101.

 ‘‘hitvā gihittaṃ anavausitattau, mukhanaṅgalī audarikau kusītau|

mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti|| –

imāya  aubhāsagāthāya saṃvaijaisi| sau satthāraṃ puratau nisin‍naṃ viya disvā tañ‍ca gāthaṃ sutvā saṃvaigajātau ñāṇassa paripākaṃ gatattā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.14.41-46) –

‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, ausadhiṃ viya tārakaṃ||

‘‘tayau māṇavakā āsuṃ, sakai sippai susikkhitā|

khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, anvainti mama pacchatau||

‘‘puṭakai satta pupphāni, nikkhittāni tapassinā|

gahaitvā tāni ñāṇamhi, vaissabhussābhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aikūnatiṃsakappamhi, vipulābhasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā satthu auvādaṃ paṭipūjaintau byatiraikamukhaina ca añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau tamaiva gāthaṃ pac‍cudāhāsi|

tattha  hitvā gihittanti gahaṭṭhabhāvaṃ paric‍cajitvā pabbajitvāti atthau| anavausitattauti anurūpaṃ avausitattau, yadatthaṃ sāsanai pabbajantassa anurūpapariñ‍ñādīnaṃ atīritattā apariyausitabhāvau akatakaraṇīyauti atthau| atha vā anavausitattauti anuavausitasabhāvau, visuddhīnaṃ maggānañ‍ca anupaṭipāṭiyā vasitabbavāsassa akatāvī, dasasu ariyavāsaisu avusitavāti atthau| mukhasaṅkhātaṃ naṅgalaṃ imassa atthīti mukhanaṅgalī| naṅgalaina viya pathaviṃ paraisu pharusavācappayaugaina attānaṃ khanantauti atthau| audarikauti udarai pasutau udarapausanatapparau| kusītauti alasau, bhāvanaṃ ananuyuñ‍jantau| aivaṃbhūtassa nipphattiṃ dassaintau āha ‘‘mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| aittha ca yathā pabbajitvā anavausitādisabhāvatāya punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau, na aivaṃ mādisau paṇḍitau| tabbiparītasabhāvatāya pana sammāpaṭipattiyā matthakaṃ pāpitattā parinibbāyatīti byatiraikamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsīti daṭṭhabbanti|

bailaṭṭhānikatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. saitucchatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

mānaina  vañ‍citāsaiti āyasmatau saitucchatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau tissassa sammāsambuddhassa kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ tissaṃ bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau sumadhuraṃ panasaphalaṃ abhisaṅkhataṃ nāḷikairasāḷavaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai añ‍ñatarassa maṇḍalikarañ‍ñau  puttau hutvā nibbatti, saitucchautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau pitari matai raj‍jai patiṭṭhitau ussāhasattīnaṃ abhāvaina rājakic‍cāni virādhaintau raj‍jaṃ parahatthagataṃ katvā dukkhappattiyā saṃvaigajātau janapadacārikaṃ carantaṃ bhagavantaṃ  disvā upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā parikammaṃ karauntau tadahaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.17.13-17) –

‘‘tissassa khau bhagavatau, pubbai phalamadāsahaṃ|

nāḷikairañ‍ca pādāsiṃ, khaj‍jakaṃ abhisammataṃ||

‘‘buddhassa tamahaṃ datvā, tissassa tu mahaisinau|

maudāmahaṃ kāmakāmī, upapaj‍jiṃ yamicchakaṃ||

‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau tairasakappamhi, rājā indasamau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā kilaisai garahantau –

102.

 ‘‘mānaina vañ‍citāsai, saṅkhāraisu saṃkilissamānāsai|

lābhālābhaina mathitā, samādhiṃ nādhigacchantī’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha mānaina vañ‍citāsaiti ‘‘saiyyauhamasmī’’tiādinayappavattaina mānaina attuk‍kaṃsanaparavambhanādivasaina kusalabhaṇḍacchaidanaina vippaladdhā| saṅkhāraisu saṃkilissamānāsaiti ajjhattikabāhiraisu cakkhādīsu caiva rūpādīsu ca saṅkhatadhammaisu saṃkilissamānā, ‘‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti taṃnimittaṃ taṇhāgāhādivasaina saṃkilaisaṃ āpaj‍jamānā| lābhālābhaina mathitāti pattacīvarādīnañ‍caiva  vatthādīnañ‍ca lābhaina taisaṃyaiva ca alābhaina taṃnimittaṃ uppan‍naihi anunayapaṭighaihi mathitā madditā abhibhūtā| nidassanamattañ‍caitaṃ avasiṭṭhalaukadhammānampaittha saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| samādhiṃ nādhigacchantīti tai aivarūpā puggalā samādhiṃ samathavipassanāvasaina cittaikaggataṃ kadācipi na vindanti na paṭilabhanti na pāpuṇanti samādhisaṃvattanikānaṃ dhammānaṃ abhāvatau, itaraisañ‍ca bhāvatau| idhāpi yathā mānādīhi abhibhūtā  aviddasunau samādhiṃ  nādhigacchanti, na aivaṃ viddasunau| tai pana mādisā taihi anabhibhūtā samādhiṃ adhigacchantaivāti byatiraikamukhaina añ‍ñābyākaraṇanti vaiditabbaṃ|

saitucchatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. bandhuratthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nāhaṃ aitaina atthikauti āyasmatau bandhuratthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai añ‍ñatarassa rañ‍ñau antaipurai gaupakau hutvā aikadivasaṃ bhagavantaṃ saparisaṃ rājaṅgaṇaina gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau kaṇavairapupphāni gahaitvā sasaṅghaṃ laukanāyakaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sīlavatīnagarai saiṭṭhiputtau hutvā nibbatti, bandhurautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina sāvatthiyaṃ gatau upāsakaihi saddhiṃ vihāraṃ gatau satthu dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā ñāṇassa paripākattā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.17.7-12) –

‘‘siddhatthau nāma bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

purakkhatau sāvakaihi, nagaraṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

‘‘rañ‍ñau antaipurai āsiṃ, gaupakau abhisammatau|

pāsādai upaviṭṭhauhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘kaṇavairaṃ gahaitvāna, bhikkhusaṅghai samaukiriṃ|

buddhassa visuṃ katvāna, tatau bhiyyau samaukiriṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sattāsītimhitau  kappai, caturāsuṃ mahiddhikā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā katañ‍ñubhāvai ṭhatvā attanau upakārassa rañ‍ñau pac‍cupakāraṃ kātuṃ sīlavatīnagaraṃ gantvā  rañ‍ñau dhammaṃ daisaintau sac‍cāni pakāsaisi| rājā sac‍capariyausānai sautāpan‍nau  hutvā attanau nagarai sudassanaṃ nāma mahantaṃ vihāraṃ kāraitvā thairassa niyyātaisi| mahālābhasak‍kārau ahausi| thairau vihāraṃ sabbañ‍ca lābhasak‍kāraṃ saṅghassa niyyātaitvā sayaṃ purimaniyāmainaiva piṇḍāya caritvā yāpaintau katipāhaṃ tattha vasitvā sāvatthiṃ gantukāmau ahausi| bhikkhū, ‘‘bhantai, tumhai idhaiva vasatha, sacai pac‍cayaihi vaikal‍laṃ, mayaṃ taṃ paripūraissāmā’’ti āhaṃsu| thairau, ‘‘na mayhaṃ, āvusau, uḷāraihi pac‍cayaihi atthau atthi, itarītaraihi pac‍cayaihi yāpaimi, dhammarasainaivamhi tittau’’ti dassaintau –

103.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ aitaina atthikau, sukhitau dhammarasaina tappitau|

pitvā rasaggamuttamaṃ, na ca kāhāmi visaina santhava’’nti|| –

gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha nāhaṃ aitaina atthikauti yaina maṃ tumhai tappaitukāmā ‘‘paripūraissāmā’’ti vadatha, aitaina āmisalābhaina pac‍cayāmisarasaina nāhaṃ atthikau, mayhaṃ aitaina atthau natthi, santuṭṭhi paramaṃ sukhanti itarītaraihaiva pac‍cayaihi yāpaimīti atthau| idāni taina anatthikabhāvai padhānakāraṇaṃ dassaintau āha ‘‘sukhitau dhammarasaina tappitau’’ti| sattatiṃsabaudhipakkhiyadhammarasaina caiva navavidhalaukuttaradhammarasaina ca tappitau pīṇitau sukhitau uttamaina sukhaina suhitauti atthau| pitvā rasaggamuttamanti sabbarasaisu aggaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ tatauyaiva uttamaṃ yathāvuttaṃ dhammarasaṃ pivitvā ṭhitau, taināha – ‘‘sabbarasaṃ dhammarasau jinātī’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 354)| na ca kāhāmi visaina santhavanti aivarūpaṃ rasuttamaṃ  dhammarasaṃ pivitvā ṭhitau visaina visasadisaina visarasaina santhavaṃ saṃsaggaṃ na karissāmi, tathākaraṇassa kāraṇaṃ natthīti atthau|

bandhuratthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. khitakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

lahukau  vata mai kāyauti āyasmatau khitakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai yakkhasaināpati hutvā nibbattau aikadivasaṃ yakkhasamāgamai nisin‍nau satthāraṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ disvā upasaṅkamitvā satthāraṃ vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| tassa satthā dhammaṃ daisaisi| sau dhammaṃ sutvā uḷāraṃ pītisaumanassaṃ pavaidaintau apphauṭaintau uṭṭhahitvā satthāraṃ vanditvā  padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbatti, khitakautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau mahāmauggal‍lānatthairassa mahiddhikabhāvaṃ sutvā ‘‘iddhimā bhavissāmī’’ti pubbahaitunā caudiyamānau pabbajitvā bhagavatau santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā samathavipassanāsu kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.17.1-6) –

‘‘padumau nāma nāmaina, dvipadindau narāsabhau|

pavanā abhinikkhamma, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

‘‘yakkhānaṃ samayau āsi, avidūrai mahaisinau|

yaina kic‍caina sampattā, ajjhāpaikkhiṃsu tāvadai||

‘‘buddhassa giramañ‍ñāya, amatassa ca daisanaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, apphauṭaitvā upaṭṭhahiṃ||

‘‘suciṇṇassa phalaṃ passa, upaṭṭhānassa satthunau|

tiṃsakappasahassaisu, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

‘‘ūnatiṃsai  kappasatai, samalaṅkatanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā savisaisaṃ iddhīsu vasībhāvaina anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauntau iddhipāṭihāriyaina anusāsanīpāṭihāriyaina ca sattānaṃ anuggahaṃ karauntau viharati| sau bhikkhūhi, ‘‘kathaṃ tvaṃ, āvusau, iddhi vaḷañ‍jaisī’’ti puṭṭhau tamatthaṃ ācikkhantau –

104.

 ‘‘lahukau vata mai kāyau, phuṭṭhau ca pītisukhaina vipulaina|

tūlamiva airitaṃ mālutaina, pilavatīva mai kāyau’’ti|| –

gāthaṃ abhāsi| ‘‘udānavasainā’’tipi vadantiyaiva|

tattha lahukau vata mai kāyauti nīvaraṇādivikkhambhanaina cuddasavidhaina cittaparidamanaina caturiddhipādakabhāvanāya suṭṭhu ciṇṇavasībhāvaina ca mai rūpakāyau sal‍lahukau vata, yaina dandhaṃ mahābhūtapac‍cayampi nāma imaṃ karajakāyaṃ  cittavasaina pariṇāmaimīti adhippāyau| phuṭṭhau ca pītisukhaina  vipulaināti sabbatthakamaiva pharantaina mahatā uḷāraina pītisahitaina sukhaina phuṭṭhau ca mai kāyauti yaujanā| idañ‍ca yathā kāyau lahukau ahausi, taṃ dassanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| sukhasañ‍ñauk‍kamanaina hi saddhiṃyaiva lahusañ‍ñauk‍kamanaṃ hauti| sukhassa pharaṇañ‍caittha taṃsamuṭṭhānarūpavasaina daṭṭhabbaṃ kathaṃ pana catutthajjhānasamaṅginau pītisukhapharaṇaṃ, samatik‍kantapītisukhañhi tanti cai? sac‍camaitaṃ, idaṃ pana na catutthajjhānalakkhaṇavasaina vuttaṃ, atha khau pubbabhāgavasaina| ‘‘pītisukhainā’’ti pana pītisahitasadisaina sukhaina, upaikkhā hi idha santasabhāvatāya ñāṇavisaisayaugatau ca sukhanti adhippaitaṃ| tathā hi vuttaṃ ‘‘sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca auk‍kamatī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.101)| pādakajjhānārammaṇaina rūpakāyārammaṇaina vā iddhicittaina sahajātaṃ sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca auk‍kamati pavisati phusati sampāpuṇātīti  ayampi tattha atthau| tathā cāha aṭṭhakathāyaṃ (paṭi॰ ma॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.3.12) – ‘‘sukhasañ‍ñā nāma upaikkhāsampayuttā sañ‍ñā| upaikkhā hi santaṃ sukhanti vuttaṃ sāyaiva sañ‍ñā nīvaraṇaihi caiva vitak‍kādipac‍canīkaihi ca vimuttattā lahusañ‍ñātipi vaiditabbā| taṃ auk‍kantassa panassa karajakāyaupi tūlapicu viya sal‍lahukau hauti| sau aivaṃ vātakkhittatūlapicunau viya sal‍lahukaina dissamānaina kāyaina brahmalaukaṃ gacchatī’’ti| taināha ‘‘tūlamiva airitaṃ mālutaina, pilavatīva mai kāyau’’ti| tassatthau – yadāhaṃ brahmalaukaṃ añ‍ñaṃ vā iddhiyā gantukāmau haumi, tadā mālutaina vāyunā airitaṃ cittaṃ tūlapicu viya ākāsaṃ laṅghantauyaiva mai kāyau hautīti|

khitakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. malitavambhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

uk‍kaṇṭhitauti  āyasmatau malitavambhatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai himavantatau avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ jātassarai sakuṇau hutvā nibbatti, padumuttarau bhagavā taṃ anuggaṇhantau tattha gantvā jātassaratīrai caṅkamati| sakuṇau bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau sarai kumudāni gahaitvā bhagavantaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kurukacchanagarai añ‍ñatarassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, malitavambhautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau pacchābhūmahāthairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tassa santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau viharati| tassa ca ayaṃ sabhāvau, yattha bhaujanasappāyau dul‍labhau, itarai sulabhā, tatau na pak‍kamati| yattha pana bhaujanasappāyau sulabhau, itarai dul‍labhā, tattha na vasati pak‍kamataiva| aivaṃ viharantau  ca haitusampan‍natāya mahāpurisajātikatāya ca nacirassaiva vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.51-57) –

‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, mahājātassarau ahu|

padumuppalasañchan‍nau, puṇḍarīkasamautthaṭau||

‘‘kukutthau nāma nāmaina, tatthāsiṃ sakuṇau tadā|

sīlavā buddhisampan‍nau, puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñaisu kauvidau||

‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

jātassarassāvidūrai, sañ‍carittha mahāmuni||

‘‘jalajaṃ kumudaṃ chaitvā, upanaisiṃ mahaisinau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, paṭiggahi mahāmuni||

‘‘tañ‍ca dānaṃ daditvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

‘‘sauḷasaitau kappasatai, āsuṃ varuṇanāmakā|

aṭṭha aitai janādhipā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā udānaintau –

105.

 ‘‘uk‍kaṇṭhitaupi na vasai, ramamānaupi pak‍kamai|

na tvaivānatthasaṃhitaṃ, vasai vāsaṃ vicakkhaṇau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha uk‍kaṇṭhitaupi na vasaiti yasmiṃ āvāsai vasantassa mai bhaujanasappāyālābhaina adhikusalaisu dhammaisu uk‍kaṇṭhā anabhirati uppaj‍jati, tattha uk‍kaṇṭhitaupi vasāmiyaiva itarasappāyalābhaina na pak‍kamai na pak‍kamāmi| na vasaiti aittha na-kārainapi pak‍kamaitipadaṃ sambandhitabbaṃ| ramamānaupi pak‍kamaiti yasmiṃ  pana āvāsai vasantassa mai pac‍cayavaikal‍lābhāvaina natthi uk‍kaṇṭhā, añ‍ñadatthu abhiramāmi, aivaṃ abhiramamānaupi avasaisasappāyālābhaina tatau pak‍kamai, na vasaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jantauvāhaṃ nacirassaiva sakatthaṃ pac‍cupādinti| ayañ‍caittha attapaṭipattipac‍cavaikkhaṇāyaṃ yaujanā| parassa auvādadānai pana vasaiyya na pak‍kamaiyyāti vidhānavasaina  yaujaitabbaṃ| na tvaivānatthasaṃhitaṃ, vasai vāsaṃ vicakkhaṇauti yasmiṃ āvāsai pac‍cayā sulabhā, samaṇadhammau na pāripūriṃ gacchati, yasmiñ‍ca āvāsai pac‍cayā dul‍labhā, samaṇadhammaupi pāripūriṃ na gacchati, aivarūpau āvāsau idha anatthasaṃhitau nāma avaḍḍhisahitauti katvā| aivarūpaṃ vāsaṃ vicakkhaṇau viñ‍ñujātikau sakatthaṃ paripūraitukāmau natvaiva vasaiyya| yattha pana pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgatau āvāsau labbhati, sattapi sappāyā labbhanti, tatthaiva vasaiyyāti atthau|

malitavambhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. suhaimantatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

sataliṅgassa atthassāti āyasmatau suhaimantatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau itau dvānavutai kappai tissassa bhagavatau kālai vanacarau hutvā vanai vasati, taṃ anuggahituṃ bhagavā arañ‍ñaṃ pavisitvā tassa āsan‍nai ṭhānai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisīdi| sau bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau sugandhāni pun‍nāgapupphāni aucinitvā bhagavantaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai pāriyantadaisai vibhavasampan‍nassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, suhaimantautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau saṅkassanagarai migadāyai viharantaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā taipiṭakau hutvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau  paṭisambhidāpattau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.46-50) –

‘‘kānanaṃ vanamaugayha, vasāmi luddakau ahaṃ|

pun‍nāgaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

‘‘taṃ  pupphaṃ aucinitvāna, sugandhaṃ gandhitaṃ subhaṃ|

thūpaṃ karitvā pulinai, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aikamhi navutai kappai, aikau āsiṃ tamaunudau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā aivaṃ cintaisi – ‘‘mayā khau yaṃ sāvakaina pattabbaṃ, taṃ anuppattaṃ, yaṃnūnāhaṃ idāni bhikkhūnaṃ anuggahaṃ karaiyya’’nti| aivaṃ cintaitvā pabhin‍napaṭisambhidatāya akilāsutāya ca attanau santikaṃ upagatai bhikkhū yathārahaṃ auvadantau anusāsantau kaṅkhaṃ chindantau dhammaṃ kathaintau kammaṭṭhānaṃ niggumbaṃ nij‍jaṭaṃ katvā ācikkhantau viharati| athaikadivasaṃ attanau santikaṃ upagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ viñ‍ñūnañ‍ca puggalānaṃ visaisaṃ ācikkhantau –

106.

 ‘‘sataliṅgassa atthassa, satalakkhaṇadhārinau|

aikaṅgadassī dummaidhau, satadassī ca paṇḍitau’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha sataliṅgassāti līnamatthaṃ gamaintīti liṅgāni, atthaisu saddassa pavattinimittāni, tāni pana sataṃ anaikāni liṅgāni aitassāti sataliṅgau| anaikatthau hi idha satasaddau, ‘‘sataṃ sahassa’’ntiādīsu viya na saṅkhyāvisaisatthau tassa sataliṅgassa| atthassāti ñaiyyassa, ñaiyyañhi ñāṇaina araṇīyatau ‘‘atthau’’ti vuc‍cati| sau ca aikaupi anaikaliṅgau, yathā ‘‘sak‍kau purindadau maghavā’’ti, ‘‘pañ‍ñā vij‍jā maidhā ñāṇa’’nti ca| yaina liṅgaina pavattinimittaina tāvatiṃsādhipatimhi indasaddau pavattau, na taina tattha sak‍kādisaddā pavattā, atha khau añ‍ñaina| tathā yaina sammādiṭṭhimhi pañ‍ñāsaddau pavattau, na taina vij‍jādisaddā| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sataliṅgassa atthassā’’ti|

satalakkhaṇadhārinauti  anaikalakkhaṇavatau| lakkhīyati aitaināti lakkhaṇaṃ, pac‍cayabhāvinau atthassa attanau phalaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cayabhāvau, taina hi sau ayaṃ imassa kāraṇanti lakkhīyati| sau ca aikassaiva atthassa anaikappabhaidau upalabbhati, taināha ‘‘satalakkhaṇadhārinau’’ti| atha vā lakkhīyantīti lakkhaṇāni, tassa tassa atthassa saṅkhatatādayau pakāravisaisā tai pana atthatau avatthāvisaisā vaiditabbā| tai ca pana taisaṃ anic‍catādisāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇaṃ liṅgainti ñāpaintīti ‘‘liṅgānī’’ti ca vuc‍canti| tassimai ākārā, yasmā aikassāpi atthassa anaikai upalabbhanti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘sataliṅgassa atthassa, satalakkhaṇadhārinau’’ti| taināha āyasmā dhammasaināpati – ‘‘sabbai dhammā sabbākāraina buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇamukhai āpāthaṃ āgacchantī’’ti (mahāni॰ 156; cūḷani॰ maugharājamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 85; paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.5)|

aikaṅgadassī dummaidhauti aivaṃ anaikaliṅgai anaikalakkhaṇai atthai yau tattha aikaṅgadassī aputhupañ‍ñatāya aikaliṅgamattaṃ aikalakkhaṇamattañ‍ca disvā attanā diṭṭhamaiva ‘‘idamaiva sac‍ca’’nti abhinivissa  ‘‘maughamañ‍ña’’nti itaraṃ paṭikkhipati, hatthidassanakaandhau viya aikaṅgagāhī dummaidhau duppañ‍ñau tattha vij‍jamānānaṃyaiva pakāravisaisānaṃ ajānanatau micchā abhinivisanatau ca| satadassī ca paṇḍitauti paṇḍitau pana tattha vij‍jamānai anaikaipi pakārai attanau pañ‍ñācakkhunā sabbasau passati| yau vā tattha labbhamānai anaikai pañ‍ñācakkhunā attanāpi passati, añ‍ñaisampi dassaiti pakāsaiti, sau paṇḍitau vicakkhaṇau atthaisu kusalau nāmāti| aivaṃ thairau uk‍kaṃsagataṃ attanau paṭisambhidāsampattiṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vibhāvaisi|

suhaimantatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. dhammasavatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

pabbajiṃ  tulayitvānāti āyasmatau dhammasavatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai suvacchau nāma brāhmaṇau hutvā tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāragū gharāvāsai dausaṃ  disvā tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā arañ‍ñāyatanai pabbatantarai assamaṃ kāraitvā bahūhi tāpasaihi saddhiṃ vasi| athassa kusalabījaṃ raupaitukāmau padumuttarau bhagavā assamasamīpai ākāsai ṭhatvā iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaisi| sau taṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau pūjaitukāmau nāgapupphāni aucināpaisi| satthā, ‘‘alaṃ imassa tāpasassa aittakaṃ kusalabīja’’nti pak‍kāmi| sau pupphāni gahaitvā satthu gamanamaggaṃ aukiritvā cittaṃ pasādaintau añ‍jaliṃ paggayha aṭṭhāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā dhammasavauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau haitusampattiyā caudiyamānau gharāvāsai ādīnavaṃ pabbaj‍jāya ānisaṃsañ‍ca disvā dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ viharantaṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.39-45) –

‘‘suvacchau nāma nāmaina, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

purakkhatau sasissaihi, vasatai pabbatantarai||

‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

‘‘vaihāsamhi caṅkamati, dhūpāyati jalatai tathā|

hāsaṃ mamaṃ viditvāna, pak‍kāmi pācināmukhau||

‘‘tañ‍ca  acchariyaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

nāgapupphaṃ gahaitvāna, gatamaggamhi aukiriṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai  kappasatai, rājā āsi mahārahau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā saumanassappattau udānavasaina –

107.

 ‘‘pabbajiṃ tulayitvāna, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha pabbajiṃ tulayitvānāti ‘‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau’’tiādinā (dī॰ ni॰ 1.191; ma॰ ni॰ 2.10; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.154) gharāvāsai, ‘‘appassādā kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā’’tiādinā (pāci॰ 417; ma॰ ni॰ 1.177) kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ tappaṭipakkhatau naikkhammai ca ānisaṃsaṃ tulabhūtāya pañ‍ñāya vicāraitvā vīmaṃsitvāti atthau| saisaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttanayamaiva| idamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

dhammasavatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. dhammasavapitutthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

sa vīsavassasatikauti  āyasmatau dhammasavapitutthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau buddhasuñ‍ñai laukai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bhūtagaṇai nāma pabbatai viharantaṃ pac‍caikasambuddhaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau tiṇasūlapupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau dārapariggahaṃ katvā dhammasavaṃ nāma puttaṃ labhitvā tasmiṃ pabbajitai sayampi vīsavassasatikau hutvā, ‘‘mama puttau tāva taruṇau pabbaji, atha kasmā nāhaṃ  pabbajissāmī’’ti sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ sacchākāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.35-38) –

‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, bhūtagaṇau nāma pabbatau|

vasataikau jinau tattha, sayambhū laukanissaṭau||

‘‘tiṇasūlaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ|

aikūnasatasahassaṃ, kappaṃ na vinipātikau||

‘‘itau aikādasai kappai, aikausiṃ dharaṇīruhau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā sañ‍jātasaumanassau udānaintau –

108.

 ‘‘sa vīsavassasatikau, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha sa vīsavassasatikauti sau vīsaṃvassasatikau, sau ahaṃ jātiyā vīsādhikavassasatikau samānau| pabbajinti pabbaj‍jaṃ upagacchiṃ| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva| idamaiva ca imassa thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausi|

dhammasavapitutthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. saṅgharakkhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

na nūnāyaṃ paramahitānukampinauti āyasmatau saṅgharakkhitatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? saupi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau itau catunavutai kappai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ pabbatapādai  vasantai satta pac‍caikasambuddhai  disvā pasan‍namānasau kadambapupphāni gahaitvā pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ ibbhakulai nibbatti, tassa saṅgharakkhitauti nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā  añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ sahāyaṃ katvā arañ‍ñai viharati| thairassa vasanaṭṭhānatau avidūrai vanagumbai aikā migī vijāyitvā taruṇaṃ chāpaṃ rakkhantī chātajjhattāpi puttasinaihaina dūrai gaucarāya na gacchati, āsan‍nai ca tiṇaudakassa alābhaina kilamati| taṃ disvā thairau, ‘‘ahau vatāyaṃ laukau taṇhābandhanabaddhau mahādukkhaṃ anubhavati, na taṃ chindituṃ sak‍kautī’’ti saṃvaigajātau tamaiva aṅkusaṃ katvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.30-34) –

‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, kuk‍kuṭau nāma pabbatau|

tamhi pabbatapādamhi, satta buddhā vasanti tai||

‘‘kadambaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, dīparājaṃva uggataṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, satta buddhai samaukiriṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, sattāsuṃ pupphanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau dutiyakaṃ bhikkhuṃ micchāvitak‍kabahulaṃ viharantaṃ ñatvā tamaiva migiṃ upamaṃ karitvā taṃ auvadantau –

109.

 ‘‘na nūnāyaṃ paramahitānukampinau, rahaugatau anuvigaṇaiti sāsanaṃ|

tathāhayaṃ viharati pākatindriyau, migī yathā taruṇajātikā vanai’’ti|| –

gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha  na nūnāyanti na-iti paṭisaidhai nipātau| nūnāti parivitak‍kai| nūna ayanti padacchaidau| paramahitānukampinauti paramaṃ ativiya, paramaina vā anuttaraina hitaina sattai anukampanasīlassa bhagavatau| rahaugatauti rahasi gatau, suñ‍ñāgāragatau kāyavivaikayuttauti atthau| anuvigaṇaitīti aittha ‘‘na nūnā’’ti padadvayaṃ ānaitvā sambandhitabbaṃ ‘‘nānuvigaṇaiti nūnā’’ti, na cintaisi mañ‍ñai, ‘‘nānuyuñ‍jatī’’ti tak‍kaimīti atthau| sāsananti paṭipattisāsanaṃ, catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānabhāvananti adhippāyau| tathā hīti tainaiva kāraṇaina, satthu sāsanassa ananuyuñ‍janatau aiva| ayanti ayaṃ bhikkhu| pākatindriyauti manacchaṭṭhānaṃ indriyānaṃ yathāsakaṃ visayaisu vissaj‍janatau sabhāvabhūtaindriyau, asaṃvutacakkhudvārādikauti atthau| yassa taṇhāsaṅgassa acchin‍natāya sau bhikkhu pākatindriyau viharati, tassa upamaṃ dassaintau ‘‘migī yathā taruṇajātikā vanai’’ti āha| yathā ayaṃ taruṇasabhāvā migī puttasnaihassa acchin‍natāya vanai dukkhaṃ anubhavati, na taṃ ativattati, aivamayampi bhikkhu saṅgassa acchin‍natāya pākatindriyau viharantau vaṭṭadukkhaṃ nātivattatīti adhippāyau| ‘‘taruṇavijātikā’’ti vā pāṭhau| abhinavappasutā bālavacchāti atthau| taṃ sutvā sau bhikkhu sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi|

saṅgharakkhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. usabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nagā nagaggaisu susaṃvirūḷhāti āyasmatau usabhatthairassa gāthā| kā uppati? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayāni puñ‍ñāni karauntau itau aikatiṃsai kappai sikhissa bhagavatau kālai daivaputtau hutvā nibbattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau dibbapupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sā pupphapūjā sattāhaṃ pupphamaṇḍapākāraina aṭṭhāsi| daivamanussānaṃ mahāsamāgamau ahausi| sau  taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai ibbhakulai nibbatti, tassa usabhauti nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau jaitavanapaṭiggahaṇai satthari laddhappasādau pabbajitvā katapubbakic‍cau arañ‍ñai pabbatapādai viharati| taina ca samayaina pāvusakālamaighai abhippavuṭṭhai pabbatasikharaisu rukkhagacchalatāya ghanapaṇṇasaṇḍinau haunti| athaikadivasaṃ thairau laiṇatau nikkhamitvā taṃ vanarāmaṇaiyyakaṃ pabbatarāmaṇaiyyakañ‍ca disvā yaunisaumanasikāravasaina ‘‘imaipi nāma rukkhādayau acaitanā utusampattiyā vaḍḍhiṃ pāpuṇanti, atha kasmā nāhaṃ utusappāyaṃ labhitvā guṇaihi vaḍḍhiṃ pāpuṇissāmī’’ti cintaintau –

110.

 ‘‘nagā nagaggaisu susaṃvirūḷhā, udaggamaighaina navaina sittā|

vivaikakāmassa arañ‍ñasañ‍ñinau, janaiti bhiyyau usabhassa kalyata’’nti|| –

gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha nagāti rukkhā, ‘‘nāgā’’ti kaici vadanti, nāgarukkhāti atthau| nagaggaisūti pabbatasikharaisu| susaṃvirūḷhāti suṭṭhu samantatau virūḷhamūlā hutvā paritau upari ca sammadaiva sañ‍jātasākhaggapal‍lavappasākhāti  atthau| udaggamaighaina navaina sittāti paṭhamuppan‍naina uḷāraina mahatā pāvusamaighaina abhippavuṭṭhā| vivaikakāmassāti kilaisavivittaṃ cittavivaikaṃ icchantassa, arañ‍ñavāsaina tāva kāyavivaikau laddhau, idāni upadhivivaikādhigamassa nissayabhūtau cittavivaikau laddhabbauti taṃ patthayamānassa, jāgariyaṃ anuyuñ‍jantassāti atthau, taināha ‘‘arañ‍ñasañ‍ñinau’’ti| arañ‍ñavāsau nāma satthārā vaṇṇitau thaumitau| sau ca khau yāvadaiva samathavipassanābhāvanāpāripūriyā, tasmā sā mayā hatthagatā kātabbāti aivaṃ arañ‍ñagatasañ‍ñinau naikkhammasaṅkappabahulassāti atthau| janaitīti uppādainti, puthuttai  hi idaṃ aikavacanaṃ| kaici pana ‘‘janaintī’’ti paṭhanti| bhiyyauti uparūpari| usabhassāti attānamaiva paraṃ viya vadati| kalyatanti kalyabhāvaṃ cittassa kammañ‍ñataṃ bhāvanāyaugyataṃ| svāyamatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| aivaṃ thairau imaṃ gāthaṃ vadantauyaiva vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.25-29) –

‘‘daivaputtau  ahaṃ santau, pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ|

mandāravaina pupphaina, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘sattāhaṃ chadanaṃ āsi, dibbaṃ mālaṃ tathāgatai|

sabbai janā samāgantvā, namassiṃsu tathāgataṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau ca dasamai kappai, rājāhausiṃ jutindharau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

ayamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇagāthā ahausīti|

usabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

aikādasamavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

12. dvādasamavaggau

1. jaintatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

duppabbaj‍jaṃ  vai duradhivāsā gaihāti āyasmatau jaintatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinantau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai daivaputtau hutvā nibbatti| sau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau kiṃkirātapupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau  imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai jaintagāmai aikassa maṇḍalikarājassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, jaintautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau daharakālaiyaiva haitusampattiyā caudiyamānau pabbaj‍jānin‍namānasau hutvā puna cintaisi – ‘‘pabbaj‍jā nāma duk‍karā, gharāpi durāvāsā, dhammau ca gambhīrau, bhaugā ca duradhigamā, kiṃ nu khau kattabba’’nti aivaṃ pana cintābahulau hutvā vicarantau aikadivasaṃ satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ suṇi| sutakālatau paṭṭhāya pabbaj‍jābhiratau hutvā satthu santikai pabbajitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya arahattaṃ sacchākāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.21-24) –

‘‘daivaputtau ahaṃ santau, pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ|

kak‍kārupupphaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau ca navamai kappai, rājā sattuttamau ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhantau, ‘‘asakkhiṃ vatāhaṃ āditau mayhaṃ uppan‍navitak‍kaṃ chinditu’’nti saumanassajātau  vitak‍kassa uppan‍nākāraṃ tassa ca sammadaiva chin‍nataṃ dassaintau –

111.

 ‘‘duppabbaj‍jaṃ  vai duradhivāsā gaihā, dhammau gambhīrau duradhigamā bhaugā|

kicchā vutti nau itarītarainaiva, yuttaṃ cintaituṃ satatamanic‍cata’’nti|| –

gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha duppabbaj‍janti appaṃ vā mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhañ‍caiva ñātiparivaṭṭañ‍ca pahāya imasmiṃ sāsanai uraṃ datvā pabbajanassa duk‍karattā dukkhaṃ pabbajanaṃ, duk‍karā pabbaj‍jāti duppabbaj‍jaṃ| vaiti nipātamattaṃ, daḷhatthau vā ‘‘pabbaj‍jā dukkhā’’ti| gaihañ‍cai āvasaiyyaṃ, duradhivāsā gaihā, yasmā gaihaṃ adhivasantaina rañ‍ñā rājakic‍caṃ, issaraina issarakic‍caṃ, gahapatinā gahapatikic‍caṃ kattabbaṃ hauti, parijanau caiva samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca saṅgahaitabbā, tasmiṃ tasmiñ‍ca kattabbai kariyamānaipi gharāvāsau chiddaghaṭau viya mahāsamuddau viya ca duppūrau, tasmā gaihā nāmaitai adhivasituṃ āvasituṃ dukkhā duk‍karāti katvā duradhivāsā durāvāsāti| pabbaj‍jañ‍cai anutiṭṭhaiyyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau, yadatthā pabbaj‍jā, sau pabbajitaina adhigantabbau paṭivaidhasaddhammau gambhīrau, gambhīrañāṇagaucarattā duddasau, duppaṭivijjhau dhammassa gambhīrabhāvaina duppaṭivijjhattā| gaihañ‍cai āvasaiyyaṃ, duradhigamā bhaugā yaihi vinā na sak‍kā gaihaṃ āvasituṃ, tai bhaugā dukkhaina kasiraina adhigantabbatāya duradhigamā| aivaṃ santai gharāvāsaṃ pahāya pabbaj‍jaṃyaiva anutiṭṭhaiyyaṃ, aivampi kicchā vutti nau itarītaraina idha imasmiṃ buddhasāsanai itarītaraina yathāladdhaina pac‍cayaina amhākaṃ vutti jīvikā kicchā dukkhā, gharāvāsānaṃ duradhivāsatāya bhaugānañ‍ca duradhigamatāya gaihai itarītaraina pac‍cayaina yāpaitabbatāya kicchā kasirā vutti amhākaṃ, tattha kiṃ kātuṃ vaṭṭatīti? yuttaṃ cintaituṃ satatamanic‍cataṃ sakalaṃ divasaṃ pubbarattāpararattañ‍ca taibhūmakadhammajātaṃ anic‍catanti, tatau uppādavayavantatau ādiantavantatau  tāvakālikatau ca na nic‍canti ‘‘anic‍ca’’nti cintaituṃ vipassituṃ yuttaṃ| anic‍cānupassanāya siddhāya itarānupassanā sukhainaiva sijjhantīti anic‍cānupassanāva aittha vuttā, anic‍cassa dukkhānattatānaṃ abyabhicaraṇatau sāsanikassa sukhaggahaṇatau ca| taināha – ‘‘yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.15), ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammaṃ’’ (mahāva॰ 16; dī॰ ni॰ 2.371; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081), ‘‘vayadhammā saṅkhārā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.218) ca tadaminā aivaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paṭipakkhavasaina aparāparaṃ uppan‍nai vitak‍kai niggahaitvā anic‍catāmukhaina vipassanaṃ ārabhitvā idāni katakic‍cau jātauti dassaiti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘attanau paṭipatti’’ntiādi| idamaiva thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausi|

jaintatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. vacchagauttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

taivij‍jauhaṃ  mahājhāyīti āyasmatau vacchagauttatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalabījaṃ raupaintau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai bandhumatīnagarai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ rañ‍ñā nāgaraihi ca saddhiṃ buddhapūjaṃ katvā tatau paraṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai vibhavasampan‍nassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, tassa vacchagauttatāya vacchagauttautvaiva samañ‍ñā ahausi| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ patvā brāhmaṇavij‍jāsu nipphattiṃ gatau vimuttiṃ gavaisantau tattha sāraṃ adisvā paribbājakapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā vicarantau satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā pañhaṃ pucchitvā tasmiṃ vissaj‍jitai pasan‍namānasau satthu santikai pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.15-20) –

‘‘udaintaṃ  sataraṃsiṃva, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

pan‍narasai yathā candaṃ, niyyantaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘aṭṭhasaṭṭhisahassāni, sabbai khīṇāsavā ahuṃ|

parivāriṃsu sambuddhaṃ, dvipadindaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

‘‘sammaj‍jitvāna taṃ vīthiṃ, niyyantai laukanāyakai|

ussāpaisiṃ dhajaṃ tattha, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dhajaṃ abhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau catutthakai kappai, rājāhausiṃ mahabbalau|

sabbākāraina sampan‍nau, sudhajau iti vissutau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā saumanassajātau udānavasaina –

112.

 ‘‘taivij‍jauhaṃ  mahājhāyī, caitausamathakauvidau|

sadatthau mai anuppattau, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha  taivij‍jauhanti yadipi maṃ pubbai tiṇṇaṃ vaidānaṃ pāraṃ gatattā ‘‘brāhmaṇau taivij‍jau’’ti sañ‍jānanti, taṃ pana samañ‍ñāmattaṃ vaidaisu vij‍jākic‍cassa abhāvatau| idāni pana pubbainivāsañāṇādīnaṃ tissan‍naṃ vij‍jānaṃ adhigatattā paramatthatau taivij‍jau ahaṃ, mahantassa anavasaisassa samudayapakkhiyassa kilaisagaṇassa jhāpanatau, mahantaina maggaphalajhānaina mahantassa uḷārassa paṇītassa nibbānassa jhāyanatau ca mahājhāyī| caitausamathakauvidauti cittasaṅkhaubhakarānaṃ saṃkilaisadhammānaṃ vūpasamanaina caitasau samādahanai kusalau| aitaina taivij‍jabhāvassa kāraṇamāha| samādhikausal‍lasahitaina hi āsavakkhayaina taivij‍jatā, na kaivalaina| sadatthauti sakatthau ka-kārassāyaṃ da-kārau katau ‘‘anuppattasadatthau’’tiādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.9; a॰ ni॰ 3.38) viya| ‘‘sadatthau’’ti ca arahattaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| tañhi attapaṭibandhaṭṭhaina attānaṃ avijahanaṭṭhaina attanau paramatthaṭṭhaina attanau atthattā ‘‘sakatthau’’ti vuc‍cati| svāyaṃ sadatthau mai mayā anuppattau adhigatau| aitaina yathāvuttaṃ mahājhāyibhāvaṃ sikhāpattaṃ katvā dassaiti| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

vacchagauttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. vanavacchatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

acchaudikā puthusilāti āyasmatau vanavacchatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalabījaṃ raupaintau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau parassa kammaṃ  katvā jīvantau kassaci aparādhaṃ katvā maraṇabhayaina taj‍jitau palāyantau antarāmaggai baudhirukkhaṃ  disvā pasan‍namānasau tassa mūlaṃ sammaj‍jitvā piṇḍibandhaihi asaukapupphaihi pūjaṃ katvā vanditvā baudhiṃ abhimukhau namassamānau pal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau māraituṃ āgatai pac‍catthikai disvā taisu cittaṃ akaupaitvā baudhiṃ aiva āvaj‍jaintau satapaurisai papātai papati| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ puñ‍ñāni katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai vibhavasampan‍nassa brāhmaṇassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, ‘‘vacchau’’tissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau bimbisārasamāgamai paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.16.7-14) –

‘‘parakammāyanai  yuttau, aparādhaṃ akāsahaṃ|

vanantaṃ abhidhāvissaṃ, bhayavairasamappitau||

‘‘pupphitaṃ pādapaṃ disvā, piṇḍibandhaṃ sunimmitaṃ|

tambapupphaṃ gahaitvāna, baudhiyaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘sammaj‍jitvāna taṃ baudhiṃ, pāṭaliṃ pādaputtamaṃ|

pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna, baudhimūlai upāvisiṃ||

‘‘gatamaggaṃ gavaisantā, āgacchuṃ mama santikaṃ|

tai ca disvānahaṃ tattha, āvaj‍jiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

‘‘vanditvāna ahaṃ baudhiṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

anaikatālai papatiṃ, giriduggai bhayānakai||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘itau ca tatiyai kappai, rājā susañ‍ñatau ahaṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā vivaikābhiratiyā vanaiyaiva vasi, taina vanavacchauti samañ‍ñā udapādi| atha kadāci thairau ñātijanānuggahatthaṃ rājagahaṃ  gatau tattha ñātakaihi upaṭṭhiyamānau katipāhaṃ vasitvā gamanākāraṃ sandassaiti| taṃ ñātakā, ‘‘bhantai, amhākaṃ anuggahatthaṃ dhuravihārai vasatha, mayaṃ upaṭṭhahissāmā’’ti yāciṃsu| thairau taisaṃ pabbatarāmaṇaiyyakittanāpadaisaina vivaikābhiratiṃ nivaidaintau –

113.

 ‘‘acchaudikā puthusilā, gaunaṅgulamigāyutā|

ambusaivālasañchan‍nā, tai sailā ramayanti ma’’nti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha acchaudikāti acchaṃ abahalaṃ sukhumaṃ udakaṃ aitaisūti ‘‘acchaudakā’’ti vattabbai liṅgavipal‍lāsaina acchaudikā’’ti vuttaṃ| aitaina taisaṃ udakasampattiṃ dassaiti| puthusilāti puthulā vitthatā mudusukhasamphassā silā aitaisūti puthusilā| aitaina nisaj‍janaṭṭhānasampattiṃ dassaiti| gun‍naṃ viya naṅgulaṃ naṅguṭṭhaṃ aitaisanti gaunaṅgulā, kāḷamak‍kaṭā, ‘‘pakatimak‍kaṭā’’tipi vadantiyaiva| gaunaṅgulaihi ca pasadādikaihi migaihi ca tahaṃ tahaṃ vicarantaihi āyutā missitāti gaunaṅgulamigāyutā| aitaina taisaṃ amanussūpacāritāya arañ‍ñalakkhaṇūpaitataṃ dassaiti| ambusaivālasañchan‍nāti pasavanatau satataṃ paggharamānasalilatāya tahaṃ tahaṃ udakasaivālasañchāditā| tai sailā ramayanti manti yatthāhaṃ vasāmi; tai aidisā sailā pabbatā vivaikābhiratiyā maṃ ramayanti, tasmā tatthaivāhaṃ gacchāmīti adhippāyau| idamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausi|

vanavacchatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. adhimuttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagarunauti āyasmatau adhimuttatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? sau kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau brāhmaṇavij‍jāsu nipphattiṃ gatau kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ  disvā gharāvāsaṃ pahāya tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā arañ‍ñai viharantau buddhuppādaṃ sutvā manussūpacāraṃ upagantvā satthāraṃ bhikkhusaṅghaparivutaṃ gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau attanau vākacīraṃ satthu pādamūlai patthari| satthā tassa ajjhāsayaṃ ñatvā tasmiṃ aṭṭhāsi| tattha ṭhitaṃ bhagavantaṃ kāḷānusāraina gandhaina pūjaitvā ‘‘samuddharasimaṃ lauka’’ntiādikāhi dasahi gāthāhi abhitthavi| taṃ satthā ‘‘anāgatai itau satasahassakappamatthakai gautamassa nāma sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai pabbajitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau bhavissatī’’ti byākaritvā pak‍kāmi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā tatau yāvāyaṃ buddhuppādau, tāva daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā adhimuttauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau brāhmaṇavij‍jāsu nipphattiṃ gantvā tattha sāraṃ apassantau pacchimabhavikattā nissaraṇaṃ gavaisantau jaitavanapaṭiggahaṇai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau satthu santikai pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ  paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.40.304-332) –

‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ, dīparukkhaṃva uj‍jalaṃ|

ausadhiṃva viraucantaṃ, vij‍jutaṃ gaganai yathā||

‘‘asambhītaṃ anuttāsiṃ, migarājaṃva kaisariṃ|

ñāṇālaukaṃ pakāsaintaṃ, maddantaṃ titthiyai gaṇai||

‘‘uddharantaṃ imaṃ laukaṃ, chindantaṃ sabbasaṃsayaṃ|

gaj‍jantaṃ migarājaṃva, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘jaṭājinadharau  āsiṃ, brahā uju patāpavā|

vākacīraṃ gahaitvāna, pādamūlai apatthariṃ||

‘‘kāḷānusāriyaṃ gayhaṃ, anulimpiṃ tathāgataṃ|

sambuddhamanulimpaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘samuddharasimaṃ laukaṃ, aughatiṇṇa mahāmuni|

ñāṇālaukaina jautaisi, nāvaṭaṃ ñāṇamuttamaṃ||

‘‘dhammacak‍kaṃ  pavattaisi, maddasai paratitthiyai|

usabhau jitasaṅgāmau, sampakampaisi maidaniṃ||

‘‘mahāsamuddai ūmiyau, vailantamhi pabhij‍jarai|

tathaiva tava ñāṇamhi, sabbadiṭṭhī pabhij‍jarai||

‘‘sukhumacchikajālaina, saramhi sampatānitai|

antaujālīkatā pāṇā, pīḷitā haunti tāvadai||

‘‘tathaiva titthiyā laukai, puthupāsaṇḍanissitā|

antauñāṇavarai tuyhaṃ, parivattanti mārisa||

‘‘patiṭṭhā vuyhataṃ aughai, tvañhi nāthau abandhunaṃ|

bhayaṭṭitānaṃ saraṇaṃ, muttitthīnaṃ parāyaṇaṃ||

‘‘aikavīrau asadisau, maittākaruṇasañ‍cayau|

asamau susamau santau, vasī tādī jitañ‍jayau||

‘‘dhīrau vigatasammauhau, anaijau akathaṃkathī|

tusitau vantadausausi, nimmalau saṃyatau suci||

‘‘saṅgātigau hatamadau, taivij‍jau tibhavantagau|

sīmātigau dhammagaru, gatatthau hitavabbhutau||

‘‘tārakau tvaṃ yathā nāvā, nidhīvassāsakārakau|

asambhītau yathā sīhau, gajarājāva dappitau||

‘‘thaumaitvā dasagāthāhi, padumuttaraṃ mahāyasaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, tuṇhī aṭṭhāsahaṃ tadā||

‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘yau mai sīlañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca, saddhammañ‍cāpi vaṇṇayi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘saṭṭhi kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

añ‍ñai daivaibhibhavitvā, issaraṃ kārayissati||

‘‘sau  pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissati||

‘‘pabbajitvāna kāyaina, pāpakammaṃ vivaj‍jiya|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘yathāpi maighau thanayaṃ, tappaiti maidaniṃ imaṃ|

tathaiva tvaṃ mahāvīra, dhammaina tappayī mamaṃ||

‘‘sīlaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, thavitvā laukanāyakaṃ|

pattaumhi paramaṃ santiṃ, nibbānaṃ padamac‍cutaṃ||

‘‘ahau nūna sa bhagavā, ciraṃ tiṭṭhaiyya cakkhumā|

añ‍ñātañ‍ca vijānaiyyuṃ, phusaiyyuṃ amataṃ padaṃ||

‘‘ayaṃ mai pacchimā jāti, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhithaumayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanā saha vasantai kāyadaḷhibahulai bhikkhū auvadantau –

114.

 ‘‘kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagarunau, hiyyamānamhi jīvitai|

sarīrasukhagiddhassa, kutau samaṇasādhutā’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagarunauti duṭṭhul‍laṃ asubhayaugyatā, kāyassa duṭṭhul‍laṃ kāyaduṭṭhul‍laṃ, kāyaduṭṭhul‍laṃ garu sambhāvitaṃ yassa sau kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagaru, anissaraṇappañ‍ñau hutvā kāyapausanappasutau kāyadaḷhibahulauti atthau, tassa kāyaduṭṭhul‍lagarunau| hiyyamānamhi jīvitaiti kun‍nadīnaṃ udakaṃ viya jīvitasaṅkhārai lahusau khīyamānai| sarīrasukhagiddhassāti paṇītāhārādīhi attanau kāyassa sukhaina gaidhaṃ āpan‍nassa| kutau samaṇasādhutāti aivarūpassa puggalassa samaṇabhāvaina sādhutā susamaṇatā kutau kaina kāraṇaina siyā, aikaṃsatau pana kāyai jīvitai ca nirapaikkhassa itarītarasantausaina santuṭṭhassa āraddhavīriyassaiva samaṇasādhutāti adhippāyau|

adhimuttatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. mahānāmatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

aisāvahiyyasai  pabbataināti āyasmatau mahānāmatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau sumaidhassa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā brāhmaṇavij‍jāsu nipphattiṃ gatau gharāvāsaṃ pahāya añ‍ñatarāya nadiyā tīrai assamaṃ kāraitvā sambahulai brāhmaṇai mantai vācaintau viharati| athaikadivasaṃ bhagavā taṃ anuggaṇhituṃ tassa asamapadaṃ upagacchi| sau bhagavantaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau āsanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā adāsi| nisin‍nai bhagavati sumadhuraṃ madhuṃ upanāmaisi| taṃ bhagavā paribhuñ‍jitvā haiṭṭhā adhimuttatthairavatthumhi vuttanayaina anāgataṃ byākaritvā pak‍kāmi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivalaukai nibbattitvā aparāparaṃ sugatīsuyaiva parivattaintau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā mahānāmauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bhagavatau santikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā naisādakai nāma pabbatai viharantau kilaisapariyuṭṭhānaṃ vikkhambhaituṃ asak‍kauntau ‘‘kiṃ mai iminā saṃkiliṭṭhacittassa jīvitainā’’ti attabhāvaṃ nibbindantau uc‍caṃ pabbatasikharaṃ abhiruhitvā ‘‘itau pātaitvā taṃ māraissāmī’’ti attānaṃ paraṃ viya niddisantau –

115.

 ‘‘aisāvahiyyasai pabbataina, bahukuṭajasal‍lakikaina|

naisādakaina girinā, yasassinā paricchadainā’’ti|| – gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tattha  aisāvahiyyasaiti aisau tvaṃ mahānāma avahiyyasai parihāyasi| pabbataināti nivāsaṭṭhānabhūtaina iminā pabbataina| bahukuṭajasal‍lakikaināti bahūhi kuṭajaihi indasālarukkhaihi sal‍lakīhi indasālarukkhaihi vā saman‍nāgataina| naisādakaināti aivaṃnāmakaina| girināti sailaina| sailau hi sandhisaṅkhātaihi pabbaihi ṭhitattā ‘‘pabbatau’’ti, pasavanādivasaina jalassa, sārabhūtānaṃ bhaisaj‍jādivatthūnañ‍ca giraṇatau ‘‘girī’’ti vuc‍cati| tadubhayatthasambhavatau panaittha ‘‘pabbatainā’’ti vatvā ‘‘girinā’’ti ca vuttaṃ| yasassināti sabbaguṇaihi vissutaina pakāsaina| paricchadaināti nānāvidharukkhagacchalatāhi samantatau chan‍naina, vasanaṭṭhānatāya vā tuyhaṃ paricchadabhūtaina| ayañhaittha adhippāyau – mahānāma, yadi kammaṭṭhānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā vitak‍kabahulau hausi, aivaṃ tvaṃ iminā chāyūdakasampan‍naina sappāyaina  nivāsanaṭṭhānabhūtaina naisādakagirinā parihāyasi, idānihaṃ taṃ itau pātaitvā māraissāmi, tasmā na labbhā vitak‍kavasikaina bhavitunti| aivaṃ thairau  attānaṃ santaj‍jaintauyaiva vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.40.333-352) –

‘‘sindhuyā nadiyā tīrai, sukatau assamau mama|

tattha vācaimahaṃ sissai, itihāsaṃ salakkhaṇaṃ||

‘‘dhammakāmā vinītā tai, sautukāmā susāsanaṃ|

chaḷaṅgai pāramippattā, sindhukūlai vasanti tai||

‘‘uppātagamanai caiva, lakkhaṇaisu ca kauvidā|

uttamatthaṃ gavaisantā, vasanti vipinai tadā||

‘‘sumaidhau nāma sambuddhau, laukai uppaj‍ji tāvadai|

amhākaṃ anukampantau, upāgacchi vināyakau||

‘‘upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ, sumaidhaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

tiṇasanthārakaṃ katvā, laukajaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

‘‘vipinātau madhuṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

sambuddhau paribhuñ‍jitvā, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

‘‘yau  taṃ adāsi madhuṃ mai, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘iminā madhudānaina, tiṇasanthārakaina ca|

tiṃsa kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘daivalaukā idhāgantvā, mātukucchiṃ upāgatai|

madhuvassaṃ pavassittha, chādayaṃ madhunā mahiṃ||

‘‘mayi nikkhantamattamhi, kucchiyā ca suduttarā|

tatrāpi madhuvassaṃ mai, vassatai nic‍cakālikaṃ||

‘‘agārā abhinikkhamma, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

lābhī an‍nassa pānassa, madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sabbakāmasamiddhauhaṃ, bhavitvā daivamānusai|

tainaiva madhudānaina, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

‘‘vuṭṭhamhi  daivai caturaṅgulai tiṇai, sampupphitai dharaṇīruhai sañchan‍nai|

suñ‍ñai gharai maṇḍaparukkhamūlakai, vasāmi nic‍caṃ sukhitau anāsavau||

‘‘majjhai mahantai hīnai ca, bhavai sabbai atik‍kamiṃ|

aj‍ja mai āsavā khīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

ayamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇagāthā ahausīti|

mahānāmatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. pārāpariyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā
