tattha anic‍cāni gahakāni, tattha tattha punappunanti tasmiṃ tasmiṃ bhavai punappunaṃ nibbattamānāni gahakāni attabhāvagaihāni na nibbāni anavaṭṭhitāni ittarāni parittakālāni| gahakāraṃ gavaisantauti imassa attabhāvagaihassa kārakaṃ taṇhāvaḍḍhakiṃ pariyaisantau aittakaṃ kālaṃ anuvicarinti adhippāyau| dukkhā jāti punappunanti idaṃ gahakārakagavaisanassa kāraṇavacanaṃ| yasmā  jarābyādhimaraṇamissatāya jāti nāmaisā punappunaṃ upagantuṃ dukkhā, na ca sā tasmiṃ adiṭṭhai nivattati, tasmā taṃ gavaisantau vicarinti atthau|

gahakāraka  diṭṭhausīti idāni pana yaina sau sak‍kā daṭṭhuṃ, taina ariyamaggañāṇacakkhunā gahakāraka diṭṭhau asi| puna gaihanti puna imasmiṃ saṃsāravaṭṭai attabhāvasaṅkhātaṃ mama gaihaṃ na kāhasi na karissasi| sabbā tai phāsukā bhaggāti tava sabbā anavasaisakilaisaphāsukā mayā bhaggā| thūṇikā ca vidālitāti idāni tayā kātabbassa attabhāvagaihassa avij‍jāsaṅkhātā kaṇṇikā ca bhin‍nā| vimariyādikataṃ cittanti mama cittaṃ vigatantaṃ kataṃ, āyatiṃ anuppattidhammataṃ āpāditaṃ| tatau aiva idhaiva vidhamissati imasmiṃyaiva bhavai viddhaṃsissati, carimakacittaniraudhaina nirujjhissatīti atthau|

sivakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. upavāṇatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

arahaṃ sugatauti āyasmatau upavāṇatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai daliddakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bhagavati parinibbutai tassa dhātuṃ gahaitvā manussadaivanāgagaruḷakumbhaṇḍayakkhagandhabbaihi sattaratanamayai sattayaujanikai thūpai katai tattha sudhautaṃ attanau uttarāsaṅgaṃ vaiḷaggai ābandhitvā dhajaṃ katvā pūjaṃ akāsi| taṃ gahaitvā abhisammatakau nāma yakkhasaināpati daivaihi caitiyapūjārakkhaṇatthaṃ ṭhapitau adissamānakāyau ākāsai dhāraintau caitiyaṃ tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ akāsi| sau taṃ disvā bhiyyausaumattāya pasan‍namānasau ahausi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā upavāṇauti laddhanāmau vayappattau jaitavanapaṭiggahaṇai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau arahattaṃ patvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.56.122-178) –

‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

jalitvā aggikkhandhauva, sambuddhau parinibbutau||

‘‘mahājanā  samāgamma, pūjayitvā tathāgataṃ|

cittaṃ katvāna sugataṃ, sarīraṃ abhiraupayuṃ||

‘‘sarīrakic‍caṃ katvāna, dhātuṃ tattha samānayuṃ|

sadaivamanussā sabbai, buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu tai||

‘‘paṭhamā  kañ‍canamayā, dutiyā ca maṇimayā|

tatiyā rūpiyamayā, catutthī phalikāmayā||

‘‘tattha pañ‍camikā caiva, lauhitaṅkamayā ahu|

chaṭṭhā masāragal‍lassa, sabbaṃ ratanamayūpari||

‘‘jaṅghā maṇimayā āsi, vaidikā ratanāmayā|

sabbasauṇṇamayau thūpau, uddhaṃ yaujanamuggatau||

‘‘daivā tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

‘‘dhātu āvaiṇikā natthi, sarīraṃ aikapiṇḍitaṃ|

imamhi buddhathūpamhi, kassāma kañ‍cukaṃ mayaṃ||

‘‘daivā sattahi ratnaihi, añ‍ñaṃ vaḍḍhaisuṃ yaujanaṃ|

thūpau dviyaujanubbaidhau, timiraṃ byapahanti sau||

‘‘nāgā tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā caiva daivā ca, buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu tai||

‘‘mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

‘‘indanīlaṃ mahānīlaṃ, athau jautirasaṃ maṇiṃ|

aikatau san‍nipātaitvā, buddhathūpaṃ achādayuṃ||

‘‘sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ āsi, yāvatā buddhacaitiyaṃ|

tiyaujanasamubbaidhaṃ, ālaukakaraṇaṃ tadā||

‘‘garuḷā ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā daivanāgā ca, buddhapūjaṃ akaṃsu tai||

‘‘mā  nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

‘‘sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ thūpaṃ, akaruṃ tai ca kañ‍cukaṃ|

yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ||

‘‘catuyaujanamubbaidhau, buddhathūpau viraucati|

aubhāsaiti disā sabbā, sataraṃsīva uggatau||

‘‘kumbhaṇḍā ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā caiva daivā ca, nāgā ca garuḷā tathā||

‘‘pac‍caikaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ|

mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā||

‘‘mayampi  thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau|

ratanaihi chādaissāma, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ||

‘‘yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ|

pañ‍cayaujanamubbaidhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā||

‘‘yakkhā tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā daivanāgā ca, garuḷā ca kumbhaṇḍakā||

‘‘pac‍caikaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ|

mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā||

‘‘mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau|

phalikā chādayissāma, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ||

‘‘yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ|

chayaujanikamubbaidhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā||

‘‘gandhabbā ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manujā daivatā nāgā, kumbhaṇḍā garuḷā tathā||

‘‘sabbai akaṃsu buddhathūpaṃ, mayamaittha akārakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

‘‘vaidiyau  satta katvāna, dhajaṃ chattaṃ akaṃsu tai|

sabbasauṇṇamayaṃ thūpaṃ, gandhabbā kārayuṃ tadā||

‘‘sattayaujanamubbaidhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā|

rattindivā na ñāyanti, ālaukau hauti sabbadā||

‘‘abhibhaunti na tassābhā, candasūrā satārakā|

samantā yaujanasatai, padīpaupi na paj‍jali||

‘‘taina kālaina yai kaici, thūpaṃ pūjainti mānusā|

na tai thūpaṃ āruhanti, ambarai ukkhipanti tai||

‘‘daivaihi ṭhapitau yakkhau, abhisammatanāmakau|

dhajaṃ vā pupphadāmaṃ vā, abhiraupaiti uttariṃ||

‘‘na tai passanti taṃ yakkhaṃ, dāmaṃ passanti gacchatau|

aivaṃ passitvā gacchantā, sabbai gacchanti suggatiṃ||

‘‘viruddhā yai pāvacanai, pasan‍nā yai ca sāsanai|

pāṭihīraṃ daṭṭhukāmā, thūpaṃ pūjainti mānusā||

‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ bhatakau tadā|

āmauditaṃ janaṃ disvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā||

‘‘uḷārau  bhagavā naisau, yassa dhātugharai disaṃ|

imā ca janatā tuṭṭhā, kāraṃ kubbaṃ na tapparai||

‘‘ahampi kāraṃ kassāmi, laukanāthassa tādinau|

tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, bhavissāmi anāgatai||

‘‘sudhautaṃ rajakaināhaṃ, uttaraiyyaṃ paṭaṃ mama|

vaiḷaggai ālaggaitvāna, dhajaṃ ukkhipimambarai||

‘‘abhisammatakau gayha, ambarai hāsi mai dhajaṃ|

vātairitaṃ dhajaṃ disvā, bhiyyau hāsaṃ janaisahaṃ||

‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, samaṇaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā, vipākaṃ pucchahaṃ dhajai||

‘‘sau  mai kathaisi ānandī, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama|

tassa dhajassa vipākaṃ, anubhaussasi sabbadā||

‘‘hatthiassarathāpattī, sainā ca caturaṅginī|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘saṭṭhitūriyasahassāni, bhairiyau samalaṅkatā|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘chaḷasīti sahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā||

‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissasi|

asītikkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissasi||

‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

puñ‍ñakammaina sañ‍ñuttau, brahmabandhu bhavissasi||

‘‘asītikauṭiṃ chaḍḍaitvā, dāsai kammakarai bahū|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissasi||

‘‘ārādhayitvā sambuddhaṃ, gautamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ|

upavāṇauti nāmaina, haissasi satthu sāvakau||

‘‘satasahassai  kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva, kilaisai jhāpayiṃ mama||

‘‘cak‍kavattissa santassa, cātuddīpissarassa mai|

tīṇi yaujanāni sāmantā, ussīyanti dhajā sadā||

‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

athāyasmā  upavāṇau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau ahausi| taina ca samayaina bhagavatau vātābādhau uppaj‍ji| thairassa ca gihisahāyau daivahitau nāma brāhmaṇau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati| sau thairaṃ catūhi pac‍cayaihi pavaidaisi| athāyasmā upavāṇau nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya tassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ upagacchi| brāhmaṇau ‘‘kainaci añ‍ñaina payaujanaina thairau āgatau’’ti ñatvā, ‘‘vadaiyyātha, bhantai, kainatthau’’ti āha| thairau tassa brāhmaṇassa payaujanaṃ ācikkhantau –

185.

 ‘‘arahaṃ sugatau laukai, vātaihābādhikau muni|

sacai uṇhaudakaṃ atthi, muninau daihi brāhmaṇa||

186.

 ‘‘pūjitau pūjanaiyyānaṃ, sak‍karaiyyāna sak‍katau|

apacitaupacaiyyānaṃ, tassa icchāmi hātavai’’ti|| – gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tassatthau – yau imasmiṃ laukai pūjanaiyyānaṃ pūjaitabbaihi sak‍kādīhi daivaihi mahābrahmādīhi ca brahmaihi pūjitau, sak‍karaiyyānaṃ sak‍kātabbaihi bimbisārakausalarājādīhi sak‍katau, apacaiyyānaṃ apacāyitabbaihi mahaisīhi khīṇāsavaihi apacitau, kilaisaihi ārakattādinā arahaṃ, saubhanagamanādinā sugatau sabbañ‍ñū muni mayhaṃ satthā daivadaivau sak‍kānaṃ atisak‍kau brahmānaṃ atibrahmā, sau dāni vātaihi vātahaitu vātakkhaubhanimittaṃ ābādhikau jātau| sacai, brāhmaṇa, uṇhaudakaṃ atthi, tassa vātābādhavūpasamanatthaṃ taṃ hātavai upanaituṃ icchāmīti| taṃ sutvā brāhmaṇau uṇhaudakaṃ tadanurūpaṃ vātārahañ‍ca  bhaisaj‍jaṃ bhagavatau upanāmaisi| taina ca satthu raugau vūpasami| tassa bhagavā anumaudanaṃ akāsīti|

upavāṇatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. isidin‍natthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

diṭṭhā  mayāti āyasmatau isidin‍natthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau bījaniṃ gahaitvā baudhiyā pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sunāparantajanapadai saiṭṭhikulai nibbattitvā isidin‍nauti laddhanāmau vayappattau satthu candanamāḷapaṭiggahaṇai pāṭihāriyaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau satthāraṃ  upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā sautāpan‍nau hutvā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| tassa hitānukampinī daivatā taṃ caudaintī –

187.

 ‘‘diṭṭhā mayā dhammadharā upāsakā, kāmā anic‍cā iti bhāsamānā|

sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu dāraisu ca tai apaikkhā||

188.

 ‘‘addhā na jānanti yataudha dhammaṃ, kāmā anic‍cā iti cāpi āhu|

rāgañ‍ca taisaṃ na balatthi chaittuṃ, tasmā sitā puttadāraṃ dhanañ‍cā’’ti|| –

gāthādvayamabhāsi|

tattha diṭṭhā mayā dhammadharā upāsakā, kāmā anic‍cā iti bhāsamānāti idhaikac‍cai pariyattidhammadharā upāsakā mayā diṭṭhā, pariyattidhammadharattā  aiva ‘‘kāmā nāmaitai anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti kāmaisu ādīnavapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammaṃ bhāsamānā, sayaṃ pana sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu dāraisu ca tai apaikkhāti sārattā hutvā bahalarāgarattā maṇīsu kuṇḍalaisu ca, maṇicitaisu vā kuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu puttadhītāsu dāraisu ca adhigatasnaihā, añ‍ñaṃ bhaṇantā añ‍ñaṃ karauntā diṭṭhā mayāti atthau|

yatauti yasmā tai upāsakā sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttaisu dāraisu ca apaikkhavantau, tasmā idha imasmiṃ buddhasāsanai dhammaṃ yāthāvatau addhā aikaṃsaina na jānanti| aivaṃ bhūtā ca ‘‘kāmā anic‍cā’’iti cāpi āhu ahausi, sattapakati vicittasabhāvāti adhippāyau| rāgañ‍ca taisaṃ na balatthi chaittunti taisaṃ upāsakānaṃ yasmā rāgaṃ chaittuṃ samucchindituṃ tādisaṃ ñāṇabalaṃ natthi, tasmā taina kāraṇaina sitā taṇhāvasaina nissitā puttadāraṃ dhanañ‍ca al‍līnā na vissaj‍jaintīti sabbamaitaṃ daivatā taṃyaiva upāsakaṃ uddissa añ‍ñāpadaisaina kathaisi| taṃ sutvā upāsakau saṃvaigajātau pabbajitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.48.46-50) –

‘‘vipassinau  bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

sumanau bījaniṃ gayha, abījiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, abījiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bījanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau imā aiva gāthā abhāsīti|

isidin‍natthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. sambulakac‍cānatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

daivau  cāti āyasmatau sambulakac‍cānatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau itau catunavutikappamatthakai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ sataraṃsiṃ nāma pac‍caikabuddhaṃ niraudhā vuṭṭhahitvā piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau tālaphalaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai gahapatikulai nibbattitvā ‘‘sambulau’’ti laddhanāmau kac‍cānagauttatāya sambulakac‍cānauti pañ‍ñāyittha|

sau vayappattau satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā himavantasamīpai bhairavāya nāma pabbataguhāyaṃ vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau viharati| athaikadivasaṃ mahā akālamaighau satapaṭalasahassapaṭalau thanaintau gaj‍jantau vij‍jul‍latā nicchāraintau gaḷagaḷāyantau uṭṭhahitvā vassituṃ ārabhi, asaniyau phaliṃsu| taṃ saddaṃ sutvā acchataracchuvanamahiṃsahatthiādayau bhītatasitā bhītaravaṃ viraviṃsu| thairau pana āraddhavipassanattā kāyai jīvitai ca nirapaikkhau vigatalaumahaṃsau taṃ acintaintau vipassanāyamaiva kammaṃ karauntau ghammāpagamaina utusappāyalābhaina samāhitacittau tāvadaiva vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā saha abhiñ‍ñāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.51.85-90) –

‘‘sataraṃsī nāma bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikā vuṭṭhahitvāna, gaucarāyābhinikkhami||

‘‘phalahatthau  ahaṃ disvā, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, tālaphalaṃ adāsahaṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā saumanassajātau udānavasaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

189.

 ‘‘daivau ca vassati, daivau ca gaḷagaḷāyati,

aikakau cāhaṃ bhairavai bilai viharāmi|

tassa mayhaṃ aikakassa bhairavai bilai viharatau,

natthi bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā||

190.

 ‘‘dhammatā mamaisā yassa mai, aikakassa bhairavai bilai|

viharatau natthi bhayaṃ vā, chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā’’ti|| –

gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tattha  daivau ca vassati, daivau ca gaḷagaḷāyatīti daivau maighau vassati ca, ‘‘gaḷagaḷā’’ti ca karauntau gaj‍jatīti atthau| gaj‍jantassa hi anukaraṇamaitaṃ| aikakau cāhaṃ bhairavai bilai viharāmīti ahañ‍ca aikakau asahāyau sappaṭibhayāyaṃ pabbataguhāyaṃ vasāmi, tassa mayhaṃ aivaṃbhūtassa mai satau natthi bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vāti cittutrāsasañ‍ñitaṃ bhayaṃ vā taṃnimittakaṃ sarīrassa chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsanamattaṃ vā natthi|

kasmāti tattha kāraṇamāha ‘‘dhammatā mamaisā’’ti| apariñ‍ñātavatthukassa hi tattha appahīnacchandarāgatāya bhayādinā bhavitabbaṃ, mayā pana sabbasau tattha pariñ‍ñātaṃ, tattha ca chandarāgau samucchin‍nau, tasmā bhayādīnaṃ abhāvau dhammatā mamaisā mama dhammasabhāvau aisauti añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

sambulakac‍cānatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. nitakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

kassa  sailūpamaṃ cittanti āyasmatau nitakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai bandhumatīnagarai ārāmagaupakau hutvā jīvantau aikadivasaṃ bhagavantaṃ ākāsaina gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau nāḷikairaphalaṃ dātukāmau ahausi| satthā taṃ anuggaṇhantau ākāsaiyaiva ṭhatvā paṭiggaṇhi| sau taṃ datvā uḷāraṃ pītisaumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā nitakauti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā arañ‍ñai viharantau ghaṭaintau arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.51.91-99) –

‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, ārāmikau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

‘‘nāḷikairaphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

ākāsai ṭhitakau santau, paṭiggaṇhi mahāyasau||

‘‘vittisañ‍jananau mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘adhigacchiṃ tadā pītiṃ, vipulañ‍ca sukhuttamaṃ|

uppaj‍jataiva ratanaṃ, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘dibbacakkhu visuddhaṃ mai, samādhikusalau ahaṃ|

abhiñ‍ñāpāramippattau, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā thairai phalasukhaina nibbānasukhaina viharantai padhānapariggāhakau thairau taṃ ārañ‍ñāyatanaṃ gantvā tattha vasantānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pariggaṇhanatthaṃ ‘‘kassa sailūpama’’ntiādinā paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha|

191. tattha  kassa sailūpamaṃ cittaṃ, ṭhitaṃ nānupakampatīti imasmiṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai vasantaisu kassa bhikkhunau cittaṃ aggaphalādhigamaina aikaghanasilāmayapabbatūpamaṃ sabbaisaṃ iñ‍janānaṃ abhāvatau vasībhāvappattiyā ca ṭhitaṃ sabbaihipi laukadhammaihi nānukampati na vaidhati| idānissa akampanākāraṃ saddhiṃ kāraṇaina dassaituṃ ‘‘viratta’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha virattaṃ rajanīyaisūti virāgasaṅkhātaina ariyamaggaina rajanīyaisu rāguppattihaitubhūtaisu taibhūmakadhammaisu virattaṃ, tattha sabbasau samucchin‍narāganti atthau| kuppanīyaiti paṭighaṭṭhānīyai, sabbasmimpi āghātavatthusmiṃ| na kuppatīti na dussati na vikāraṃ āpaj‍jati| yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittanti yassa ariyapuggalassa cittaṃ manau aivaṃ vuttanayaina tādibhāvaina bhāvitaṃ, kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatīti taṃ puggalaṃ kutau sattatau saṅkhāratau vā dukkhaṃ upagamissati, na tādisassa dukkhaṃ atthīti atthau|

192. aivaṃ aniyamavasaina pucchitamatthaṃ nitakatthairau attūpanāyikaṃ katvā vissaj‍jaintau ‘‘mama sailūpamaṃ citta’’ntiādinā dutiyagāthāya añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| taṃ vuttatthamaiva|

nitakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. sauṇapauṭiriyaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

na tāva supituṃ hautīti āyasmatau sauṇassa pauṭiriyaputtassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai vanacarau hutvā jīvantau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau kurañ‍jiyaphalaṃ  satthunau adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kapilavatthusmiṃ pauṭiriyassa nāma gāmabhaujakassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, sauṇautissa nāmaṃ ahausi| sau vayappattau bhaddiyassa sākiyarañ‍ñau saināpati ahausi| atha bhaddiyarājai haiṭṭhā vuttanayaina pabbajitai, saināpati  ‘‘rājāpi nāma pabbaji, kiṃ mayhaṃ gharāvāsainā’’ti pabbaji? pabbajitvā pana niddārāmau viharati, na bhāvanamanuyuñ‍jati| taṃ bhagavā anupiyāyaṃ ambavanai viharantau attanau aubhāsaṃ pharāpaitvā tainassa satiṃ janaitvā imāya gāthāya taṃ auvadantau –

193.

 ‘‘na tāva supituṃ hauti, ratti nakkhattamālinī|

paṭijaggitumaivaisā, ratti hauti vijānatā||

194.

 ‘‘hatthikkhandhāvapatitaṃ, kuñ‍jarau cai anuk‍kamai|

saṅgāmai mai mataṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai jīvai parājitau’’ti|| – gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tattha na tāva supituṃ hauti, ratti nakkhattamālinīti aṭṭhahi akkhaṇaihi vaj‍jitaṃ navamaṃ khaṇaṃ labhitvā ṭhitassa viñ‍ñujātikassa yāva na arahattaṃ hatthagataṃ hauti, tāva ayaṃ nakkhattamālinī ratti supituṃ niddāyituṃ na hauti, supanassa kālau na hauti| apica khau paṭijaggitumaivaisā, ratti hauti vijānatāti aisā ratti nāma manussānaṃ migapakkhīnañ‍ca niddūpagamanaina visaisatau nissaddavailābhūtā paṭipattiṃ attani sañ‍jaggituṃ jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuñ‍jitumaiva vijānatā viñ‍ñunā icchitā hautīti|

taṃ sutvā sauṇau saṃviggataramānasau hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhapaitvā abbhaukāsikaṅgaṃ adhiṭṭhāya vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau ‘‘hatthikkhandhauva patita’’nti dutiyaṃ gāthamāha| tattha avapatitanti avamukhaṃ patitaṃ uddhaṃpādaṃ adhaumukhaṃ patitaṃ| kuñ‍jarau cai anuk‍kamaiti kuñ‍jarau anuk‍kamaiyya cai| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yadāhaṃ hatthimāruhitvā saṅgāmaṃ paviṭṭhau hatthikkhandhatau patitau, tadāhaṃ saṅgāmai taina hatthinā madditau matau ahausiṃ cai, taṃ mai maraṇaṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai idāni kilaisaihi parājitau jīvaiyyaṃ, taṃ na saiyyauti| imaṃ gāthaṃ vadantauyaiva vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.52.1-6) –

‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, vipinai vicaraṃ ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sabbadhammāna pāraguṃ||

‘‘kurañ‍jiyaphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittassa tādinau, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā ‘‘satthārā vuttaṃ, attanā vutta’’nti ubhayañhi gāthaṃ ‘‘hatthikkhandhāvapatita’’ntiādinā pac‍cudāhāsi| taina idamaiva añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

sauṇapauṭiriyaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. nisabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

pañ‍ca  kāmaguṇai hitvāti āyasmatau nisabhatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau kapitthaphalamadāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kauliyajanapadai kulagaihai nibbattitvā nisabhauti laddhanāmau vayappattau sākiyakauliyānaṃ saṅgāmai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā tadahaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.52.7-11) –

‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, kapitthaṃ adadiṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

sau  arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau sahāyabhikkhū pamādavihāraina kālaṃ vītināmaintai disvā tai auvadantau –

195.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai hitvā, piyarūpai manauramai|

saddhāya gharā nikkhamma, dukkhassantakarau bhavai’’ti|| –

paṭhamaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi|

tassatthau – bālaputhuj‍janassa piyāyitabbasabhāvatāya piyarūpai manuñ‍ñasabhāvatāya manauramai rūpādikai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai kāmakauṭṭhāsai hitvā pahāya paric‍cajitvā kammaphalasaddhāya ratanattayasaddhāya ca vasaina gharā gharabandhanatau nikkhamma nikkhamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ upagatau viñ‍ñujātikau pabbajitakālatau paṭṭhāya ghaṭaintau vāyamantau vaṭṭadukkhassa antakarau bhavai bhavaiyyāti| aivaṃ tai bhikkhū auvaditvā ‘‘ayaṃ parai aiva sañ‍ñāpaintau viharati, ‘‘sayaṃ  pana akārakau’ti mā cintayitthā’’ti taisaṃ attanau paṭipan‍nabhāvaṃ pakāsaintau –

196.

 ‘‘nābhinandāmi  maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ|

kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, sampajānau patissatau’’ti|| –

dutiyagāthāya añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| tattha nābhinandāmi maraṇanti maraṇaṃ na abhikaṅkhāmi| nābhinandāmi jīvitanti idaṃ pana tassa kāraṇavacanaṃ, yasmā nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ, tasmā nābhinandāmi maraṇanti| yau hi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇāya kilaisābhisaṅkhārai ācinauti upacinauti, sau punabbhavābhinibbattiṃ abhinandantau nāntariyakatāya attanau maraṇampi abhinandati nāma kāraṇassa appahīnattā, khīṇāsavau pana sabbasau ācayagāmidhammai pahāya apacayagāmidhammai patiṭṭhitau pariñ‍ñātavatthukau sabbasau jīvitaṃ anabhinandantau maraṇampi anabhinandati nāma kāraṇassa aiva suppahīnattā| taināha – ‘‘nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ, nābhinandāmi jīvita’’nti| yadi aivaṃ khīṇāsavassa parinibbānābhikaṅkhā, yāva parinibbānā avaṭṭhānañ‍ca kathanti āha ‘‘kālañ‍ca paṭikaṅkhāmi, sampajānau patissatau’’ti, kilaisaparinibbānai siddhai satipañ‍ñāvaipul‍lappattiyā satau sampajānau kaivalaṃ  khandhaparinibbānakālaṃ paṭikaṅkhāmi, taṃ udikkhamānau āgamayamānau viharāmi, na pana mai maraṇai jīvitai vā abhinandanā atthi arahattamaggainaiva tassa samugghāṭitattāti|

nisabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. usabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

ambapal‍lavasaṅkāsanti āyasmatau usabhatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau kausambaphalaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kapilavatthusmiṃ sākiyarājakulai nibbattitvā usabhauti laddhanāmau vayappattau satthu ñātisamāgamai buddhānubhāvaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbaji| sau pabbajitakālatau  paṭṭhāya samaṇadhammaṃ akatvā divā saṅgaṇikārāmau sakalarattiṃ niddāyamānau vītināmaiti| sau aikadivasaṃ muṭṭhassati asampajānau niddaṃ auk‍kantau supinai kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā ambapal‍lavavaṇṇaṃ cīvaraṃ pārupitvā hatthigīvāyaṃ nisīditvā nagaraṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhaṃ tatthaiva manussai sampattai disvā laj‍jāya hatthikkhandhatau auruyha attānaṃ disvā paṭibuddhau, ‘‘īdisaṃ nāma supinaṃ muṭṭhassatinā asampajānaina  niddāyamānaina mayā diṭṭha’’nti uppan‍nasaṃvaigau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.52.12-16) –

‘‘kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, kausambaṃ adadiṃ tadā||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

atha  thairau yathādiṭṭhaṃ supinaṃ aṅkusaṃ katvā arahattassa adhigatattā tassaiva supinassa kittanavasaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

197.

 ‘‘ambapal‍lavasaṅkāsaṃ, aṃsai katvāna cīvaraṃ|

nisin‍nau hatthigīvāyaṃ, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ||

198.

 ‘‘hatthikkhandhatau auruyha, saṃvaigaṃ alabhiṃ tadā|

sauhaṃ dittau tadā santau, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti|| – gāthādvayamāha|

tattha ambapal‍lavasaṅkāsaṃ, aṃsai katvāna cīvaranti ambapal‍lavākāraṃ pavāḷavaṇṇaṃ cīvaraṃ khandhai karitvā uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā| gāmanti attanau rājadhāniṃ hatthikkhandhai nisin‍nau piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ, paviṭṭhamattauva mahājanaina aulaukiyamānau hatthikkhandhatau auruyha ṭhitau paṭibujjhiṃ, pabuddhauva saṃvaigaṃ alabhiṃ tadā ‘‘muṭṭhassati asampajānau hutvā niddāyauk‍kamanaina aitaṃ jāta’’nti| aparai pana ‘‘rājāva hutvā rattibhāgai aivarūpaṃ supinaṃ disvā vibhātāya rattiyā hatthikkhandhaṃ āruyha nagaravīthiyaṃ carantau taṃ supinaṃ saritvā hatthikkhandhatau auruyha saṃvaigaṃ labhitvā satthu santikai pabbajitvā arahattaṃ patvā udānaṃ udānaintau imā gāthā abhāsī’’ti vadanti| dittauti tasmiṃ rājakālai jātimadabhaugamadādiparidappitau samānau saṃvaigamalabhinti yaujanā|

usabhatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. kappaṭakuratthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

ayamiti  kappaṭauti āyasmatau kappaṭakuratthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti| ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ bhagavantaṃ vinatāya nāma nadiyā tīrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau kaitakapupphaihi pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai  sāvatthiyaṃ duggatakulai nibbattitvā yāva vayappatti, tāva añ‍ñaṃ upāyaṃ ajānantau kappaṭakhaṇḍanivāsanau sarāvahatthau tattha tattha kuraṃ pariyaisantau vicari, taina kappaṭakurautvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| sau vayappattau tiṇaṃ vik‍kiṇitvā jīvikaṃ kappaintau aikadivasaṃ tiṇalāvanatthaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ gatau tattha añ‍ñataraṃ khīṇāsavatthairaṃ disvā taṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā nisīdi| tassa thairau dhammaṃ kathaisi| sau dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau ‘‘kiṃ mai imāya kicchajīvikāyā’’ti pabbajitvā attanau nivatthakappaṭacauḷaṃ aikasmiṃ ṭhānai nikkhipi| yadā cassa anabhirati uppaj‍jati, tadā taṃ kappaṭaṃ aulaukaintassa anabhirati vigacchati, saṃvaigaṃ paṭilabhi| aivaṃ karauntau sattakkhattuṃ uppabbaji| tassa taṃ kāraṇaṃ bhikkhū bhagavatau āraucaisuṃ| athaikadivasaṃ kappaṭakurau bhikkhu dhammasabhāyaṃ parisapariyantai nisin‍nau niddāyati, taṃ bhagavā caudaintau –

199.

 ‘‘ayamiti kappaṭau kappaṭakurau, acchāya atibharitāya|

amataghaṭikāyaṃ dhammakaṭamattau, katapadaṃ jhānāni aucaituṃ||

200.

 ‘‘mā khau tvaṃ kappaṭa pacālaisi, mā tvaṃ upakaṇṇamhi tāḷaissaṃ|

na hi tvaṃ kappaṭa mattamañ‍ñāsi, saṅghamajjhamhi pacalāyamānau’’ti|| –

gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tattha ayamiti kappaṭau kappaṭakurauti kappaṭakurau bhikkhu ‘‘ayaṃ mama kappaṭau, imaṃ paridahitvā yathā tathā jīvāmī’’ti aivaṃ uppan‍namicchāvitak‍kau acchāya atibharitāya amataghaṭikāyaṃ mama amataghaṭai tahaṃ tahaṃ vassantai ‘‘amatamadhigataṃ ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ  dhammaṃ daisaimi’’ (mahāva॰ 12; ma॰ ni॰ 1.286; 2.342)| ‘‘andhībhūtasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, āhañchaṃ amatadundubhi’’ntiādinā (mahāva॰ 11; ma॰ ni॰ 1.285; 2.341) ghausaitvā  mayā dhammāmatai pavassiyamānai katapadaṃ jhānāni aucaituṃ laukiyalaukuttarajjhānāni upacaituṃ bhāvaituṃ katapadaṃ kaṭamaggavihitabhāvanāmaggaṃ idaṃ mama sāsanaṃ, tathāpi dhammakaṭamattau mama sāsanadhammatau  uk‍kaṇṭhacittau apagatamānasau kappaṭakurauti taṃ caudaitvā punapissa sahauḍḍhaṃ cauraṃ gaṇhantau viya pamādavihāraṃ dassaintau ‘‘mā khau tvaṃ, kappaṭa, pacālaisī’’ti gāthamāha|

tattha mā khau tvaṃ, kappaṭa, pacālaisīti tvaṃ, kappaṭakura, ‘‘mama dhammaṃ suṇissāmī’’ti nisīditvā mā khau pacālaisi mā pacalāhi mā niddaṃ upagacchi| mā tvaṃ upakaṇṇamhi tāḷaissanti taṃ niddāyamānaṃ upakaṇṇamhi kaṇṇasamīpai daisanāhatthaina ahaṃ mā patāḷaissaṃ| yathā itau paraṃ kilaisappahānāya ahaṃ taṃ na auvadaiyyaṃ, tathā paṭipaj‍jāhīti atthau| na hi tvaṃ, kappaṭa, mattamañ‍ñāsīti tvaṃ, kappaṭa, saṅghamajjhamhi pacalāyamānau mattaṃ pamāṇaṃ na vā mañ‍ñasi, ‘‘ayamatidul‍labhau khaṇau paṭiladdhau, sau mā upajjhagā’’ti aittakampi na jānāsi, passa yāva ca tai aparaddhanti caudaisi|

aivaṃ bhagavatā dvīhi gāthāhi gāḷhaṃ taṃ niggayha caudanāya katāya aṭṭhivaidhaviddhau viya caṇḍagajau maggaṃ autarantau viya ca sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.52.17-22) –

‘‘vinatānadiyā tīrai, vihāsi purisuttamau|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

‘‘madhugandhassa pupphaina, kaitakassa ahaṃ tadā|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhasaiṭṭhamapūjayiṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā satthārā vuttagāthādvayamaiva attanau arahattādhigamanassa aṅkusabhūtanti pac‍cudāhāsi| tainassa tadaiva añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

kappaṭakuratthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

catutthavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. pañ‍camavaggau

1. kumārakassapatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

ahau  buddhā ahau dhammāti āyasmatau kumārakassapatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇi| ‘‘kulagaihai’’ti pana aṅguttaraṭṭhakathāyaṃ (a॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.217) vuttaṃ| sau satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ cittakathikānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā sayampi taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ ākaṅkhantau paṇidhānaṃ katvā tadanurūpāni puñ‍ñāni karauntau kassapassa bhagavatau kālai samaṇadhammaṃ katvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai saiṭṭhidhītāya kucchimhi paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi| sā kira kumārikākālaiyaiva pabbajitukāmā hutvā mātāpitarau yācitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ alabhamānā kulagharaṃ gatāpi gabbhasaṇṭhitampi ajānantī sāmikaṃ ārādhaitvā taina anuñ‍ñātā bhikkhunīsu pabbajitā| tassā gabbhinibhāvaṃ disvā bhikkhuniyau daivadattaṃ pucchiṃsu| sau ‘‘assamaṇī’’ti āha| puna dasabalaṃ pucchiṃsu| satthā upālitthairaṃ paṭicchāpaisi| thairau sāvatthinagaravāsīni kulāni visākhañ‍ca upāsikaṃ pak‍kausāpaitvā sarājikāya parisāya vinicchinantau ‘‘purai laddhau gabbhau, pabbaj‍jā araugā’’ti āha| satthā ‘‘suvinicchitaṃ adhikaraṇa’’nti thairassa sādhukāraṃ adāsi|

sā bhikkhunī suvaṇṇabimbasadisaṃ puttaṃ vijāyi| taṃ rājā pasainadikausalau pausaisi| ‘‘kassapau’’ti cassa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| aparabhāgai  alaṅkaritvā satthu santikaṃ naitvā pabbājaisi| kumārakālai pabbajitattā bhagavatā ‘‘kassapaṃ pak‍kausatha, idaṃ phalaṃ vā khādanīyaṃ vā kassapassa daithā’’ti vuttai ‘‘katarakassapassā’’ti| ‘‘kumārakassapassā’’ti| aivaṃ gahitanāmattā rañ‍ñau pausāvanikaputtattā ca vuḍḍhakālaipi kumārakassapautvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha|

sau pabbajitakālatau paṭṭhāya vipassanāya caiva kammaṃ karauti, buddhavacanañ‍ca uggaṇhāti| atha taina saddhiṃ pabbatamatthakai samaṇadhammaṃ katvā anāgāmī hutvā suddhāvāsai nibbattau  mahābrahmā ‘‘vipassanāya mukhaṃ dassaitvā maggaphalappattiyā upāyaṃ karissāmī’’ti pañ‍cadasa pañhai abhisaṅkharitvā andhavanai vasantassa  thairassa ‘‘imai pañhai satthāraṃ pucchaiyyāsī’’ti ācikkhitvā gatau| sau tai pañhai bhagavantaṃ pucchi| bhagavāpissa byākāsi| thairau satthārā kathitaniyāmainaiva tai uggaṇhitvā vipassanaṃ gabbhaṃ gaṇhāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.54.150-177) –

‘‘itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji nāyakau|

sabbalaukahitau vīrau, padumuttaranāmakau||

‘‘tadāhaṃ brāhmaṇau hutvā, vissutau vaidapāragū|

divāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintaṃ, baudhayantaṃ sadaivakaṃ|

vicittakathikānaggaṃ, vaṇṇayantaṃ mahājanai||

‘‘tadā muditacittauhaṃ, nimantaitvā tathāgataṃ|

nānārattaihi vatthaihi, alaṅkaritvāna maṇḍapaṃ||

‘‘nānāratanapaj‍jautaṃ, sasaṅghaṃ bhaujayiṃ tahiṃ|

bhaujayitvāna sattāhaṃ, nānaggarasabhaujanaṃ||

‘‘nānācittaihi pupphaihi, pūjayitvā sasāvakaṃ|

nipac‍ca pādamūlamhi, taṃ ṭhāna patthayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘tadā munivarau āha, karuṇaikarasāsayau|

passathaitaṃ dijavaraṃ, padumānanalaucanaṃ||

‘‘pītipāmauj‍jabahulaṃ, samuggatatanūruhaṃ|

hāsamhitavisālakkhaṃ, mama sāsanalālasaṃ||

‘‘patitaṃ pādamūlai mai, aikāvatthasumānasaṃ|

aisa patthaiti taṃ ṭhānaṃ, vicittakathikattanaṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kumārakassapau nāma, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘vicittapupphadussānaṃ, ratanānañ‍ca vāhasā|

vicittakathikānaṃ sau, aggataṃ pāpuṇissati||

‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

‘‘paribbhamaṃ bhavābhavai, raṅgamajjhai yathā naṭau|

sākhamigatrajau hutvā, migiyā kucchimauk‍kamiṃ||

‘‘tadā mayi kucchigatai, vajjhavārau upaṭṭhitau|

sākhaina cattā mai mātā, nigraudhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā||

‘‘taina sā migarājaina, maraṇā parimaucitā|

paric‍cajitvā sapāṇaṃ, mamaivaṃ auvadī tadā||

‘‘nigraudhamaiva saivaiyya, na sākhamupasaṃvasai|

nigraudhasmiṃ mataṃ saiyyau, yañ‍cai sākhamhi jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tainānusiṭṭhā migayūthapaina, ahañ‍ca mātā ca tathaitarai ca|

āgamma rammaṃ tusitādhivāsaṃ, gatā pavāsaṃ sagharaṃ yathaiva||

‘‘punau kassapavīrassa, atthamaintamhi sāsanai|

āruyha sailasikharaṃ, yuñ‍jitvā jinasāsanaṃ||

‘‘idānāhaṃ  rājagahai, jātau saiṭṭhikulai ahuṃ|

āpan‍nasattā mai mātā, pabbaji anagāriyaṃ||

‘‘sagabbhaṃ taṃ viditvāna, daivadattamupānayuṃ|

sau avauca vināsaitha, pāpikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ imaṃ||

‘‘idānipi munindaina, jinaina anukampitā|

sukhinī ajanī mayhaṃ, mātā bhikkhunupassayai||

‘‘taṃ viditvā mahīpālau, kausalau maṃ apausayi|

kumāraparihānaina, nāmaināhañ‍ca kassapau||

‘‘mahākassapamāgamma, ahaṃ kumārakassapau|

vammikasadisaṃ kāyaṃ, sutvā buddhaina daisitaṃ||

‘‘tatau cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, anupādāya sabbasau|

pāyāsiṃ damayitvāhaṃ, aitadaggamapāpuṇiṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā cittakathikabhāvaina satthārā aitadaggai ṭhapitau attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā ratanattayaguṇavibhāvanamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

201.

 ‘‘ahau  buddhā ahau dhammā, ahau nau satthu sampadā|

yattha aitādisaṃ dhammaṃ, sāvakau sacchikāhiti||

202.

 ‘‘asaṅkhaiyyaisu kappaisu, sak‍kāyādhigatā ahū|

taisamayaṃ pacchimakau, carimauyaṃ samussayau|

jātimaraṇasaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|| – gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tattha ahauti acchariyatthai nipātau| buddhāti sabbañ‍ñubuddhā, gāravavasaina bahuvacanaṃ, ahau acchariyā sambuddhāti atthau| dhammāti pariyattidhammaina saddhiṃ nava laukuttaradhammā| ahau nau satthu sampadāti amhākaṃ satthu dasabalassa ahau sampattiyau| yatthāti yasmiṃ satthari  brahmacariyavāsaina| aitādisaṃ dhammaṃ, sāvakau sacchikāhitīti aitādisaṃ aivarūpaṃ suvisuddhajjhānābhiñ‍ñāparivāraṃ anavasaisakilaisakkhayāvahaṃ santaṃ paṇītaṃ anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ sāvakaupi nāma sacchikarissati, tasmā aivaṃvidhaguṇavisaisādhigamahaitubhūtā ahau acchariyā buddhā bhagavantau, acchariyā dhammaguṇā, acchariyā amhākaṃ satthu sampattiyauti ratanattayassa guṇādhimuttiṃ pavaidaisīti| dhammasampattikittanainaiva hi saṅghasuppaṭipatti kittitā hautīti|

aivaṃ sādhāraṇavasaina dassitaṃ dhammassa sacchikiriyaṃ idāni attupanāyikaṃ katvā dassaintau ‘‘asaṅkhaiyyaisū’’ti gāthamāha| tattha asaṅkhaiyyaisūti gaṇanapathaṃ vītivattaisu mahākappaisu| sak‍kāyāti pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| tai hi paramatthatau vij‍jamānadhammasamūhatāya ‘‘sak‍kāyā’’ti vuc‍canti| ahūti nivattanūpāyassa anadhigatattā anapagatā ahaisuṃ| taisamayaṃ pacchimakau carimauyaṃ samussayauti  yasmā ayaṃ sabbapacchimakau, tatau aiva carimau, tasmā jātimaraṇasahitau khandhādipaṭipāṭisañ‍ñitau saṃsārau idāni āyatiṃ punabbhavābhāvatau punabbhavau natthi, ayamantimā jātīti atthau|

kumārakassapatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. dhammapālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yau havai daharau bhikkhūti āyasmatau dhammapālatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau atthadassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina vanantaṃ upagatau satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau  pilakkhaphalamadāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai satthari parinibbutai avantiraṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā dhammapālauti laddhanāmau vayappattau tak‍kasilaṃ gantvā sippaṃ uggahaitvā paṭinivattaintau antarāmaggai aikasmiṃ vihārai añ‍ñataraṃ thairaṃ disvā tassa santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā  vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.39.21-25) –

‘‘vanantarai buddhaṃ disvā, atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pilakkhassādadiṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā samāpattisukhaina vītināmaintau aikadivasaṃ tasmiṃ vihārai dvai sāmaṇairai rukkhaggai pupphāni aucinantai ārūḷhasākhāya bhaggāya patantai disvā thairau iddhānubhāvaina hatthaina gahaitvā araugaiyaiva bhūmiyaṃ ṭhapaitvā taisaṃ sāmaṇairānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintau –

203.

 ‘‘yau havai daharau bhikkhu, yuñ‍jati buddhasāsanai|

jāgarau sa hi suttaisu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

204.

 ‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti|| – imā dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha yauti aniyamavacanaṃ| havaiti daḷhatthai nipātau| daharauti taruṇau| bhikkhatīti bhikkhu| yuñ‍jatīti ghaṭati vāyamati| jāgarauti  jāgaraṇadhammasaman‍nāgatau| suttaisūti supantaisu| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti yau bhikkhu daharauva samānau taruṇau ‘‘tathāhaṃ pacchā vuḍḍhakālai jānissāmī’’ti acintaitvā buddhānaṃ sāsanai appamādapaṭipattiyaṃ yuñ‍jati samathavipassanābhāvanāya yaugaṃ karauti, sau suttaisu avij‍jāniddāya suttaisu pamattaisu saddhādijāgaradhammasaman‍nāgamaina jāgarau, tatau aiva attahitaparahitapāripūriyā amaughaṃ avañjhaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ, yasmā ca aitadaivaṃ, tasmā saddhañ‍ca ‘‘atthi kammaṃ atthi kammavipākau’’tiādinayappavattaṃ kammaphalasaddhañ‍ca, saddhūpanibandhattā sīlassa tadupanissayaṃ catupārisuddhisīlañ‍ca, ‘‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākhātau dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’’ti aivaṃ pavattaratanattayappasādañ‍ca, vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya pariñ‍ñādivasaina catusac‍cadhammadassanañ‍ca maidhāvī dhammaujapañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu buddhānaṃ sāsanaṃ auvādaṃ anusiṭṭhiṃ anussarantau ādittampi attanau sīsaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā anuyuñ‍jaitha, tattha anuyaugaṃ ātappaṃ karaiyyāti atthau|

dhammapālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. brahmālitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

kassindriyāni samathaṅgatānīti āyasmatau brahmālitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinantau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau vanditvā pādaphalaṃ adāsi| satthā anumaudanaṃ vatvā pak‍kāmi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā brahmālīti laddhanāmau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau haitusampattiyā caudiyamānau saṃsārai sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau tādisaina kalyāṇamittasan‍nissayaina buddhasāsanai pabbajitvā patirūpakammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā arañ‍ñai viharantau ñāṇassa paripākagatattā nacirassaiva vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.51.63-67) –

‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, pādaphalaṃ adāsahaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau  pana hutvā maggasukhaina phalasukhaina vītināmaintau aikadivasaṃ padhānapariggāhakaina thairaina tasmiṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai bhikkhū uddissa vuttaṃ padhānānuyaugaṃ pariggaṇhantau –

205.

 ‘‘kassindriyāni  samathaṅgatāni, assā yathā sārathinā sudantā|

pahīnamānassa anāsavassa, daivāpi kassa pihayanti tādinau||

206.

 ‘‘mayhindriyāni samathaṅgatāni, assā yathā sārathinā sudantā|

pahīnamānassa anāsavassa, daivāpi mayhaṃ pihayanti tādinau’’ti|| –

gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tassatthau – imasmiṃ arañ‍ñāyatanai vasantaisu bhikkhūsu kassa bhikkhunau thairassa vā navassa vā majjhimassa vā chaikaina sārathinā sudantā assā viya manacchaṭṭhāni indriyāni samathaṃ dantabhāvaṃ nibbisaivanabhāvaṃ gatāni| kassa navavidhampi mānaṃ pahāya ṭhitattā pahīnamānassa catun‍nampi āsavānaṃ abhāvaina anāsavassa iṭṭhādīsu tādilakkhaṇappattiyā tādinau daivāpi pihayanti manussāpi sammāpaṭipattidassanādinā ca ādaraina patthaintīti|

tattha ca gāthāyaṃ purimaḍḍhaina anāgāmimaggādhigamau puṭṭhau, anāgāminaupi hi indriyāni pahīnakāmarāgabyāpādatāya samathaṃ nibbisaivanataṃ gatāni haunti| itaraina arahattamaggapaṭilābhau, arahā hi ‘‘pahīnamānau anāsavau tādī’’ti ca vuc‍cati|

athāyasmā brahmāli padhānapariggāhakaina vuttaṃ ‘‘kassindriyānī’’ti gāthaṃ pac‍canubhāsi| tadatthaṃ attūpanāyikavasaina vissaj‍jaintau ‘‘mayhindriyānī’’tiādikāya dutiyagāthāya añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi, tattha mayhindriyānīti mama cakkhādīni indriyāni| saisaṃ vuttanayamaiva|

brahmālitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. maugharājatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

chavipāpaka  cittabhaddakāti āyasmatau maugharājatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthu  santikai dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ lūkhacīvaradharānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ ākaṅkhantau paṇidhānaṃ katvā tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau atthadassissa bhagavatau kālai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā brāhmaṇānaṃ vij‍jāsippaisu nipphattiṃ gatau brāhmaṇamāṇavai vij‍jāsippāni sikkhāpaintau aikadivasaṃ atthadassiṃ bhagavantaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaparivutaṃ gacchantaṃ  disvā pasan‍namānasau pañ‍capatiṭṭhitaina vanditvā sirasi añ‍jaliṃ katvā ‘‘yāvatā rūpinau sattā’’tiādinā chahi gāthāhi abhitthavitvā bhājanaṃ pūraitvā madhuṃ upanāmaisi| satthā madhuṃ paṭiggahaitvā anumaudanaṃ akāsi|

sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau kassapassa bhagavatau kālai kaṭṭhavāhanassa nāma rañ‍ñau amac‍cau hutvā taina satthu ānayanatthaṃ purisasahassaina paisitau satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vīsativassasahassāni samaṇadhammaṃ katvā tatau cutau aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ sugatīsuyaiva parivattaintau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā maugharājāti laddhanāmau bāvarībrāhmaṇassa santikai uggahitasippau saṃvaigajātau tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā tāpasasahassaparivārau ajitādīhi saddhiṃ satthu santikaṃ paisitau taisaṃ pan‍narasamau hutvā pañhai pucchitvā pañhavissaj‍janapariyausānai arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.4.64-83) –

‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

bhikkhusaṅghaparibyūḷhau, rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

‘‘sissaihi samparivutau, gharamhā abhinikkhamiṃ|

nikkhamitvānahaṃ tattha, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘abhivādiya sambuddhaṃ, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘yāvatā  rūpinau sattā, arūpī vā asañ‍ñinau|

sabbai tai tava ñāṇamhi, antau haunti samaugadhā||

‘‘sukhumacchikajālaina, udakaṃ yau parikkhipai|

yai kaici udakai pāṇā, antaujālai bhavanti tai||

‘‘yaisañ‍ca caitanā atthi, rūpinau ca arūpinau|

sabbai tai tava ñāṇamhi, antau haunti samaugadhā||

‘‘samuddharasimaṃ laukaṃ, andhakārasamākulaṃ|

tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, kaṅkhāsautaṃ taranti tai||

‘‘avij‍jānivutai laukai, andhakāraina autthaṭai|

tava ñāṇamhi jautantai, andhakārā padhaṃsitā||

‘‘tuvaṃ cakkhūsi sabbaisaṃ, mahātamapanūdanau|

tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, nibbāyati bahuj‍janau||

‘‘puṭakaṃ  pūrayitvāna, madhukhuddamanaiḷakaṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, upanaisiṃ mahaisinau||

‘‘paṭiggaṇhi mahāvīrau, sahatthaina mahā isi|

bhuñ‍jitvā tañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñū, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami||

‘‘antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, atthadassī narāsabhau|

mama cittaṃ pasādaintau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘yainidaṃ thavitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhau ca thaumitau|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati||

‘‘catuddasañ‍ca khattuṃ sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ aṭṭhasataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

‘‘pañ‍caiva satakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ asaṅkhaiyyaṃ, mahiyā kārayissati||

‘‘ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissati||

‘‘gambhīraṃ  nipuṇaṃ atthaṃ, ñāṇaina vicinissati|

maugharājāti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘tīhi vij‍jāhi sampan‍naṃ, katakic‍camanāsavaṃ|

gautamau satthavāhaggau, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘hitvā mānusakaṃ yaugaṃ, chaitvāna bhavabandhanaṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā satthalūkhaṃ suttalūkhaṃ rajanalūkhanti visaisaina tividhainapi lūkhaina saman‍nāgataṃ paṃsukūlaṃ dhāraisi| taina naṃ satthā lūkhacīvaradharānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| aparabhāgai purimakammappac‍cayā parihārassa akaraṇatau thairassa sarīrai daddupīḷakādīni uppaj‍jitvā vaḍḍhiṃsu| sau ‘‘saināsanaṃ dussatī’’ti haimantaipi magadhakkhaittaisu palālasanthārāni attharitvā saiti| taṃ aikadivasaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ upasaṅkamitvā vanditvā aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ satthā paṭisanthāravasaina ‘‘chavipāpakā’’tiādinā  paṭhamagāthāya pucchi|

207. tattha chavipāpakāti daddukacchupīḷakāhi bhin‍nacchavibhāvatau hīnacchavika duṭṭhacchavika| cittabhaddakāti anavasaisakilaisappahānaina brahmavihārasaivanāya ca bhaddacitta sundaracitta| maugharājāti tassa ālapanaṃ| satataṃ samāhitauti aggaphalasamādhinā nic‍cakālaṃ abhiṇhaṃ samāhitamānasau| haimantikasītakālarattiyauti haimantasamayai sītakālarattiyau| ac‍cantasaṃyaugai caitaṃ upayaugavacanaṃ| ‘‘haimantikā sītakālarattiyau’’tipi pāḷi| tattha haimantikāti haimantaugadhā haimantapariyāpan‍nāti atthau| bhikkhu tvaṃ sīti bhikkhu kau tvaṃ asi, aivaṃbhūtau paraisu tava saināsanaṃ katvā adaintaisu saṅghikañ‍ca saināsanaṃ apavisantau| kathaṃ karissasīti yathāvuttai sītakālai kathaṃ attabhāvaṃ pavattaisīti satthā pucchi| aivaṃ pana puṭṭhau thairau satthu tamatthaṃ kathaintau –

208.

 ‘‘sampan‍nasassā  magadhā, kaivalā iti mai sutaṃ|

palālacchan‍nakau saiyyaṃ, yathañ‍ñai sukhajīvinau’’ti|| – gāthamāha|

tattha sampan‍nasassāti nipphan‍nasassā| magadhāti magadharaṭṭhaṃ vadati| magadhā nāma jānapadinau rājakumārā, taisaṃ nivāsau aikaupi janapadau ruḷhīvasaina ‘‘magadhā’’tvaiva bahuvacanaina vuc‍cati| kaivalāti anavasaisā| iti mai sutanti aivaṃ mayā sutaṃ| tattha yau adiṭṭhau padaisau, tassa vasaina sutanti vuttaṃ| taina aidisai kālai magadhaisu yattha katthaci mayā vasituṃ sak‍kāti dassaiti| palālacchan‍nakau saiyyaṃ, yathañ‍ñai sukhajīvinauti yathā añ‍ñai sukhajīvinau bhikkhū saināsanasappāyaṃ laddhā sundaraihi attharaṇapāvuraṇaihi sukhaina sayanti, aivaṃ ahampi palālasanthāramaiva haiṭṭhā santharitvā upari tiriyañ‍ca palālacchadanainaiva chāditasarīratāya palālacchan‍nakau saiyyaṃ sayiṃ, saiyyaṃ kappaisinti attanau yathālābhasantausaṃ vibhāvaiti|

maugharājatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. visākhapañ‍cālaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

na  ukkhipai nau ca parikkhipai paraiti āyasmatau visākhassa pañ‍cālaputtassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau itau cuddasai kappai pac‍cantagāmai daliddakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ phalapariyaisanaṃ carantaihi tasmiṃ gāmai manussaihi saddhiṃ arañ‍ñaṃ gatau tattha aikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau val‍liphalaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ  buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai maṇḍalikarājakulai nibbattitvā visākhauti laddhanāmau pañ‍cālarājadhītuyā puttabhāvatau pacchā pañ‍cālaputtauti pañ‍ñāyittha| sau pitari matai raj‍jaṃ kāraintau satthari attanau gāmasamīpagatai satthu santikaṃ gantvā  dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā satthārā saddhiṃ sāvatthiṃ gatau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.39.31-36) –

‘‘sabbai janā samāgamma, agamiṃsu vanaṃ tadā|

phalamanvaisamānā tai, alabhiṃsu phalaṃ tadā||

‘‘tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, val‍liphalamadāsahaṃ||

‘‘catuddasai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā thairau ñātīnaṃ anukampāya jātibhūmiṃ agamāsi| tattha manussā thairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā kālaina kālaṃ dhammaṃ suṇantā aikadivasaṃ ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau dhammakathikau hautī’’ti dhammakathikalakkhaṇaṃ pucchiṃsu| thairau taisaṃ dhammakathikalakkhaṇaṃ kathaintau –

209.

 ‘‘na ukkhipai nau ca parikkhipai parai, na aukkhipai pāragataṃ na airayai|

na cattavaṇṇaṃ parisāsu byāharai, anuddhatau sammitabhāṇi subbatau||

210.

 ‘‘susukhumanipuṇatthadassinā, matikusalaina nivātavuttinā|

saṃsaivitabuddhasīlinā, nibbānaṃ na hi taina dul‍labha’’nti|| –

gāthādvayaṃ abhāsi|

tattha na ukkhipaiti attānaṃ na ukkhipaiyya, jātiādīhi bāhusac‍cādīhi ca attuk‍kaṃsanaṃ na karaiyya| nau ca parikkhipai paraiti parai parapuggalai taihaiva jātiādīhi nau parikkhipai paricchinditvā na  khipaiyya guṇaparidhaṃsanavasaina  vā na khipaiyya| na aukkhipai parai ic‍caivaṃ sambandhau| parai aujjhāpanavasaina na aukkhipai haiṭṭhatau katvā parai na aulaukāpaiyya, na aujjhāpaiyyāti atthau| ‘‘na ukkhipai’’ti kaici paṭhanti, sau aivatthau| pāragatanti  saṃsārapāraṃ viya vij‍jāya pāraṃ gataṃ khīṇāsavaṃ taivij‍jaṃ chaḷabhiñ‍ñaṃ vā na airayai na ghaṭṭayai na āsādaiyya| na cattavaṇṇaṃ parisāsu byāharaiti attanau vaṇṇaṃ guṇaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ nikāmayamānau khattiyaparisādīsu na bhāsaiyya| anuddhatauti uddhac‍carahitau| uddhatassa hi vacanaṃ nādiyanti| sammitabhāṇīti sammadaiva mitabhāṇī, kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasañhitamaiva vācaṃ bhāsanasīlauti atthau| itau añ‍ñathā vadantassa vacanaṃ agahaṇīyaṃ hauti| subbatauti sundaravatau sīlasampan‍nau| ‘‘siyā’’ti kiriyāpadaṃ ānaitvā yaujaitabbaṃ|

aivaṃ thairau saṅkhaipainaiva dhammakathikalakkhaṇaṃ vatvā taisaṃ guṇānaṃ attani labbhamānataṃ adhimuc‍citvā bhiyyausaumattāya abhippasan‍naṃ mahājanaṃ ñatvā ‘‘aivaṃvidhassa dhammakathikassa vimuttāyatanasan‍nissitassa na nibbānaṃ dul‍labhaṃ, atha khau sulabhamaivā’’ti dassaintau ‘‘susukhumanipuṇatthadassinā’’ti dutiyagāthamāha| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

visākhapañ‍cālaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. cūḷakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nadanti maurā susikhā supaikhuṇāti āyasmatau cūḷakatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ kusalaṃ upacinantau itau aikatiṃsai kappai sikhissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau chattapaṇṇiphalaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā cūḷakauti laddhanāmau vayappattau dhanapāladamanai satthari  laddhappasādau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau indasālaguhāyaṃ vasati, sau aikadivasaṃ guhādvārai nisin‍nau magadhakkhaittaṃ aulaukaisi| tasmiṃ khaṇai pāvusakālamaighau gambhīramadhuranigghausau satapaṭalasahassapaṭalau añ‍jana  sikharasan‍nikāsau nabhaṃ pūraitvā pavassati, mayūrasaṅghā ca maighagaj‍jitaṃ sutvā haṭṭhatuṭṭhā kaikāsaddaṃ muñ‍citvā tattha tattha padaisai nac‍cantā vicaranti| thairassapi āvāsagabbhai maighavātaphassaihi apagatadhammattā passaddhakarajakāyai kal‍lataṃ pattai utusappāyalābhaina cittaṃ aikaggaṃ ahausi, kammaṭṭhānavīthiṃ autari, sau taṃ ñatvā kālasampadādikittanamukhaina attānaṃ bhāvanāya ussāhaintau –

211.

 ‘‘nadanti  maurā susikhā supaikhuṇā, sunīlagīvā sumukhā sugaj‍jinau|

susaddalā cāpi mahāmahī ayaṃ, subyāpitambu suvalāhakaṃ nabhaṃ||

212.

 ‘‘sukal‍larūpau sumanassa jhāyataṃ, sunik‍kamau sādhu subuddhasāsanai|

susuk‍kasuk‍kaṃ nipuṇaṃ sududdasaṃ, phusāhi taṃ uttamamac‍cutaṃ pada’’nti|| –

dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha nadanti maurā susikhā supaikhuṇā, sunīlagīvā sumukhā sugaj‍jinauti aitai matthakai uṭṭhitāhi sundarāhi sikhāhi saman‍nāgatattā susikhā, nānāvaṇṇaihi anaikaihi saubhanaihi bhaddakapiñchaihi saman‍nāgatattā supaikhuṇā, rājīvavaṇṇasaṅkāsāya sundarāya nīlavaṇṇāya gīvāya saman‍nāgatattā sunīlagīvā, sundaramukhatāya sumukhā, manuñ‍ñavāditāya sugaj‍jinau, maurā sikhaṇḍinau chaj‍jasaṃvādī kaikāsaddaṃ muñ‍cantā  nadanti ravanti| susaddalā cāpi mahāmahī ayanti ayañ‍ca mahāpathavī susaddalā sundaraharitatiṇā| subyāpitambūti abhinavavuṭṭhiyā tahaṃ tahaṃ vissandamānasalilatāya suṭṭhu byāpitajalā vitatajalā| ‘‘susuk‍katambū’’tipi pāṭhau, suvisuddhajalāti atthau| suvalāhakaṃ nabhanti idañ‍ca nabhaṃ ākāsaṃ nīluppaladalasan‍nibhaihi samantatau pūraitvā ṭhitaihi sundaraihi valāhakaihi maighaihi suvalāhakaṃ|

sukal‍larūpau  sumanassa jhāyatanti idāni utusappāyalābhaina suṭṭhu kal‍larūpau kammaniyasabhāvau tvaṃ, nīvaraṇaihi anajjhārūḷhacittatāya sundaramanassa yaugāvacarassa yaṃ ārammaṇūpanijjhānavasaina lakkhaṇūpanijjhānavasaina ca jhāyataṃ| sunik‍kamau…pai॰… ac‍cutaṃ padanti aivaṃ jhāyantau ca sādhu subuddhassa sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai sundaranik‍kamau hutvā suparisuddhasīlatāya susuk‍kaṃ, visuddhasabhāvatāya sabbassapi saṃkilaisassa gaucarabhāvānupagamanatau suk‍kaṃ, nipuṇañāṇagaucaratāya nipuṇaṃ, paramagambhīratāya sududdasaṃ, paṇītabhāvaina saiṭṭhabhāvaina ca uttamaṃ, nic‍casabhāvatāya ac‍cutaṃ padaṃ taṃ nibbānaṃ phusāhi attapac‍cakkhakaraṇaina sammāpaṭipattiyā sacchikarauhīti|

aivaṃ thairau attānaṃ auvadantauva utusappāyalābhaina samāhitacittau vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.39.37-42) –

‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ, puṇṇamāyaiva candimaṃ|

jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘kadaliphalaṃ  paggayha, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vanditvāna apak‍kamiṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ  pana patvā thairau attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā pītisaumanassajātau ‘‘nadanti maurā’’tiādinā tāyaiva gāthā pac‍cudāhāsi| tainassa idamaiva añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

cūḷakatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. anūpamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

nandamānāgataṃ cittāti āyasmatau anūpamatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau itau aikatiṃsai kappai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ padumaṃ nāma pac‍caikabuddhaṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ rathiyaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau aṅkaulapupphaihi pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai ibbhakulai nibbattitvā rūpasampattiyā anūpamauti laddhanāmau vayappattau upanissayasampan‍natāya kāmai pahāya pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau arañ‍ñai viharati| tassa cittaṃ bahiddhā rūpādiārammaṇaisu vidhāvati| kammaṭṭhānaṃ parivaṭṭati| thairau vidhāvantaṃ cittaṃ niggaṇhantau –

213.

 ‘‘nandamānāgataṃ  cittaṃ, sūlamāraupamānakaṃ|

taina tainaiva vajasi, yaina sūlaṃ kaliṅgaraṃ||

214.

 ‘‘tāhaṃ cittakaliṃ brūmi, taṃ brūmi cittadubbhakaṃ|

satthā tai dul‍labhau laddhau, mānatthai maṃ niyaujayī’’ti|| –

imāhi dvīhi gāthāhi auvadi|

tattha nandamānāgataṃ cittāti nandamāna abhinandamāna citta abhinandamānaṃ āgataṃ uppan‍naṃ| sūlamāraupamānakanti dukkhuppattiṭṭhānatāya sūlasadisattā sūlaṃ taṃ taṃ bhavaṃ kammakilaisaihi aittakaṃ kālaṃ āraupiyamānaṃ| taina tainaiva vajasi, yaina sūlaṃ kaliṅgaranti yattha yattha sūlasaṅkhātā  bhavā kaliṅgarasaṅkhātā adhikuṭṭanakā kāmaguṇā ca taina tainaiva, pāpacitta, vajasi, taṃ tadaiva ṭhānaṃ upagacchasi, attanau anatthaṃ na sal‍lakkhaisi|

tāhaṃ cittakaliṃ brūmīti taṃ tasmā pamattabhāvatau cittakaliṃ cittakālakaṇṇiṃ ahaṃ kathayāmi| punapi taṃ brūmi kathaimi cittadubbhakaṃ cittasaṅkhātassa attanau bahūpakārassa santānassa anatthāvahanatau cittadubbhiṃ| ‘‘cittadubbhagā’’tipi paṭhanti| cittasaṅkhātaalakkhikaappapuñ‍ñāti atthau| kinti brūhīti cai? āha ‘‘satthā tai dul‍labhau laddhau, mānatthai maṃ niyaujayī’’ti| kappānaṃ asaṅkhyaiyyampi nāma buddhasuñ‍ñau laukau hauti, satthari uppan‍naipi manussattasaddhāpaṭilābhādayau dul‍labhā aiva, laddhaisu ca taisu satthāpi dul‍labhauyaiva hauti| aivaṃ dul‍labhau satthā idāni tayā laddhau, tasmiṃ laddhai sampatipi anatthai ahitai āyatiñ‍ca anatthāvahai dukkhāvahai akusalai maṃ mā niyaujaisīti| aivaṃ thairau attanau cittaṃ auvadantau aiva vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.37.16-19) –

‘‘padumau  nāma sambuddhau, cittakūṭai vasī tadā|

disvāna taṃ ahaṃ buddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ||

‘‘aṅkaulaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, aucinitvānahaṃ tadā|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, pūjayiṃ padumaṃ jinaṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

anūpamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. vaj‍jitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

saṃsaraṃ  dīghamaddhānanti āyasmatau vaj‍jitatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau itau pañ‍casaṭṭhimai kappai aikasmiṃ pac‍cantagāmai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau vanacarakau hutvā vicarantau aikadivasaṃ upasantaṃ  nāma pac‍caikabuddhaṃ pabbataguhāyaṃ viharantaṃ addasa| sau tassa upasamaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau campakapupphaina pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai ibbhakulai nibbattau jātadivasatau paṭṭhāya mātugāmahatthaṃ gatau raudati| brahmalaukatau kira cavitvā idhāgatau yasmā mātugāmasamphassaṃ na sahati, tasmā mātugāmasamphassavaj‍janatau vaj‍jitautvaiva nāmaṃ jātaṃ| sau vayappattau satthu yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā tadahaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.37.27-30) –

‘‘upasantau ca sambuddhau, vasatī pabbatantarai|

aikacampakamādāya, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pac‍caikamunimuttamaṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, pūjayiṃ aparājitaṃ||

‘‘pañ‍casaṭṭhimhitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā attanau pubbainivāsaṃ anussaritvā dhammasaṃvaigaina –

215.

 ‘‘saṃsaraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ, gatīsu parivattisaṃ|

apassaṃ ariyasac‍cāni, andhabhūtau puthuj‍janau||

216.

 ‘‘tassa mai appamattassa, saṃsārā vinaḷīkatā|

sabbā gatī samucchin‍nā, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|| – dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  saṃsaranti saṃsarantau, tasmiṃ tasmiṃ bhavai ādānanikkhaipavasaina aparāparaṃ sandhāvantau| dīghamaddhānanti cirakālaṃ anādimati saṃsārai aparimāṇakālaṃ| gatīsūti sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ  vasaina sugatiduggatīsu| parivattisanti ghaṭīyantaṃ viya paribbhamantau cavanupapaj‍janavasaina aparāparaṃ parivattiṃ| tassa pana parivattanassa kāraṇamāha ‘‘apassaṃ ariyasac‍cāni, andhabhūtau puthuj‍janau’’ti| dukkhādīni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni ñāṇacakkhunā apassantau appaṭivijjhantau, tatau aiva avij‍jandhatāya andhabhūtau puthūnaṃ jananādīhi kāraṇaihi puthuj‍janau hauntau gatīsu parivattisanti yaujanā, tainaivāha bhagavā –

‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍cā’’ti (mahāva॰ 287; dī॰ ni॰ 2.155; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1091; naitti॰ 114)|

tassa mayhaṃ vuttanayaina pubbai puthuj‍janassaiva satau idāni satthārā din‍nanayaina appamattassa appamādapaṭipattiyā samathavipassanābhāvanaṃ matthakaṃ pāpaitvā ṭhitassa| saṃsārā vinaḷīkatāti saṃsaranti sattā aitaihīti ‘‘saṃsārā’’ti laddhanāmā kammakilaisā aggamaggaina samucchin‍nattā vigatanaḷā nimmūlā katā| sabbā gatī samucchin‍nāti aivaṃ kammakilaisavaṭṭānaṃ vinaḷīkatattā nirayādikā sabbāpi gatiyau sammadaiva ucchin‍nā viddhaṃsitā, tatau aiva natthi dāni āyatiṃ punabbhavauti idamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausīti|

vaj‍jitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. sandhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

assatthai haritaubhāsaiti āyasmatau sandhitatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau itau aikatiṃsai kappai sikhissa bhagavatau kālai aikau gaupālakau ahausi| sau satthari parinibbutai añ‍ñataraṃ thairaṃ upasaṅkamitvā  tassa santikai buddhaguṇapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammaṃ sutvā pasan‍namānasau ‘‘kuhiṃ bhagavā’’ti pucchitvā parinibbutabhāvaṃ sutvā ‘‘aivaṃ mahānubhāvā buddhāpi nāma anic‍catāvasaṃ gacchanti, ahau saṅkhārā addhuvā’’ti anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhi| taṃ thairau baudhipūjāya ussāhaisi| sau kālaina kālaṃ baudhirukkhasamīpaṃ gantvā  vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā buddhaguṇai anussarantau baudhiṃ vandati| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai ibbhakulai nibbattitvā sandhitauti laddhanāmau vayappattau anic‍catāpaṭisaṃyuttaṃ dhammakathaṃ sutvā saṃvaigajātau pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā ñāṇassa paripākaṃ gatattā nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| sau attanau pubbainivāsaṃ anussarantau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai baudhivandanaṃ buddhānussatiṃ anic‍casañ‍ñāpaṭilābhañ‍ca anussaritvā tadupanissayaina attanau visaisādhigamaṃ pakāsaintau –

217.

 ‘‘assatthai haritaubhāsai, saṃvirūḷhamhi pādapai|

aikaṃ buddhagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhitthaṃ patissatau||

218.

 ‘‘aikatiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

tassā sañ‍ñāya vāhasā, pattau mai āsavakkhayau’’ti|| –

dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha assatthaiti assatthaṭṭhānīyai, yvāyaṃ aitarahi amhākaṃ bhagavatau baudhirukkhau assatthau, aitassa ṭhānai tadā sikhissa bhagavatau baudhirukkhau puṇḍarīkau ṭhitauti sau assatthaṭṭhānīyatāya ‘‘assatthai’’ti vuttaṃ| sattānaṃ assāsajananatau vā| aparai pana ‘‘assattharukkhamūlai nisīditvā tadā buddhānussatiyā bhāvitattā thairau ‘assatthai’ti avaucā’’ti vadanti| haritaubhāsaiti haritaihi sāramaṇivaṇṇaihi aubhāsamānai| saṃvirūḷhamhīti suṭṭhu virūḷhai suppatiṭṭhitai, sughananicitapattapalāsapal‍lavaihi virūḷhasañchan‍naiti ca vadanti| pādapaiti rukkhai| aikaṃ buddhagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhitthaṃ patissatauti buddhārammaṇaṃ  ārammaṇassa aikajātiyattā aikaṃ ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinayappavattaṃ buddhānussatisahagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ buddhaguṇānaṃ patipatisaraṇatau patissatau hutvā alabhiṃ|

kadā pana sā sañ‍ñā laddhā, kīvatāya siddhāti āha ‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai’’tiādi| itau bhaddakappatau uddhaṃ ārauhanavasaina aikatiṃsai kappai| yaṃ sañ‍ñanti yaṃ buddhānussatisahagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, yaṃ vā buddhānaṃ anic‍cataṃ disvā tadanusāraina sabbāsaṅkhāraisu tadā anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ alabhiṃ| tassā sañ‍ñāya vāhasāti tassā yathāvuttāya sañ‍ñāya kāraṇabhāvaina taṃ upanissayaṃ katvā| pattau mai āsavakkhayauti idāni  mayā āsavānaṃ khayau niraudhau adhigatauti imāyaiva ca imassa thairassa apadānagāthāpi| yathāha (apa॰ thaira 1.22.27-30) –

‘‘assatthai haritaubhāsai…pai॰… pattau mai āsavakkhayau||

‘‘itau tairasakappamhi, dhaniṭṭhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

sandhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

pañ‍camavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

niṭṭhitā ca dukanipātavaṇṇanā|

3. tikanipātau

1. aṅgaṇikabhāradvājatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

tikanipātai  ayauni suddhimanvaisanti āyasmatau aṅgaṇikabhāradvājatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau itau aikatiṃsai kappai sikhissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau pañ‍capatiṭṭhitaina vanditvā añ‍jaliṃ paggaṇhi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai himavantasamīpai uk‍kaṭṭhai nāma nagarai vibhavasampan‍nassa brāhmaṇassa gaihai nibbattitvā aṅgaṇikabhāradvājauti laddhanāmau vayappattau vij‍jāsippaisu nipphattiṃ gatau naikkhammajjhāsayatāya paribbājakapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā amaraṃ tapaṃ carantau tattha tattha vicarantau sammāsambuddhaṃ janapadacārikaṃ carantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā taṃ micchātapaṃ pahāya sāsanai pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.23.48-51) –

‘‘usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ, vaissabhuṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhasaiṭṭhamavandahaṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘catuvīsatikappamhi, vikatānandanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā vimuttisukhaina viharantau ñātīnaṃ anukampāya attanau jātibhūmiṃ gantvā bahū ñātakai saraṇaisu ca sīlaisu ca patiṭṭhāpaitvā tatau nivattitvā kururaṭṭhai kuṇḍiyassa  nāma nigamassa avidūrai  arañ‍ñai vasantau kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina uggārāmaṃ gatau uttarāpathatau āgataihi sandiṭṭhaihi brāhmaṇaihi  samāgatau taihi, ‘‘bhau bhāradvāja, kiṃ disvā brāhmaṇānaṃ samayaṃ pahāya imaṃ samayaṃ gaṇhī’’ti pucchitau taisaṃ itau buddhasāsanatau bahiddhā suddhi natthīti dassaintau –

219.

 ‘‘ayauni suddhimanvaisaṃ, aggiṃ paricariṃ vanai|

suddhimaggaṃ ajānantau, akāsiṃ amaraṃ tapa’’nti|| – paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha ayaunīti ayaunisau anupāyaina| suddhinti saṃsārasuddhiṃ bhavanissaraṇaṃ| anvaisanti gavaisantau| aggiṃ paricariṃ vanaiti ‘‘ayaṃ suddhimaggau’’ti adhippāyaina arañ‍ñāyatanai aggihutasālāyaṃ agyāgāraṃ katvā āhutiṃ paggaṇhantau aggidaivaṃ paricariṃ vaidai vuttavidhinā pūjaisiṃ| suddhimaggaṃ ajānantau, akāsiṃ amaraṃ tapanti suddhiyā nibbānassa maggaṃ ajānantau aggiparicaraṇaṃ viya pañ‍catapatappanādiattakilamathānuyaugaṃ ‘‘suddhimaggau’’ti mañ‍ñāya akāsiṃ acariṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ|

aivaṃ thairau assamatau assamaṃ gacchantau viya vaidai vuttavidhinā aggiparicaraṇādinā anuṭṭhāya suddhiyā appattabhāvaina bahiddhā suddhiyā abhāvaṃ dassaitvā idāni imasmiṃyaiva sāsanai suddhi ca mayā adhigatāti dassaintau –

220.

 ‘‘taṃ sukhaina sukhaṃ laddhaṃ, passa dhammasudhammataṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| – dutiyagāthamāha|

tattha tanti yassatthāya suddhiṃ anvaisantau tassa maggaṃ ajānantau aggiṃ paricariṃ amaraṃ tapaṃ acariṃ, taṃ nibbānasukhaṃ sukhaina samathavipassanāya sukhāya paṭipadāya attakilamathānuyaugaṃ anupagamma mayā laddhaṃ pattaṃ adhigataṃ| passa dhammasudhammatanti satthu sāsanadhammassa sudhammataṃ aviparītaniyyānikadhammasabhāvaṃ  passa jānāhīti dhammālapanavasaina vadati, attānaṃ vā ālapati| tassa laddhabhāvaṃ pana dassaintau –

‘‘tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| –

āha, taṃ vuttatthamaiva| aivaṃ suddhiyā adhigatattā ‘‘itau paṭṭhāyāhaṃ paramatthatau brāhmaṇau’’ti dassaintau –

221.

 ‘‘brahmabandhu  purai āsiṃ, idāni khaumhi brāhmaṇau|

taivij‍jau nhātakaucamhi, sauttiyau camhi vaidagū’’ti|| – tatiyaṃ gāthamāha|

tassatthau – itau pubbai jātimattaina brāhmaṇabhāvatau brāhmaṇānaṃ samañ‍ñāya brahmabandhu nāma āsiṃ| bāhitapāpattā pana idāni khau arahattādhigamaina paramatthatau brāhmaṇau ca amhi| itau pubbai bhavasañ‍cayakarānaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidasaṅkhātānaṃ vij‍jānaṃ ajjhayanaina samañ‍ñāmattaina taivij‍jau nāma hutvā idāni bhavakkhayakarāya vij‍jāya vasaina tissan‍naṃ vij‍jānaṃ adhigatattā paramatthatau taivij‍jau ca amhi| tathā itau pubbai bhavassādagadhitāya nhātakavatanipphattiyā samañ‍ñāmattaina nhātakau nāma hutvā idāni aṭṭhaṅgikamaggajalaina suvikkhālitakilaisamalatāya paramatthatau nhātakau camhi| itau pubbai avimuttabhavassādamantajjhānaina vauhāramattatau sauttiyau nāma hutvā idāni suvimuttabhavassādadhammajjhānaina paramatthatau sauttiyau camhi| itau pubbai appaṭinissaṭṭhapāpadhammānaṃ vaidānaṃ gatamattaina vaidagū nāma hutvā idāni vaidasaṅkhātaina maggañāṇaina saṃsāramahaughassa vaidassa catusac‍cassa ca pāraṃ gatattā adhigatattā ñātattā paramatthatau vaidagū jātauti| taṃ sutvā brāhmaṇā sāsanai uḷāraṃ pasādaṃ pavaidaisuṃ|

aṅgaṇikabhāradvājatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

2. pac‍cayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

pañ‍cāhāhaṃ  pabbajitauti āyasmatau pac‍cayatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau itau aikanavutai kappai vipassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ vinatāya nāma nadiyā tīrai gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau manuñ‍ñadassanāni mahantāni udumbaraphalāni aucinitvā upanāmaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau imasmiṃ bhaddakappai kassapai bhagavati laukai uppaj‍jitvā pavattavaradhammacak‍kai vainaiyyajanānuggahaṃ karauntai tassa sāsanai pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā bhāvanamanuyuñ‍jantau aikadivasaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ cintaitvā

ativiya  sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau vihārai nisin‍nau ‘‘arahattaṃ appatvā itau na nikkhamissāmī’’ti  cittaṃ adhiṭṭhāya vāyamantau ñāṇassa aparipak‍kattā vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaituṃ nāsakkhi| sau kālaṅkatvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rauhitanagarai khattiyakulai nibbattitvā pac‍cayauti laddhanāmau vayappattau pitu ac‍cayaina raj‍jai patiṭṭhitau aikadivasaṃ mahārājabaliṃ kātuṃ ārabhi| tattha mahājanau san‍nipati| tasmiṃ samāgamai tassa pasādañ‍jananatthaṃ satthā mahājanassa paikkhantassaiva ākāsai vaissavaṇaina nimmitai ratanamayakūṭāgārai ratanamayasīhāsanai nisīditvā dhammaṃ daisaisi| mahatau janakāyassa dhammābhisamayau ahausi| taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā pac‍cayarājāpi raj‍jaṃ pahāya purimahaitusañ‍cauditau pabbaji| sau yathā kassapassa bhagavatau kālai paṭiñ‍ñaṃ akāsi, aivaṃ paṭiñ‍ñaṃ katvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaintau ñāṇassa paripākaṃ gatattā tāvadaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.39.15-20) –

‘‘vinatānadiyā tīrai, vihāsi purisuttamau|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

‘‘tasmiṃ pasan‍namānasau, kilaisamaladhauvanai|

udumbaraphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘imamhi bhaddakai kappai, saṃviggamānamānasau|

kassapassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘tathā pabbajitau santau, bhāvanaṃ anuyuñ‍jisaṃ|

na vihārā nikkhamissaṃ, iti katvāna mānasaṃ||

‘‘uttamatthaṃ asampattau, na ca pattaumhi tāvadai|

idāni pana ñāṇassa, paripākaina nibbutau|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, phusitvā ac‍cutaṃ padaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattikittanamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

222.

 ‘‘pañ‍cāhāhaṃ  pabbajitau, saikhau appattamānasau|

vihāraṃ mai paviṭṭhassa, caitasau paṇidhī ahu||

223.

 ‘‘nāsissaṃ na pivissāmi, vihāratau na nikkhamai|

napi passaṃ nipātaissaṃ, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhatai||

224.

 ‘‘tassa maivaṃ viharatau, passa vīriyaparak‍kamaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti|| –

imā tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha pañ‍cāhāhaṃ pabbajitauti pañ‍cāhau ahaṃ, pabbajitau hutvā pañ‍cāhau, pabbajitadivasatau pañ‍camau ahau niṭṭhitauti atthau| saikhau appattamānasauti adhisīlasikkhādīnaṃ sikkhanatau saikhau| anavasaisatau mānaṃ siyati samucchindatīti mānasau, aggamaggau, taṃnibbattitau mānasatau āgataṃ mānasaṃ, arahattaṃ, taṃ, sau vā appattau  aitaināti appattamānasau| vihāraṃ mai paviṭṭhassa, caitasau paṇidhī ahūti aivaṃ saikhassa mai vasanakavihāraṃ auvarakaṃ paviṭṭhassa satau aivarūpau idāni vuc‍camānākārau caitaupaṇidhi ahausi, aivaṃ mayā cittaṃ paṇihitanti atthau|

nāsissantiādinā  cittapaṇidhiṃ dassaiti| tattha nāsissanti yaṃkiñ‍ci bhaujanaṃ na bhuñ‍jissaṃ na bhuñ‍jissāmi taṇhāsal‍lai mama hadayagatai anūhatai anuddhataiti aivaṃ sabbapadaisu yaujaitabbaṃ| na pivissāmīti yaṃkiñ‍ci pātabbaṃ na pivissāmi| vihāratau na nikkhamaiti imasmā idāni mayā nisin‍nagabbhatau na nikkhamaiyyaṃ| napi passaṃ nipātaissanti mama sarīrassa dvīsu passaisu aikampi passaṃ kāyakilamathavinaudanatthaṃ na nipātaissaṃ, aikapassainapi na nipaj‍jissāmīti atthau|

tassa maivaṃ viharatauti tassa mai aivaṃ cittaṃ paṇidhāya daḷhavīriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ katvā vipassanānuyaugavasaina viharatau| passa vīriyaparak‍kamanti vidhinā īrayitabbatau ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’ paraṃ ṭhānaṃ ak‍kamanatau ‘‘parak‍kamau’’ti ca laddhanāmaṃ ussauḷhībhūtaṃ vāyāmaṃ passa jānāhi| yassa panānubhāvaina mayā tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti vuttatthamaiva|

pac‍cayatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

3. bākulatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yau  pubbai karaṇīyānīti āyasmatau bākulatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi kira atītai itau kappasatasahassādhikassa asaṅkhyaiyyassa matthakai anaumadassissa bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vayappattau tayau vaidai uggahaitvā tattha sāraṃ apassantau ‘‘samparāyikatthaṃ gavaisissāmī’’ti isipabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā pabbatapādai viharantau pañ‍cābhiñ‍ñāaṭṭhasamāpattilābhī hutvā viharantau buddhuppādaṃ sutvā satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā saraṇaisu  patiṭṭhitau satthu udarābādhai uppan‍nai arañ‍ñatau bhaisaj‍jāni āharitvā taṃ vūpasamaitvā tattha puñ‍ñaṃ āraugyatthāya pariṇāmaitvā tatau cutau brahmalaukai nibbattitvā aikaṃ asaṅkhyaiyyaṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau padumuttarabuddhakālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagaihai nibbattau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ appābādhānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā sayaṃ taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ ākaṅkhantau paṇidhānaṃ katvā yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ upacinitvā sugatīsuyaiva saṃsarantau vipassissa bhagavatau nibbattitau puraitaramaiva bandhumatīnagarai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattau purimanayainaiva isipabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā jhānābhiñ‍ñālābhī hutvā pabbatapādai vasantau buddhuppādaṃ sutvā satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā saraṇaisu  patiṭṭhitau bhikkhūnaṃ tiṇapupphakaraugai uppan‍nai taṃ vūpasamaitvā tattha yāvatāyukaṃ ṭhatvā tatau cutau brahmalaukai nibbattitvā aikanavutikappai daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau kassapassa bhagavatau kālai bārāṇasiyaṃ kulagaihai nibbattitvā gharāvāsaṃ vasantau aikaṃ jiṇṇaṃ vinassamānaṃ mahāvihāraṃ disvā tattha upausathāgārādikaṃ sabbaṃ āvasathaṃ kāraitvā tattha bhikkhusaṅghassa sabbaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ paṭiyādaitvā yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau amhākaṃ bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva kausambiyaṃ saiṭṭhigaihai nibbatti| sau araugabhāvāya mahāyamunāya nhāpiyamānau dhātiyā hatthatau macchaina gilitau macchai kaivaṭṭahatthagatai bārāṇasisaiṭṭhibhariyāya vik‍kiṇitvā gahitai phāliyamānaipi puñ‍ñabalaina araugauyaiva hutvā tāya puttauti gahaitvā pausiyamānau taṃ pavattiṃ sutvā janakaihi mātāpitūhi ‘‘ayaṃ amhākaṃ puttau, daitha nau putta’’nti anuyaugai katai rañ‍ñā ‘‘ubhayaisampi sādhāraṇau hautū’’ti dvin‍naṃ kulānaṃ dāyādabhāvaina vinicchayaṃ katvā ṭhapitattā bākulauti laddhanāmau vayappattau hutvā mahatiṃ sampattiṃ anubhavantau āsītikau hutvā satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā sattāhamaiva puthuj‍janau ahausi, aṭṭhamai aruṇai saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.40.386-411) –

‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, saubhitau nāma pabbatau|

assamau sukatau mayhaṃ, sakasissaihi māpitau||

‘‘maṇḍapā ca bahū tattha, pupphitā sindhuvārakā|

kapitthā ca bahū tattha, pupphitā jīvajīvakā||

‘‘nigguṇḍiyau bahū tattha, badarāmalakāni ca|

phārusakā alābū ca, puṇḍarīkā ca pupphitā||

‘‘āḷakā bailuvā tattha, kadalī mātuluṅgakā|

mahānāmā bahū tattha, aj‍junā ca piyaṅgukā||

‘‘kausambā saḷalā nimbā, nigraudhā ca kapitthanā|

aidisau assamau mayhaṃ, sasissauhaṃ tahiṃ vasiṃ||

‘‘anaumadassī bhagavā, sayambhū laukanāyakau|

gavaisaṃ paṭisal‍lānaṃ, mamassamamupāgami||

‘‘upaitamhi mahāvīrai, anaumadassimahāyasai|

khaṇaina laukanāthassa, vātābādhau samuṭṭhahi||

‘‘vicarantau arañ‍ñamhi, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, cakkhumantaṃ mahāyasaṃ||

‘‘iriyañ‍cāpi disvāna, upalakkhaisahaṃ tadā|

asaṃsayañhi buddhassa, byādhi nau udapaj‍jatha||

‘‘khippaṃ assamamāgañchiṃ, mama sissāna santikai|

bhaisaj‍jaṃ kattukāmauhaṃ, sissai āmantayiṃ tadā||

‘‘paṭissuṇitvāna mai vākyaṃ, sissā sabbai sagāravā|

aikajjhaṃ san‍nipatiṃsu, satthugāravatā mama||

‘‘khippaṃ pabbatamāruyha, sabbausadhamahāsahaṃ|

pānīyayaugaṃ katvāna, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

‘‘paribhuttai mahāvīrai, sabbañ‍ñulaukanāyakai|

khippaṃ vātau vūpasami, sugatassa mahaisinau||

‘‘passaddhaṃ  darathaṃ disvā, anaumadassī mahāyasau|

sakāsanai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

‘‘yau mai pādāsi bhaisaj‍jaṃ, byādhiñ‍ca samayī mama|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘kappasatasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

vāditai tūriyai tattha, maudissati sadā ayaṃ||

‘‘manussalaukamāgantvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsakappamhi, anaumau nāma khattiyau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambumaṇḍassa issarau||

‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau|

tāvatiṃsaipi khaubhaitvā, issaraṃ kārayissati||

‘‘daivabhūtau manussau vā, appābādhau bhavissati|

pariggahaṃ vivaj‍jaitvā, byādhiṃ laukai tarissati||

‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

‘‘kilaisai jhāpayitvāna, taṇhāsautaṃ tarissati|

bākulau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘idaṃ sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘anaumadassī bhagavā, sayambhū laukanāyakau|

vivaikānuvilaukaintau, mamassamamupāgami||

‘‘upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ, sabbañ‍ñuṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

sabbausadhaina tappaisiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

‘‘tassa  mai sukataṃ kammaṃ, sukhaittai bījasampadā|

khaipaituṃ naiva sak‍kaumi, tadā hi sukataṃ mama||

‘‘lābhā mama suladdhaṃ mai, yauhaṃ addakkhi nāyakaṃ|

taina kammāvasaisaina, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

‘‘sabbamaitaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhaisaj‍jassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā aikadivasaṃ satthārā attanau sāvakai paṭipāṭiyā ṭhānantarai ṭhapaintaina appābādhānaṃ  aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapitau sau parinibbānasamayai saṅghamajjhai bhikkhūnaṃ auvādamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

225.

 ‘‘yau pubbai karaṇīyāni, pacchā sau kātumicchati|

sukhā sau dhaṃsatai ṭhānā, pacchā ca manutappati||

226.

 ‘‘yañhi kayirā tañhi vadai, yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vadai|

akarauntaṃ bhāsamānaṃ, parijānanti paṇḍitā||

227.

 ‘‘susukhaṃ vata nibbānaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ, yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti|| – gāthāttayamabhāsi|

tattha  yau pubbai karaṇīyāni, pacchā sau kātumicchatīti yau puggalau pubbai puraitaraṃ jarāraugādīhi anabhibhūtakālaiyaiva kātabbāni attanau hitasukhāvahāni kammāni pamādavasaina akatvā pacchā sau kātabbakālaṃ atik‍kamitvā kātuṃ icchati| sauti ca nipātamattaṃ| tadā pana jarāraugādīhi abhibhūtattā kātuṃ na sak‍kauti, asak‍kauntau ca sukhā sau dhaṃsatai ṭhānā, pacchā ca manutappatīti sau puggalau sukhā  ṭhānā saggatau nibbānatau ca tadupāyassa anuppāditattā parihāyantau ‘‘akataṃ mai kalyāṇa’’ntiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.248; naitti॰ 120) pacchā ca anutappati vippaṭisāraṃ āpaj‍jati| ma-kārau padasandhikarau| ahaṃ pana karaṇīyaṃ katvā aiva tumhai aivaṃ vadāmīti dassaintau ‘‘yañhi kayirā’’ti dutiyaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha parijānantīti ‘‘aittakau aya’’nti paricchij‍ja jānanti na bahuṃ mañ‍ñantīti atthau| sammāpaṭipattivasaina hi yathāvādī tathākārī aiva saubhati, na tatau añ‍ñathā| karaṇīyapariyāyaina sādhāraṇatau vuttamatthaṃ idāni sarūpatau dassaituṃ ‘‘susukhaṃ vatā’’tiādinā tatiyaṃ gāthamāha| tassatthau – sammā sāmaṃ sabbadhammānaṃ buddhattā sammāsambuddhaina bhagavatā daisitaṃ sabbasau saukahaitūnaṃ abhāvatau asaukaṃ vigatarāgādirajattā virajaṃ catūhi yaugaihi anupaddutattā khaimaṃ nibbānaṃ suṭṭhu sukhaṃ vata, kasmā? yattha yasmiṃ nibbānai sakalaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ nirujjhati ac‍cantamaiva vūpasamatīti|

bākulatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

4. dhaniyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā
