sukhañ‍cai  jīvituṃ icchaiti āyasmatau dhaniyatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau sikhissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau naḷamālāya pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai rājagahai kumbhakārakulai nibbattitvā dhaniyauti laddhanāmau vayappattau kumbhakārakammaina jīvati| taina ca samayaina satthā dhaniyassa kumbhakārassa sālāyaṃ nisīditvā puk‍kusātissa kulaputtassa chadhātuvibhaṅgasuttaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.342 ādayau) daisaisi| sau taṃ sutvā katakic‍cau ahausi| dhaniyau tassa parinibbutabhāvaṃ sutvā ‘‘niyyānikaṃ vata buddhasāsanaṃ, yattha aikarattiparicayaināpi vaṭṭadukkhatau muñ‍cituṃ sak‍kā’’ti paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā  kuṭimaṇḍanānuyuttau  viharantau kuṭikaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca bhagavatā garahitau saṅghikai saināsanai vasantau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.48.1-7) –

‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

vipinaggaina gacchantaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

‘‘naḷamālaṃ gahaitvāna, nikkhamantau ca tāvadai|

tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, naḷamālamapūjayiṃ|

dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ mahāvīraṃ, sabbalaukānukampakaṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ mālamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā yai bhikkhū dhutaṅgasamādhānaina attānaṃ uk‍kaṃsaitvā saṅghabhattādiṃ sādiyantai añ‍ñai bhikkhū avajānanti, taisaṃ auvādadānamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauntau –

228.

 ‘‘sukhañ‍cai jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā|

saṅghikaṃ nātimañ‍ñaiyya, cīvaraṃ pānabhaujanaṃ||

229.

 sukhañ‍cai  jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā|

ahi mūsikasaubbhaṃva, saivaitha sayanāsanaṃ||

230.

 sukhañ‍cai jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā|

itarītaraina tussaiyya, aikadhammañ‍ca bhāvayai’’ti|| – tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha sukhañ‍cai jīvituṃ icchai, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavāti sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ samaṇabhāvai apaikkhavā sikkhāya tibbagāravau hutvā sukhaṃ jīvituṃ icchaiyya cai, anaisanaṃ pahāya sāmañ‍ñasukhaina sacai jīvitukāmauti atthau| saṅghikaṃ nātimañ‍ñaiyya, cīvaraṃ pānabhaujananti saṅghatau ābhataṃ cīvaraṃ āhāraṃ na avamañ‍ñaiyya, saṅghassa uppaj‍janakalābhau nāma parisuddhuppādau hautīti taṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa ājīvapārisuddhisambhavaina sāmañ‍ñasukhaṃ hatthagatamaivāti adhippāyau| ahi mūsikasaubbhaṃvāti ahi viya mūsikāya khatabilaṃ saivaitha saivaiyya saināsanaṃ| yathā nāma sappau sayamattanau āsayaṃ akatvā mūsikāya añ‍ñaina vā katai āsayai vasitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamati, aivamaivaṃ bhikkhu sayaṃ saināsanakaraṇā saṃkilaisaṃ anāpaj‍jitvā yattha katthaci vasitvā pak‍kamaiyyāti atthau|

idāni vuttai avuttai ca pac‍cayai yathālābhasantausainaiva sāmañ‍ñasukhaṃ hauti, na añ‍ñathāti dassaintau āha ‘‘itarītaraina tussaiyyā’’ti, yaina kainaci hīnaina vā paṇītaina vā yathāladdhaina pac‍cayaina santausaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti atthau| aikadhammanti appamādabhāvaṃ, tañhi anuyuñ‍jantassa anavaj‍jaṃ sabbaṃ laukiyasukhaṃ laukuttarasukhañ‍ca hatthagatamaiva hauti| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘appamattau hi jhāyantau, pappauti vipulaṃ sukha’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 2.352; dha॰ pa॰ 27)|

dhaniyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

5. mātaṅgaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

atisītanti  āyasmatau mātaṅgaputtatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai himavantasamīpai mahatau jātassarassa haiṭṭhā mahati nāgabhavanai mahānubhāvau nāgarājā hutvā nibbattau aikadivasaṃ nāgabhavanatau nikkhamitvā vicarantau satthāraṃ ākāsaina gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau attanau sīsamaṇinā pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai mātaṅgassa nāma kuṭumbikassa puttau  hutvā nibbattau  mātaṅgaputtautvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| sau viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau alasajātikau hutvā kiñ‍ci kammaṃ akarauntau ñātakaihi añ‍ñaihi ca garahitau ‘‘sukhajīvinau imai samaṇā sakyaputtiyā’’ti sukhajīvitaṃ ākaṅkhantau bhikkhūhi kataparicayau hutvā satthāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā añ‍ñai bhikkhū iddhimantai disvā iddhibalaṃ patthaitvā satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā bhāvanaṃ anuyuñ‍jantau chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.48.8-29) –

‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, gacchatai anilañ‍jasai||

‘‘avidūrai himavantassa, mahājātassarau ahu|

tattha mai bhavanaṃ āsi, puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyutaṃ||

‘‘bhavanā abhinikkhamma, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

‘‘vicinaṃ naddasaṃ pupphaṃ, pūjayissanti nāyakaṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

‘‘mama sīsai maṇiṃ gayha, pūjayiṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

imāya maṇipūjāya, vipākau hautu bhaddakau||

‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

‘‘sau tai ijjhatu saṅkappau, labhassu vipulaṃ sukhaṃ|

imāya maṇipūjāya, anubhauhi mahāyasaṃ||

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna bhagavā, jalajuttamanāmakau|

agamāsi buddhasaiṭṭhau, yattha cittaṃ paṇīhitaṃ||

‘‘saṭṭhikappāni daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

anaikasatakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

‘‘pubbakammaṃ sarantassa, daivabhūtassa mai satau|

maṇi nibbattatai mayhaṃ, ālaukakaraṇau mamaṃ||

‘‘chaḷasītisahassāni, nāriyau mai pariggahā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā||

‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivārainti maṃ nic‍caṃ, maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘sauṇṇamayā  maṇimayā, lauhitaṅkamayā tathā|

bhaṇḍā mai sukatā haunti, yadicchasi piḷandhanā||

‘‘kūṭāgārā gahā rammā, sayanañ‍ca mahārahaṃ|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ||

‘‘lābhā taisaṃ suladdhañ‍ca, yai labhanti upassutiṃ|

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ manussānaṃ, ausadhaṃ sabbapāṇinaṃ||

‘‘mayhampi sukataṃ kammaṃ, yauhaṃ adakkhi nāyakaṃ|

vinipātā pamuttaumhi, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunūpapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

divasañ‍caiva rattiñ‍ca, ālaukau hauti mai sadā||

‘‘tāyaiva maṇipūjāya, anubhautvāna sampadā|

ñāṇālaukau mayā diṭṭhau, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ maṇiṃ abhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā puggalādhiṭṭhānavasaina kausaj‍jaṃ garahantau attanau ca vīriyārambhaṃ kittaintau –

231.

 ‘‘atisītaṃ atiuṇhaṃ, atisāyamidaṃ ahu|

iti vissaṭṭhakammantai, khaṇā ac‍cainti māṇavai||

232.

 ‘‘yau ca sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca, tiṇā bhiyyau na mañ‍ñati|

karaṃ purisakic‍cāni, sau sukhā na vihāyati||

233.

 ‘‘dabbaṃ  kusaṃ pauṭakilaṃ, usīraṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ|

urasā panudissāmi, vivaikamanubrūhaya’’nti|| – gāthāttayamāha|

tattha atisītanti himapātavaddalādinā ativiya sītaṃ, idaṃ ahūti ānaitvā sambandhau| atiuṇhanti dhammaparitāpādinā ativiya uṇhaṃ, ubhayainapi utuvasaina kausaj‍javatthumāha| atisāyanti divasassa pariṇatiyā atisāyaṃ, sāyaggahaṇainaiva caittha pātaupi saṅgayhati, tadubhayaina kālavasaina kausaj‍javatthumāha| itīti iminā pakāraina| aitaina ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā kammaṃ kattabbaṃ hautī’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 8.80; dī॰ ni॰ 3.334) vuttaṃ kausaj‍javatthuṃ  saṅgaṇhāti| vissaṭṭhakammantaiti paric‍cattayaugakammantai| khaṇāti buddhuppādādayau brahmacariyavāsassa aukāsā| ac‍caintīti atik‍kamanti| māṇavaiti sattai| tiṇā bhiyyau na mañ‍ñatīti tiṇatau upari na mañ‍ñati, tiṇaṃ viya mañ‍ñati, sītuṇhāni abhibhavitvā attanā kattabbaṃ karauti| karanti karauntau| purisakic‍cānīti vīrapurisaina kattabbāni attahitaparahitāni| sukhāti sukhatau, nibbānasukhatauti adhippāyau| tatiyagāthāya atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

mātaṅgaputtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

6. khuj‍jasaubhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yai cittakathī bahussutāti āyasmatau khuj‍jasaubhitatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ bhagavantaṃ mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau dasahi gāthāhi abhitthavi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai pāṭaliputtanagarai brāhmaṇakulai nibbatti, ‘‘saubhitau’’tissa nāmaṃ  ahausi| thaukaṃ khuj‍jadhātukatāya pana khuj‍jasaubhitautvaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| sau vayappattau satthari parinibbutai ānandatthairassa santikai pabbajitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.49-58) –

‘‘kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘tamandhakāraṃ nāsaitvā, santāraitvā bahuṃ janaṃ|

ñāṇālaukaina jautantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘vasīsatasahassaihi, nīyantaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

uddharantaṃ bahū sattai, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘āhanantaṃ dhammabhairiṃ, maddantaṃ titthiyai gaṇai|

sīhanādaṃ vinadantaṃ, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘yāvatā brahmalaukatau, āgantvāna sabrahmakā|

pucchanti nipuṇai pañhai, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘yassañ‍jaliṃ karitvāna, āyācanti sadaivakā|

taina puñ‍ñaṃ anubhaunti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘sabbai  janā samāgantvā, sampavārainti cakkhumaṃ|

na vikampati ajjhiṭṭhau, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘nagaraṃ pavisatau yassa, ravanti bhairiyau bahū|

vinadanti gajā mattā, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘vīthiyā gacchatau yassa, sabbābhā jautatai sadā|

abbhun‍natā samā haunti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘byāharantassa buddhassa, cak‍kavāḷampi suyyati|

sabbai sattai viñ‍ñāpaiti, kau disvā na pasīdati||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau  pana hutvā paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai rājagahai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ san‍nipatitaina saṅghaina ‘‘āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaihī’’ti āṇattau pathaviyaṃ nimuj‍jitvā thairassa puratau uṭṭhahitvā saṅghassa sāsanaṃ āraucaitvā sayaṃ puraitaraṃ ākāsaina gantvā sattapaṇṇiguhādvāraṃ sampāpuṇi| taina ca samayaina mārassa mārakāyikānañ‍ca paṭisaidhanatthaṃ daivasaṅghaina paisitā añ‍ñatarā daivatā sattapaṇṇiguhādvārai ṭhitā hauti, tassā khuj‍jasaubhitau thairau attanau āgamanaṃ kathaintau –

234.

 ‘‘yai  cittakathī bahussutā, samaṇā pāṭaliputtavāsinau|

taisañ‍ñatarauyamāyuvā, dvārai tiṭṭhati khuj‍jasaubhitau’’ti|| – paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha cittakathīti vicittadhammakathikā, saṅkhipanaṃ, vitthāraṇaṃ gambhīrakaraṇaṃ uttānīkaraṇaṃ kaṅkhāvinaudanaṃ dhammapatiṭṭhāpananti aivamādīhi nānānayaihi paraisaṃ ajjhāsayānurūpaṃ dhammassa kathanasīlāti atthau| bahussutāti pariyattipaṭivaidhabāhusac‍capāripūriyā bahussutā| sabbasau samitapāpatāya samaṇā| pāṭaliputtavāsinau, taisañ‍ñatarauti pāṭaliputtanagaravāsitāya pāṭaliputtavāsinau, taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau, ayaṃ āyuvā dīghāyu āyasmā| dvārai tiṭṭhatīti sattapaṇṇiguhāya dvārai tiṭṭhati, saṅghassa anumatiyā pavisitunti atthau| taṃ sutvā sā daivatā thairassa āgamanaṃ saṅghassa nivaidaintī –

235.

 ‘‘yai  cittakathī…pai॰… dvārai tiṭṭhati mālutairitau’’ti|| – dutiyaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha mālutairitauti iddhicittajanitaina vāyunā airitau, iddhibalaina āgatauti atthau|

aivaṃ  tāya daivatāya nivaiditaina saṅghaina kataukāsau thairau saṅghassa santikaṃ gacchantau –

236.

 ‘‘suyuddhaina suyiṭṭhaina, saṅgāmavijayaina ca|

brahmacariyānuciṇṇaina, aivāyaṃ sukhamaidhatī’’ti|| –

imāya tatiyagāthāya añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

tattha suyuddhaināti pubbabhāgai tadaṅgavikkhambhanappahānavasaina kilaisaihi suṭṭhu yujjhanaina| suyiṭṭhaināti antarantarā kalyāṇamittaihi din‍nasappāyadhammadānaina| saṅgāmavijayaina cāti samucchaidappahānavasaina sabbasau kilaisābhisaṅkhāranimmathanaina laddhasaṅgāmavijayaina ca| brahmacariyānuciṇṇaināti anuciṇṇaina aggamaggabrahmacariyaina| aivāyaṃ sukhamaidhatīti aivaṃ vuttappakāraina ayaṃ khuj‍jasaubhitau nibbānasukhaṃ phalasamāpattisukhañ‍ca aidhati, anubhavatīti atthau|

khuj‍jasaubhitatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

7. vāraṇatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yaudha kauci manussaisūti āyasmatau vāraṇatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau itau dvānavutai kappai tissassa bhagavatau uppattitau puraitaramaiva  brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā brāhmaṇānaṃ vij‍jāsippaisu pāragū hutvā isipabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitvā catupaṇṇāsasahassānaṃ antaivāsikānaṃ mantai vācaintau vasati| taina ca samayaina tissassa bhagavatau baudhisattabhūtassa tusitā kāyā cavitvā carimabhavai mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamanaina mahāpathavikampau ahausi| taṃ disvā mahājanau bhītau saṃviggau naṃ isiṃ upasaṅkamitvā pathavikampanakāraṇaṃ pucchi| sau ‘‘mahābaudhisattau mātukucchiṃ auk‍kami, taināyaṃ pathavikampau, tasmā mā bhāyathā’’ti buddhuppādassa pubbanimittabhāvaṃ kathaitvā samassāsaisi, buddhārammaṇañ‍ca pītiṃ paṭivaidaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu  saṃsarantau imasmiṃ  buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vāraṇauti laddhanāmau vayappattau añ‍ñatarassa ārañ‍ñakassa thairassa santikai dhammaṃ sutvā laddhappasādau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauti| sau aikadivasaṃ buddhupaṭṭhānaṃ gacchantau antarāmaggai ahinakulai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ kalahaṃ katvā kālaṅkatai disvā ‘‘imai sattā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaviraudhaina jīvitakkhayaṃ pattā’’ti saṃviggamānasau hutvā bhagavatau santikaṃ gatau, tassa bhagavā cittācāraṃ ñatvā tadanurūpamaiva auvādaṃ daintau –

237.

 ‘‘yaudha kauci manussaisu, parapāṇāni hiṃsati|

asmā laukā paramhā ca, ubhayā dhaṃsatai narau||

238.

 ‘‘yau ca maittaina cittaina, sabbapāṇānukampati|

bahuñhi sau pasavati, puñ‍ñaṃ tādisakau narau||

239.

 ‘‘subhāsitassa sikkhaitha, samaṇūpāsanassa ca|

aikāsanassa ca rahau, cittavūpasamassā cā’’ti|| – tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha yaudha kauci manussaisūti idha manussaisu yau kauci khattiyau vā brāhmaṇau vā vaissau vā suddau vā gahaṭṭhau vā pabbajitau vā| manussaggahaṇañ‍caittha uk‍kaṭṭhasattanidassananti daṭṭhabbaṃ| parapāṇāni hiṃsatīti parasattai māraiti vibādhati ca| asmā laukāti idha laukatau| paramhāti paralaukatau| ubhayā dhaṃsataiti ubhayatau dhaṃsati, ubhayalaukapariyāpan‍nahitasukhatau parihāyatīti atthau| narauti sattau|

aivaṃ parapīḷālakkhaṇaṃ pāpadhammaṃ dassaitvā idāni parapīḷānivattilakkhaṇaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ dassaintau ‘‘yau ca maittainā’’tiādinā dutiyaṃ gāthamāha| tattha maittaina cittaināti maittāsampayuttaina cittaina appanāpattaina itarītaraina vā| sabbapāṇānukampatīti sabbai pāṇai attanau aurasaputtai viya maittāyati| bahuñhi sau pasavati, puñ‍ñaṃ tādisakau narauti  sau tathārūpau maittāvihārī puggalau bahuṃ mahantaṃ anappakaṃ kusalaṃ pasavati paṭilabhati adhigacchati|

idāni  taṃ sasambhārai samathavipassanādhammai niyaujaintau ‘‘subhāsitassā’’tiādinā tatiyaṃ gāthamāha| tattha subhāsitassa sikkhaithāti appicchakathādibhaidaṃ subhāsitaṃ pariyattidhammaṃ savanadhāraṇaparipucchādivasaina sikkhaiyya| samaṇūpāsanassa cāti samitapāpānaṃ samaṇānaṃ kalyāṇamittānaṃ upāsakānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā payirupāsanañ‍caiva paṭipattiyā taisaṃ samīpacariyañ‍ca  sikkhaiyya| aikāsanassa ca rahau cittavūpasamassa cāti aikassa asahāyassa kāyavivaikaṃ anubrūhantassa rahau kammaṭṭhānānuyaugavasaina āsanaṃ nisaj‍jaṃ sikkhaiyya| aivaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuñ‍jantau bhāvanañ‍ca matthakaṃ pāpaintau samucchaidavasaina kilaisānaṃ cittassa vūpasamañ‍ca sikkhaiyya| yāhi adhisīlasikkhādīhi kilaisā ac‍cantamaiva vūpasantā pahīnā haunti, tā maggaphalasikkhā sikkhantassa ac‍cantamaiva cittaṃ vūpasantaṃ nāma hautīti| gāthāpariyausānai vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.59-72) –

‘‘ajjhaugāhaitvā himavaṃ, mantai vācaimahaṃ tadā|

catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ||

‘‘adhitā vaidagū sabbai, chaḷaṅgai pāramiṃ gatā|

sakavij‍jāhupatthaddhā, himavantai vasanti tai||

‘‘cavitvā tusitā kāyā, daivaputtau mahāyasau|

uppaj‍ji mātukucchismiṃ, sampajānau patissatau||

‘‘sambuddhai upapaj‍jantai, dasasahassi kampatha|

andhā cakkhuṃ alabhiṃsu, uppaj‍jantamhi nāyakai||

‘‘sabbākāraṃ pakampittha, kaivalā vasudhā ayaṃ|

nigghausasaddaṃ sutvāna, ubbij‍jiṃsu mahājanā||

‘‘sabbai janā samāgamma, āgacchuṃ mama santikaṃ|

vasudhāyaṃ pakampittha, kiṃ vipākau bhavissati||

‘‘avacāsiṃ  tadā taisaṃ, mā bhaitha natthi vau bhayaṃ|

visaṭṭhā hautha sabbaipi, uppādauyaṃ suvatthikau||

‘‘aṭṭhahaitūhi samphussa, vasudhāyaṃ pakampati|

tathā nimittā dissanti, aubhāsau vipulau mahā||

‘‘asaṃsayaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhau, uppaj‍jissati cakkhumā|

sañ‍ñāpaitvāna janataṃ, pañ‍casīlai kathaisahaṃ||

‘‘sutvāna pañ‍casīlāni, buddhuppādañ‍ca dul‍labhaṃ|

ubbaigajātā sumanā, tuṭṭhahaṭṭhā ahaṃsu tai||

‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ nimittaṃ viyākariṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, byākaraṇassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

vāraṇatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

8. vassikatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

aikaupi  saddhau maidhāvīti āyasmatau vassikatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau atthadassissa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍nacittau pilakkhaphalāni adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kausalaraṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vassikauti laddhanāmau vayappattau satthu yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauntau ābādhikau ahausi| atha naṃ ñātakā vaij‍japaridiṭṭhaina bhaisaj‍javidhinā upaṭṭhahitvā araugamakaṃsu| sau tamhā ābādhā vuṭṭhitau saṃvaigajātau bhāvanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.40-44) –

‘‘vanantarai  buddhaṃ disvā, atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pilakkhassa phalaṃ adā||

‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau  pana hutvā ākāsaina ñātakānaṃ santikai gantvā ākāsai ṭhitau dhammaṃ daisaitvā tai saraṇaisu sīlaisu ca patiṭṭhāpaisi| taisu kaici kālaṅkatā saraṇaisu sīlaisu ca patiṭṭhitattā saggai nibbattiṃsu| atha naṃ satthā buddhupaṭṭhānaṃ upagataṃ ‘‘kiṃ tai, vassika, ñātīnaṃ āraugya’’nti pucchi| sau ñātīnaṃ attanā kataṃ upakāraṃ satthu kathaintau –

240.

 ‘‘aikaupi saddhau maidhāvī, assaddhānīdha ñātinaṃ|

dhammaṭṭhau sīlasampan‍nau, hauti atthāya bandhunaṃ||

241.

 ‘‘niggayha anukampāya, cauditā ñātayau mayā|

ñātibandhavapaimaina, kāraṃ katvāna bhikkhusu||

242.

 ‘‘tai abbhatītā kālaṅkatā, pattā tai tidivaṃ sukhaṃ|

bhātarau mayhaṃ mātā ca, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tatthāyaṃ  paṭhamagāthāya atthau – yau kammaphalasaddhāya ca ratanattayasaddhāya ca vasaina saddhau, tatau aiva kammassakatañāṇādiyaugatau maidhāvī, satthu auvādadhammai navalaukuttaradhammai ca ṭhitattā dhammaṭṭhau, ācārasīlassa maggasīlassa phalasīlassa ca vasaina sīlasampan‍nau, sau aikaupi yathāvuttāya saddhāya abhāvaina assaddhānaṃ idha imasmiṃ laukai ‘‘amhākaṃ imai’’ti ñātabbaṭṭhaina ñātīnaṃ, tathā paimabandhanaina bandhanaṭṭhaina ‘‘bandhū’’ti ca laddhanāmānaṃ bandhavānaṃ atthāya hitāya hautīti|

aivaṃ  sādhāraṇatau vuttamatthaṃ attūpanāyikaṃ katvā dassaituṃ ‘‘niggayhā’’tiādinā itaragāthā vuttā| tattha niggayha anukampāya, cauditā ñātayau mayāti idānipi duggatā kusalaṃ akatvā āyatiṃ parik‍kilaisaṃ puna mānubhavitthāti niggahaitvā ñātayau mayā auvaditā| ñātibandhavapaimaina ‘‘amhākaṃ ayaṃ bandhavau’’ti aivaṃ pavattaina paimaina mama auvādaṃ atik‍kamituṃ asak‍kauntā kāraṃ katvāna bhikkhūsu pasan‍nacittā hutvā cīvarādipac‍cayadānaina caiva upaṭṭhānaina ca bhikkhūsu sak‍kārasammānaṃ katvā tai abbhatītā kālaṅkatā hutvā imaṃ laukaṃ atik‍kantā| puna taiti nipātamattaṃ| tidivaṃ sukhanti daivalaukapariyāpan‍nasukhaṃ, sukhaṃ vā iṭṭhaṃ tidivaṃ adhigatā| ‘‘kai pana tai’’ti āha| ‘‘bhātarau mayhaṃ mātā ca, maudanti kāmakāminau’’ti| attanā yathākāmitavatthukāmasamaṅginau hutvā abhiramantīti atthau|

vassikatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

9. yasaujatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsauti  āyasmatau yasaujatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau vipassissa bhagavatau kālai ārāmagaupakakulai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ vipassiṃ bhagavantaṃ ākāsaina gacchantaṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau labujaphalaṃ adāsi|

sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthinagaradvārai kaivaṭṭagāmai pañ‍cakulasatajaiṭṭhakassa kaivaṭṭassa puttau hutvā nibbatti, yasaujautissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau attanau sahāyaihi kaivaṭṭaputtaihi saddhiṃ macchagahaṇatthaṃ aciravatiyaṃ nadiyaṃ jālaṃ khipi| tatthaikau suvaṇṇavaṇṇau mahāmacchau antaujālaṃ pāvisi| taṃ tai rañ‍ñau pasainadissa dassaisuṃ| rājā ‘‘imassa suvaṇṇavaṇṇassa macchassa vaṇṇakāraṇaṃ bhagavā jānātī’’ti macchaṃ gāhāpaitvā bhagavatau  dassaisi| bhagavā ‘‘ayaṃ kassapassa sammāsambuddhassa sāsanai ausak‍kamānai pabbajitvā micchā paṭipaj‍jantau sāsanaṃ ausak‍kāpaitvā nirayai nibbattau aikaṃ buddhantaraṃ nirayai pac‍citvā tatau cutau aciravatiyaṃ macchau hutvā nibbattau’’ti vatvā tassa bhaginīnañ‍ca nirayai nibbattabhāvaṃ, tassa bhātikatthairassa parinibbutabhāvañ‍ca tainaiva kathāpaitvā imissā aṭṭhuppattiyā kapilasuttaṃ daisaisi|

satthu daisanaṃ sutvā yasaujau saṃvaigajātau saddhiṃ attanau sahāyaihi bhagavatau santikai pabbajitvā patirūpai ṭhānai vasantau aikadivasaṃ saparisau bhagavantaṃ vandituṃ jaitavanaṃ agamāsi| tassa āgamanai saināsanapañ‍ñāpanādinā vihārai uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddau ahausi| taṃ sutvā ‘‘bhagavā saparisaṃ yasaujaṃ paṇāmaisī’’ti (udā॰ 23) sabbaṃ udānai āgatanayaina vaiditabbaṃ| paṇāmitau pana āyasmā yasaujau kasābhihatau bhaddau assājānīyau viya saṃviggamānasau saddhiṃ parisāya vaggumudāya nadiyā tīrai vasantau ghaṭaintau vāyamantau vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā antauvassaiyaiva chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.32-39) –

‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, ārāmikau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

‘‘labujaṃ phalamādāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

ākāsaiva ṭhitau santau, paṭiggaṇhi mahāyasau||

‘‘vittisañ‍jānanau mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

‘‘adhigañchiṃ tadā pītiṃ, vipulaṃ sukhamuttamaṃ|

uppaj‍jataiva ratanaṃ, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñaṃ  pana samānaṃ saparisaṃ āyasmantaṃ yasaujaṃ satthā pak‍kausitvā ānaiñ‍jasamāpattinā paṭisanthāramakāsi| sau sabbaipi  dhutaṅgadhammai samādāya vattati| tainassa sarīraṃ kisaṃ ahausi lūkhaṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ, taṃ bhagavā paramappicchatāya pasaṃsantau –

243.

 ‘‘kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsau, kisau dhamanisanthatau|

mattañ‍ñū an‍napānamhi, adīnamānasau narau’’ti|| – paṭhamaṃ gāthamāha|

tattha kālapabbaṅgasaṅkāsauti maṃsūpacayavigamaina kisadusaṇṭhitasarīrāvayavatāya dantilatāpabbasadisaṅgau, taināha ‘‘kisau dhamanisanthatau’’ti| kisauti maunaiyyapaṭipadāpūraṇaina kisasarīrau| dhamanisanthatauti dhamanīhi santhatagattau appamaṃsalauhitatāya pākaṭīhi kaṇḍarasirāhi vitatasarīrau| mattañ‍ñūti pariyaisanapaṭiggahaṇaparibhaugavissaj‍janaisu pamāṇañ‍ñū| adīnamānasauti kausaj‍jādīhi anabhibhūtattā alīnacittau akusītavutti| narauti purisau, paurisassa dhurassa vahanatau paurisalakkhaṇasampan‍nau purisadhaurayhauti adhippāyau|

aivaṃ thairau satthārā pasaṭṭhau pasaṭṭhabhāvānurūpaṃ attanau adhivāsanakhantivīriyārambhavivaikābhiratikittanamukhaina bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ kathaintau –

244.

 ‘‘phuṭṭhau ḍaṃsaihi makasaihi, arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai|

nāgau saṅgāmasīsaiva, satau tatrādhivāsayai||

245.

 ‘‘yathā brahmā tathā aikau, yathā daivau tathā duvai|

yathā gāmau tathā tayau, kaulāhalaṃ tatuttari’’nti|| –

imā dvai gāthā abhāsi|

tattha nāgau saṅgāmasīsaivāti yathā nāma ājānaiyyau hatthināgau yuddhamaṇḍalai asisattitaumarādippahārai adhivāsaitvā parasainaṃ viddhaṃsaiti, aivaṃ bhikkhu arañ‍ñasmiṃ brahāvanai arañ‍ñāniyaṃ ḍaṃsādiparissayai satau  sampajānau adhivāsaiyya, adhivāsaitvā ca bhāvanābalaina mārabalaṃ vidhamaiyya|

yathā brahmāti yathā brahmā aikakau cittappakauparahitau jhānasukhaina nic‍camaiva sukhitau viharati tathā aikauti bhikkhupi aikau adutiyau vivaikasukhamanubrūhaintau sukhaṃ viharati| aikassa sāmañ‍ñasukhaṃ paṇītanti hi vuttaṃ| aitaina aikavihārī bhikkhu ‘‘brahmasamau’’ti auvādaṃ daiti| yathā daivau tathā duvaiti yathā daivānaṃ antarantarā  cittappakaupaupi siyā, tathā dvin‍naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sahavāsai ghaṭṭanāpi bhavaiyyāti sadutiyavāsaina bhikkhu ‘‘daivasamau’’ti vuttau| yathā gāmau tathā tayauti asmimaiva pāṭhai tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sahavāsau gāmavāsasadisau vivaikavāsau na hautīti adhippāyau| kaulāhalaṃ tatuttarinti tatau tayatau upari ca bahūnaṃ saṃvāsau kaulāhalaṃ uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddamahājanasan‍nipātasadisau, tasmā aikavihārinā bhavitabbanti adhippāyauti|

yasaujatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

10. sāṭimattiyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

ahu tuyhaṃ purai saddhāti āyasmatau sāṭimattiyatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau siddhatthassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau aikadivasaṃ satthāraṃ disvā pasan‍namānasau tālavaṇṭaṃ adāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai magadharaṭṭhai brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā sāṭimattiyauti laddhanāmau vayappattau haitusampan‍natāya ārañ‍ñakabhikkhūnaṃ santikai pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.38.43-47) –

‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, tālavaṇṭamadāsahaṃ|

sumanaihi paṭicchan‍naṃ, dhārayāmi mahārahaṃ||

‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, tālavaṇṭamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā bhikkhū auvadati anusāsati bahū ca sattai dhammaṃ kathaitvā saraṇaisu ca sīlaisu ca patiṭṭhāpaisi| añ‍ñatarañ‍ca kulaṃ assaddhaṃ appasan‍naṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ akāsi| taina tasmiṃ kulai manussā thairai abhippasan‍nā ahaisuṃ| tatthaikā dārikā abhirūpā dassanīyā thairaṃ piṇḍāya paviṭṭhaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ bhaujanaina parivisati| athaikadivasaṃ mārau ‘‘aivaṃ imassa ayasau vaḍḍhissati, appatiṭṭhau bhavissatī’’ti cintaitvā thairassa rūpaina gantvā taṃ dārikaṃ hatthai aggahaisi| dārikā ‘‘nāyaṃ manussasamphassau’’ti ca añ‍ñāsi, hatthañ‍ca muñ‍cāpaisi| taṃ disvā gharajanau thairai appasādaṃ janaisi| punadivasai thairau taṃ kāraṇaṃ anāvaj‍jaintau taṃ gharaṃ agamāsi| tattha manussā anādaraṃ akaṃsu| thairau taṃ kāraṇaṃ āvaj‍jaintau mārassa kiriyaṃ disvā ‘‘tassa gīvāyaṃ kuk‍kurakuṇapaṃ paṭimuñ‍catū’’ti adhiṭṭhahitvā  tassa maucanatthaṃ upagataina māraina atītadivasai katakiriyaṃ  kathāpaitvā taṃ taj‍jaitvā vissaj‍jaisi| taṃ disvā gharasāmikau ‘‘khamatha, bhantai, ac‍caya’’nti khamāpaitvā ‘‘aj‍jataggai ahamaiva, bhantai, tumhai upaṭṭhahāmī’’ti āha| thairau tassa dhammaṃ kathaintau –

246.

 ‘‘ahu tuyhaṃ purai saddhā, sā tai aj‍ja na vij‍jati|

yaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyhamaivaitaṃ, natthi duc‍caritaṃ mama||

247.

 ‘‘anic‍cā hi calā saddhā, aivaṃ diṭṭhā hi sā mayā|

raj‍jantipi viraj‍janti, tattha kiṃ jiyyatai muni||

248.

 ‘‘pac‍cati muninau bhattaṃ, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ kulai kulai|

piṇḍikāya carissāmi, atthi jaṅghabalaṃ mamā’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha  ahu tuyhaṃ purai saddhā, sā tai aj‍ja na vij‍jatīti, upāsaka, itau pubbai tava mayi ‘‘ayyau dhammacārī samacārī’’tiādinā saddhā ahausi, sā saddhā tai tava aj‍ja idāni na upalabbhati| tasmā yaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyhamaivaitanti catupac‍cayadānaṃ, tuyhamaiva aitaṃ hautu, na taina mayhaṃ atthau, sammā pasan‍nacittaina hi dānaṃ nāma dātabbanti adhippāyau| atha vā yaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyhamaivaitanti yaṃ tava mayi aj‍ja agāravaṃ pavattaṃ, taṃ tuyhamaiva, tassa phalaṃ tayā aiva pac‍canubhavitabbaṃ, na mayāti atthau| natthi duc‍caritaṃ mamāti mama pana duc‍caritaṃ nāma natthi maggainaiva duc‍caritahaitūnaṃ kilaisānaṃ samucchin‍nattā|

anic‍cā hi calā saddhāti yasmā pauthuj‍janikā saddhā anic‍cā aikantikā na hauti, tatau aiva calā assapiṭṭhai ṭhapitakumbhaṇḍaṃ viya, thusarāsimhi nikhātakhāṇukaṃ viya ca anavaṭṭhitā| aivaṃ diṭṭhā hi sā mayāti aivaṃ bhūtā ca sā saddhā mayā tayi diṭṭhā pac‍cakkhatau viditā| raj‍jantipi viraj‍jantīti aivaṃ tassā anavaṭṭhitattā aiva imai sattā kadāci katthaci mittasanthavavasaina raj‍janti sinaihampi karaunti, kadāci viraj‍janti virattacittā haunti| tattha kiṃ jiyyatai munīti tasmiṃ puthuj‍janānaṃ raj‍janai viraj‍janai ca muni pabbajitau kiṃ jiyyati, kā tassa hānīti atthau|

‘‘sacai mama pac‍cayai na gaṇhatha, kathaṃ tumhai yāpaithā’’ti aivaṃ mā cintayīti dassaintau ‘‘pac‍catī’’ti gāthamāha| tassatthau muninau pabbajitassa bhattaṃ nāma kulai kulai anugharaṃ divasai divasai  thaukaṃ thaukaṃ pac‍catai, na ca tuyhaṃ aiva gaihai| piṇḍikāya carissāmi, atthi jaṅghabalaṃ mamāti atthi mai jaṅghabalaṃ, nāhaṃ aubhaggajaṅghau na khañ‍jau na ca pādaraugī, tasmā  piṇḍikāya missakabhikkhāya carissāmi, ‘‘yathāpi bhamarau puppha’’ntiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 49; naitti॰ 123) satthārā vuttanayaina piṇḍāya caritvā yāpaissāmīti dassaiti|

sāṭimattiyatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

11. upālitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

saddhāya  abhinikkhammāti āyasmatau upālitthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai haṃsavatīnagarai kulagharai nibbattau aikadivasaṃ satthu dhammaṃ suṇantau satthāraṃ aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ vinayadharānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaintaṃ disvā adhikārakammaṃ katvā taṃ ṭhānantaraṃ patthaisi| sau yāvajīvaṃ kusalaṃ katvā daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai kappakagaihai paṭisandhiṃ gaṇhi, upālītissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| sau vayappattau anuruddhādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ khattiyānaṃ pasādakau hutvā tathāgatai anupiyambavanai viharantai pabbajanatthāya nikkhamantaihi chahi khattiyaihi saddhiṃ nikkhamitvā pabbaji| tassa pabbaj‍jāvidhānaṃ pāḷiyaṃ āgatamaiva (cūḷava॰ 330)|

sau pabbajitvā upasampan‍nau satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘mayhaṃ, bhantai, arañ‍ñavāsaṃ anujānāthā’’ti āha| bhikkhu tava arañ‍ñai vasantassa aikamaiva dhuraṃ vaḍḍhissati, amhākaṃ pana santikai vasantassa ganthadhurañ‍ca vipassanādhurañ‍ca paripūraissatīti| thairau satthu vacanaṃ sampaṭicchitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 1.1.441-595) –

‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, sujātau nāma brāhmaṇau|

asītikauṭinicayau, pahūtadhanadhañ‍ñavā||

‘‘ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

lakkhaṇai itihāsai ca, sadhammai pāramiṃ gatau||

‘‘paribbājā aikasikhā, gautamā buddhasāvakā|

carakā tāpasā caiva, caranti mahiyā tadā||

‘‘taipi  maṃ parivārainti, brāhmaṇau vissutau iti|

bahuj‍janau maṃ pūjaiti, nāhaṃ pūjaimi kiñ‍canaṃ||

‘‘pūjārahaṃ na passāmi, mānatthaddhau ahaṃ tadā|

buddhauti vacanaṃ natthi, tāva nuppaj‍jatai jinau||

‘‘ac‍cayaina ahaurattaṃ, padumuttaranāmakau|

sabbaṃ tamaṃ vinaudaitvā, laukai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā||

‘‘vitthārikai  bāhujañ‍ñai, puthubhūtai ca sāsanai|

upāgami tadā buddhau, nagaraṃ haṃsasavhayaṃ||

‘‘pitu atthāya sau buddhau, dhammaṃ daisaisi cakkhumā|

taina kālaina parisā, samantā yaujanaṃ tadā||

‘‘sammatau manujānaṃ sau, sunandau nāma tāpasau|

yāvatā buddhaparisā, pupphaihacchādayī tadā||

‘‘catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintai, saiṭṭhai ca pupphamaṇḍapai|

kauṭisatasahassānaṃ, dhammābhisamayau ahu||

‘‘sattarattindivaṃ buddhau, vassaitvā dhammavuṭṭhiyau|

aṭṭhamai divasai pattai, sunandaṃ kittayī jinau||

‘‘daivalaukai manussai vā, saṃsarantau ayaṃ bhavai|

sabbaisaṃ pavarau hutvā, bhavaisu saṃsarissati||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

mantāṇiputtau puṇṇauti, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘aivaṃ kittayi sambuddhau, sunandaṃ tāpasaṃ tadā|

hāsayantau janaṃ sabbaṃ, dassayantau sakaṃ balaṃ||

‘‘katañ‍jalī namassanti, sunandaṃ tāpasaṃ janā|

buddhai kāraṃ karitvāna, saudhaisi gatimattanau||

‘‘tattha mai ahu saṅkappau, sutvāna muninau vacaṃ|

ahampi kāraṃ kassāmi, yathā passāmi gautamaṃ||

‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, kiriyaṃ cintayiṃ mama|

kyāhaṃ kammaṃ ācarāmi, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai||

‘‘ayañ‍ca pāṭhikau bhikkhu, sabbapāṭhissa sāsanai|

vinayai agganikkhittau, taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayai ahaṃ||

‘‘idaṃ  mai amitaṃ bhaugaṃ, akkhaubhaṃ sāgarūpamaṃ|

taina bhaugaina buddhassa, ārāmaṃ māpayai ahaṃ||

‘‘saubhanaṃ nāma ārāmaṃ, nagarassa puratthatau|

kiṇitvā satasahassaina, saṅghārāmaṃ amāpayiṃ||

‘‘kūṭāgārai ca pāsādai, maṇḍapai hammiyai guhā|

caṅkamai sukatai katvā, saṅghārāmaṃ amāpayiṃ||

‘‘jantāgharaṃ aggisālaṃ, athau udakamāḷakaṃ|

nhānagharaṃ māpayitvā, bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ||

‘‘āsandiyau pīṭhakai ca, paribhaugai ca bhājanai|

ārāmikañ‍ca bhaisaj‍jaṃ, sabbamaitaṃ adāsahaṃ||

‘‘ārakkhaṃ paṭṭhapaitvāna, pākāraṃ kārayiṃ daḷhaṃ|

mā naṃ kauci vihaiṭhaisi, santacittāna tādinaṃ||

‘‘satasahassaināvāsaṃ, saṅghārāmai amāpayiṃ|

vaipul‍laṃ taṃ māpayitvā, sambuddhaṃ upanāmayiṃ||

‘‘niṭṭhāpitau mayārāmau, sampaṭiccha tuvaṃ muni|

niyyādaissāmi taṃ vīra, adhivāsaihi cakkhuma||

‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, adhivāsaisi nāyakau||

‘‘adhivāsanamañ‍ñāya, sabbañ‍ñussa mahaisinau|

bhaujanaṃ paṭiyādaitvā, kālamāraucayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘āraucitamhi kālamhi, padumuttaranāyakau|

khīṇāsavasahassaihi, ārāmaṃ mai upāgami||

‘‘nisin‍naṃ kālamañ‍ñāya, an‍napānaina tappayiṃ|

bhuttāviṃ kālamañ‍ñāya, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

‘‘kītau satasahassaina, tattakainaiva kāritau|

saubhanau nāma ārāmau, sampaṭiccha tuvaṃ muni||

‘‘iminārāmadānaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

bhavai nibbattamānauhaṃ, labhāmi mama patthitaṃ||

‘‘paṭiggahaitvā sambuddhau, saṅghārāmaṃ sumāpitaṃ|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

‘‘yau sau buddhassa pādāsi, saṅghārāmaṃ sumāpitaṃ|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

‘‘hatthī  assā rathā pattī, sainā ca caturaṅginī|

parivāraissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘saṭṭhi tūrasahassāni, bhairiyau samalaṅkatā|

parivāraissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘chaḷasītisahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā||

‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivāraissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

sahassakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

‘‘daivarājaina pattabbaṃ, sabbaṃ paṭilabhissati|

anūnabhaugau hutvāna, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati||

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

upāli nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

‘‘vinayai pāramiṃ patvā, ṭhānāṭhānai ca kauvidau|

jinasāsanaṃ dhāraintau, viharissatināsavau||

‘‘sabbamaitaṃ  abhiñ‍ñāya, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

‘‘aparimaiyyupādāya, patthaimi tava sāsanaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, sabbasaṃyaujanakkhayau||

‘‘yathā sūlāvutau pausau, rājadaṇḍaina taj‍jitau|

sūlai sātaṃ avindantau, parimuttiṃva icchati||

‘‘tathaivāhaṃ mahāvīra, bhavadaṇḍaina taj‍jitau|

kammasūlāvutau santau, pipāsāvaidanaṭṭitau||

‘‘bhavai sātaṃ na vindāmi, ḍayhantau tīhi aggibhi|

parimuttiṃ gavaisāmi, yathāpi rājadaṇḍitau||

‘‘yathā visādau purisau, visaina paripīḷitau|

agadaṃ sau gavaisaiyya, visaghātāyupālanaṃ||

‘‘gavaisamānau  passaiyya, agadaṃ visaghātakaṃ|

taṃ pivitvā sukhī assa, visamhā parimuttiyā||

‘‘tathaivāhaṃ mahāvīra, yathā visahatau narau|

sampīḷitau avij‍jāya, saddhammāgadamaisahaṃ||

‘‘dhammāgadaṃ gavaisantau, addakkhiṃ sakyasāsanaṃ|

aggaṃ sabbausadhānaṃ taṃ, sabbasal‍lavinaudanaṃ||

‘‘dhammausadhaṃ pivitvāna, visaṃ sabbaṃ samūhaniṃ|

ajarāmaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ, nibbānaṃ phassayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘yathā bhūtaṭṭitau pausau, bhūtaggāhaina pīḷitau|

bhūtavaij‍jaṃ gavaisaiyya, bhūtasmā parimuttiyā||

‘‘gavaisamānau passaiyya, bhūtavij‍jāsu kauvidaṃ|

tassa sau vihanai bhūtaṃ, samūlañ‍ca vināsayai||

‘‘tathaivāhaṃ mahāvīra, tamaggāhaina pīḷitau|

ñāṇālaukaṃ gavaisāmi, tamatau parimuttiyā||

‘‘athaddasaṃ  sakyamuniṃ, kilaisatamasaudhanaṃ|

sau mai tamaṃ vinaudaisi, bhūtavaij‍jauva bhūtakaṃ||

‘‘saṃsārasautaṃ sañchindiṃ, taṇhāsautaṃ nivārayiṃ|

bhavaṃ ugghāṭayiṃ sabbaṃ, bhūtavaij‍jauva mūlatau||

‘‘garuḷau yathā aupatati, pan‍nagaṃ bhakkhamattanau|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, vikkhaubhaiti mahāsaraṃ||

‘‘pan‍nagaṃ sau gahaitvāna, adhausīsaṃ vihaiṭhayaṃ|

ādāya sau pak‍kamati, yainakāmaṃ vihaṅgamau||

‘‘tathaivāhaṃ mahāvīra, yathāpi garuḷau balī|

asaṅkhataṃ gavaisantau, dausai vikkhālayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘diṭṭhau ahaṃ dhammavaraṃ, santipadamanuttaraṃ|

ādāya viharāmaitaṃ, garuḷau pan‍nagaṃ yathā||

‘‘āsāvatī nāma latā, jātā cittalatāvanai|

tassā vassasahassaina, aikaṃ nibbattatai phalaṃ||

‘‘taṃ daivā payirupāsanti, tāvadūraphalai sati|

daivānaṃ sā piyā aivaṃ, āsāvatī latuttamā||

‘‘satasahassupādāya, tāhaṃ paricarai muni|

sāyaṃ pātaṃ namassāmi, daivā āsāvatiṃ yathā||

‘‘avañjhā  pāricariyā, amaughā ca namassanā|

dūrāgatampi maṃ santaṃ, khaṇauyaṃ na virādhayi||

‘‘paṭisandhiṃ na passāmi, vicinantau bhavai ahaṃ|

nirūpadhi vippamuttau, upasantau carāmahaṃ||

‘‘yathāpi padumaṃ nāma, sūriyaraṃsaina pupphati|

tathaivāhaṃ mahāvīra, buddharaṃsaina pupphitau||

‘‘yathā balākayaunimhi, na vij‍jati pumau sadā|

maighaisu gaj‍jamānaisu, gabbhaṃ gaṇhanti tā sadā||

‘‘cirampi  gabbhaṃ dhārainti, yāva maighau na gaj‍jati|

bhāratau parimuc‍canti, yadā maighau pavassati||

‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, dhammamaighaina gaj‍jatau|

saddaina dhammamaighassa, dhammagabbhaṃ agaṇhahaṃ||

‘‘satasahassupādāya, puñ‍ñagabbhaṃ dharaimahaṃ|

nappamuc‍cāmi bhāratau, dhammamaighau na gaj‍jati||

‘‘yadā tuvaṃ sakyamuni, rammai kapilavatthavai|

gaj‍jasi dhammamaighaina, bhāratau parimuc‍cahaṃ||

‘‘suñ‍ñataṃ animittañ‍ca, tathāppaṇihitampi ca|

caturau ca phalai sabbai, dhammaivaṃ vijanayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘aparimaiyyupādāya, patthaimi tava sāsanaṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, santipadamanuttaraṃ||

‘‘vinayai pāramiṃ pattau, yathāpi pāṭhikau isi|

na mai samasamau atthi, dhāraimi sāsanaṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘vinayai khandhakai cāpi, tikacchaidai ca pañ‍cakai|

aittha mai vimati natthi, akkharai byañ‍janaipi vā||

‘‘niggahai paṭikammai ca, ṭhānāṭhānai ca kauvidau|

ausāraṇai vuṭṭhāpanai, sabbattha pāramiṃ gatau||

‘‘vinayai khandhakai vāpi, nikkhipitvā padaṃ ahaṃ|

ubhatau vinivaiṭhaitvā, rasatau ausaraiyyahaṃ||

‘‘niruttiyā sukusalau, atthānatthai ca kauvidau|

anañ‍ñātaṃ mayā natthi, aikaggau satthu sāsanai||

‘‘rūpadakkhau ahaṃ aj‍ja, sakyaputtassa sāsanai|

kaṅkhaṃ sabbaṃ vinaudaimi, chindāmi sabbasaṃsayaṃ||

‘‘padaṃ  anupadañ‍cāpi, akkharañ‍cāpi byañ‍janaṃ|

nidānai pariyausānai, sabbattha kauvidau ahaṃ||

‘‘yathāpi  rājā balavā, niggaṇhitvā parantapai|

vijinitvāna saṅgāmaṃ, nagaraṃ tattha māpayai||

‘‘pākāraṃ parikhañ‍cāpi, aisikaṃ dvārakauṭṭhakaṃ|

aṭṭālakai ca vividhai, kārayai nagarai bahū||

‘‘siṅghāṭakaṃ cac‍carañ‍ca, suvibhattantarāpaṇaṃ|

kārayaiyya sabhaṃ tattha, atthānatthavinicchayaṃ||

‘‘nigghātatthaṃ amittānaṃ, chiddāchiddañ‍ca jānituṃ|

balakāyassa rakkhāya, saināpac‍caṃ ṭhapaiti sau||

‘‘ārakkhatthāya bhaṇḍassa, nidhānakusalaṃ naraṃ|

mā mai bhaṇḍaṃ vinassīti, bhaṇḍarakkhaṃ ṭhapaiti sau||

‘‘mamattau hauti yau rañ‍ñau, vuddhiṃ yassa ca icchati|

tassādhikaraṇaṃ daiti, mittassa paṭipaj‍jituṃ||

‘‘uppātaisu nimittaisu, lakkhaṇaisu ca kauvidaṃ|

ajjhāyakaṃ mantadharaṃ, paurauhic‍cai ṭhapaiti sau||

‘‘aitaihaṅgaihi sampan‍nau, khattiyauti pavuc‍cati|

sadā rakkhanti rājānaṃ, cak‍kavākauva dukkhitaṃ||

‘‘tathaiva tvaṃ mahāvīra, hatāmittauva khattiyau|

sadaivakassa laukassa, dhammarājāti vuc‍cati||

‘‘titthiyai nihanitvāna, mārañ‍cāpi sasainakaṃ|

tamandhakāraṃ vidhamitvā, dhammanagaraṃ amāpayi||

‘‘sīlaṃ pākārakaṃ tattha, ñāṇaṃ tai dvārakauṭṭhakaṃ|

saddhā tai aisikā vīra, dvārapālau ca saṃvarau||

‘‘satipaṭṭhānamaṭṭālaṃ, pañ‍ñā tai cac‍caraṃ munai|

iddhipādañ‍ca siṅghāṭaṃ, dhammavīthi sumāpitā||

‘‘suttantaṃ abhidhammañ‍ca, vinayañ‍cāpi kaivalaṃ|

navaṅgaṃ buddhavacanaṃ, aisā dhammasabhā tava||

‘‘suñ‍ñataṃ  animittañ‍ca, vihārañ‍cappaṇīhitaṃ|

ānaiñ‍jañ‍ca niraudhau ca, aisā dhammakuṭī tava||

‘‘pañ‍ñāya aggau nikkhittau, paṭibhānai ca kauvidau|

sāriputtauti nāmaina, dhammasaināpatī tava||

‘‘cutūpapātakusalau, iddhiyā pāramiṃ gatau|

kaulitau nāma nāmaina, paurauhic‍cau tavaṃ munai||

‘‘paurāṇakavaṃsadharau, uggataijau durāsadau|

dhutavādīguṇainaggau, akkhadassau tavaṃ munai||

‘‘bahussutau dhammadharau, sabbapāṭhī ca sāsanai|

ānandau nāma nāmaina, dhammārakkhau tavaṃ munai||

‘‘aitai sabbai atik‍kamma, pamaisi bhagavā mamaṃ|

vinicchayaṃ mai pādāsi, vinayai viñ‍ñudaisitaṃ||

‘‘yau kauci vinayai pañhaṃ, pucchati buddhasāvakau|

tattha mai cintanā natthi, tañ‍ñaivatthaṃ kathaimahaṃ||

‘‘yāvatā buddhakhaittamhi, ṭhapaitvā taṃ mahāmuni|

vinayai mādisau natthi, kutau bhiyyau bhavissati||

‘‘bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aivaṃ gaj‍jati gautamau|

upālissa samau natthi, vinayai khandhakaisu ca||

‘‘yāvatā buddhabhaṇitaṃ, navaṅgaṃ satthusāsanaṃ|

vinayaugadhaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ, vinayamūlapassinau||

‘‘mama kammaṃ saritvāna, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

‘‘satasahassupādāya, imaṃ ṭhānaṃ apatthayiṃ|

sau mai atthau anuppattau, vinayai pāramiṃ gatau||

‘‘sakyānaṃ nandijananau, kappakau āsahaṃ purai|

vijahitvāna taṃ jātiṃ, puttau jātau mahaisinau||

‘‘itau  dutiyakai kappai, añ‍jasau nāma khattiyau|

anantataijau amitayasau, bhūmipālau mahaddhanau||

‘‘tassa rañ‍ñau ahaṃ puttau, candanau nāma khattiyau|

jātimadainupatthaddhau, yasabhaugamadaina ca||

‘‘nāgasatasahassāni, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā|

tidhāpabhin‍nā mātaṅgā, parivārainti maṃ sadā||

‘‘sabalaihi paraitauhaṃ, uyyānaṃ gantukāmakau|

āruyha sirikaṃ nāgaṃ, nagarā nikkhamiṃ tadā||

‘‘caraṇaina ca sampan‍nau, guttadvārau susaṃvutau|

daivalau nāma sambuddhau, āgacchi puratau mama||

‘‘paisaitvā  sirikaṃ nāgaṃ, buddhaṃ āsādayiṃ tadā|

tatau sañ‍jātakaupau sau, nāgau nuddharatai padaṃ||

‘‘nāgaṃ ruṇṇamanaṃ disvā, buddhai kaudhaṃ akāsahaṃ|

vihaisayitvā sambuddhaṃ, uyyānaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

‘‘sātaṃ tattha na vindāmi, sirau paj‍jalitau yathā|

pariḷāhaina ḍayhāmi, macchauva baḷisādakau||

‘‘sasāgarantā pathavī, ādittā viya hauti mai|

pitu santikupāgamma, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

‘‘āsīvisaṃva kupitaṃ, aggikkhandhaṃva āgataṃ|

mattaṃva kuñ‍jaraṃ dantiṃ, yaṃ sayambhumasādayiṃ||

‘‘āsāditau mayā buddhau, ghaurau uggatapau jinau|

purā sabbai vinassāma, khamāpaissāma taṃ muniṃ||

‘‘nau cai taṃ nijjhāpaissāma, attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ|

auraina sattadivasā, raṭṭhaṃ mai vidhamissati||

‘‘sumaikhalau kausiyau ca, siggavau cāpi sattakau|

āsādayitvā isayau, duggatā tai saraṭṭhakā||

‘‘yadā  kuppanti isayau, sañ‍ñatā brahmacārinau|

sadaivakaṃ vināsainti, sasāgaraṃ sapabbataṃ||

‘‘tiyaujanasahassamhi, purisai san‍nipātayiṃ|

ac‍cayaṃ daisanatthāya, sayambhuṃ upasaṅkamiṃ||

‘‘al‍lavatthā al‍lasirā, sabbaiva pañ‍jalīkatā|

buddhassa pādai nipatitvā, idaṃ vacanamabravuṃ||

‘‘khamassu tvaṃ mahāvīra, abhiyācati taṃ janau|

pariḷāhaṃ vinaudaihi, mā nau raṭṭhaṃ vināsaya||

‘‘sadaivamānusā sabbai, sadānavā sarakkhasā|

ayaumayaina kūṭaina, siraṃ bhindaiyyu mai sadā||

‘‘dakai aggi na saṇṭhāti, bījaṃ sailai na rūhati|

agadai kimi na saṇṭhāti, kaupau buddhai na jāyati||

‘‘yathā ca bhūmi acalā, appamaiyyau ca sāgarau|

anantakau ca ākāsau, aivaṃ buddhā akhaubhiyā||

‘‘sadā khantā mahāvīrā, khamitā ca tapassinau|

khantānaṃ khamitānañ‍ca, gamanaṃ taṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sambuddhau, pariḷāhaṃ vinaudayaṃ|

mahājanassa puratau, nabhaṃ abbhuggamī tadā||

‘‘taina kammainahaṃ vīra, hīnattaṃ ajjhupāgatau|

samatik‍kamma taṃ jātiṃ, pāvisiṃ abhayaṃ puraṃ||

‘‘tadāpi maṃ mahāvīra, ḍayhamānaṃ susaṇṭhitaṃ|

pariḷāhaṃ vinaudaisi, sayambhuñ‍ca khamāpayiṃ||

‘‘aj‍jāpi maṃ mahāvīra, ḍayhamānaṃ tihaggibhi|

nibbāpaisi tayau aggī, sītibhāvañ‍ca pāpayiṃ||

‘‘yaisaṃ sautāvadhānatthi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau|

atthaṃ tuyhaṃ pavakkhāmi, yathā diṭṭhaṃ padaṃ mama||

‘‘sayambhuṃ  taṃ vimānaitvā, santacittaṃ samāhitaṃ|

taina kammainahaṃ aj‍ja, jātaumhi nīcayauniyaṃ||

‘‘mā vau khaṇaṃ virādhaitha, khaṇātītā hi saucarai|

sadatthai vāyamaiyyātha, khaṇau vau paṭipāditau||

‘‘aikac‍cānañ‍ca vamanaṃ, aikac‍cānaṃ viraicanaṃ|

visaṃ halāhalaṃ aikai, aikac‍cānañ‍ca ausadhaṃ||

‘‘vamanaṃ paṭipan‍nānaṃ, phalaṭṭhānaṃ viraicanaṃ|

ausadhaṃ phalalābhīnaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ gavaisinaṃ||

‘‘sāsanaina viruddhānaṃ, visaṃ halāhalaṃ yathā|

āsīvisau diṭṭhavisau, aivaṃ jhāpaiti taṃ naraṃ||

‘‘sakiṃ pītaṃ halāhalaṃ, uparundhati jīvitaṃ|

sāsanaina virujjhitvā, kappakauṭimhi ḍayhati||

‘‘khantiyā avihiṃsāya, maittacittavatāya ca|

sadaivakaṃ sau tāraiti, tasmā tai avirādhiyā||

‘‘lābhālābhai na saj‍janti, sammānanavimānanai|

pathavīsadisā buddhā, tasmā tai na virādhiyā||

‘‘daivadattai ca vadhakai, caurai aṅgulimālakai|

rāhulai dhanapālai ca, sabbaisaṃ samakau muni||

‘‘aitaisaṃ paṭighau natthi, rāgaumaisaṃ na vij‍jati|

sabbaisaṃ samakau buddhau, vadhakassaurasassa ca||

‘‘panthai disvāna kāsāvaṃ, chaḍḍitaṃ mīḷhamakkhitaṃ|

sirasmiṃ añ‍jaliṃ katvā, vanditabbaṃ isiddhajaṃ||

‘‘abbhatītā  ca yai buddhā, vattamānā anāgatā|

dhajainānaina sujjhanti, tasmā aitai namassiyā||

‘‘satthukappaṃ suvinayaṃ, dhāraimi hadayainahaṃ|

namassamānau vinayaṃ, viharissāmi sabbadā||

‘‘vinayau  āsayau mayhaṃ, vinayau ṭhānacaṅkamaṃ|

kappaimi vinayai vāsaṃ, vinayau mama gaucarau||

‘‘vinayai pāramippattau, samathai cāpi kauvidau|

upāli taṃ mahāvīra, pādai vandati satthunau||

‘‘sau ahaṃ vicarissāmi, gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ|

namassamānau sambuddhaṃ, dhammassa ca sudhammataṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

tattha hi naṃ satthā sayamaiva sakalaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ uggaṇhāpaisi| sau aparabhāgai bhārukacchakavatthuṃ (pārā॰ 78) aj‍jukavatthuṃ (pārā॰ 158) kumārakassapavatthunti imāni tīṇi vatthūni vinicchayi| satthā aikaikasmiṃ vinicchitai sādhukāraṃ datvā tayaupi vinicchayai aṭṭhuppattiṃ katvā thairaṃ vinayadharānaṃ aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi| sau aparabhāgai aikasmiṃ upausathadivasai pātimaukkhuddaisasamayai bhikkhū auvadantau –

249.

 ‘‘saddhāya  abhinikkhamma, navapabbajitau navau|

mittai bhajaiyya kalyāṇai, suddhājīvai atanditai||

250.

 ‘‘saddhāya abhinikkhamma, navapabbajitau navau|

saṅghasmiṃ viharaṃ bhikkhu, sikkhaitha vinayaṃ budhau||

251.

 ‘‘saddhāya abhinikkhamma, navapabbajitau navau|

kappākappaisu kusalau, caraiyya apurakkhatau’’ti|| – tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha saddhāyāti saddhānimittaṃ, na jīvikatthanti atthau| saddhāyāti vā kammaphalāni ratanattayaguṇañ‍ca saddahitvā| abhinikkhammāti gharāvāsatau nikkhamitvā| navapabbajitauti navau hutvā pabbajitau, paṭhamavayai aiva pabbajitau| navauti sāsanai sikkhāya abhinavau daharau| mittai bhajaiyya kalyāṇai suddhājīvai atanditaiti ‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 7.37) vuttalakkhaṇai kalyāṇamittai, micchājīvavivaj‍janaina suddhājīvai, āraddhavīriyatāya atanditai  bhajaiyya upasaṅkamaiyya, taisaṃ auvādānusāsanīpaṭiggahaṇavasaina saivaiyya| saṅghasmiṃ viharanti saṅghai bhikkhusamūhai vattapaṭivattapūraṇavasaina viharantau| sikkhaitha vinayaṃ budhauti baudhañāṇatāsukusalau hutvā vinayapariyattiṃ sikkhaiyya| vinayau hi sāsanassa āyu, tasmiṃ ṭhitai sāsanaṃ ṭhitaṃ hauti| ‘‘buddhau’’ti ca paṭhanti, sau aivatthau| kappākappaisūti kappiyākappiyaisu kusalau suttavasaina suttānulaumavasaina ca nipuṇau chaikau| apurakkhatauti na purakkhatau taṇhādīhi kutauci puraikkhāraṃ apac‍cāsīsantau hutvā viharaiyya|

upālitthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

12. uttarapālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

paṇḍitaṃ vata maṃ santanti āyasmatau uttarapālatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau vipassissa bhagavatau gamanamaggai saituṃ kārāpaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā uttarapālauti laddhanāmau vayappattau yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ disvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā samaṇadhammaṃ karauti| tassa aikadivasaṃ ayaunisaumanasikāravasaina  anubhūtārammaṇaṃ anussarantassa kāmarāgau uppaj‍ji| sau tāvadaiva sahauḍḍhaṃ cauraṃ gaṇhantau viya attanau cittaṃ niggahaitvā saṃvaigajātau paṭipakkhamanasikāraina kilaisai vikkhambhaitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau bhāvanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.16-20) –

‘‘vipassinau bhagavatau, caṅkamantassa sammukhā|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, saituṃ kārāpayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ saituṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saitudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau paṭipattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā sīhanādaṃ nadantau –

252.

 ‘‘paṇḍitaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ, alamatthavicintakaṃ|

pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā laukai, sammauhā pātayiṃsu maṃ||

253.

 ‘‘pakkhandau  māravisayai, daḷhasal‍lasamappitau|

asakkhiṃ mac‍curājassa, ahaṃ pāsā pamuc‍cituṃ||

254.

 ‘‘sabbai kāmā pahīnā mai, bhavā sabbai padālitā|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha paṇḍitaṃ vata maṃ santanti sutacintāmayāya pañ‍ñāya vasaina pañ‍ñāsampan‍nampi nāma maṃ samānaṃ| alamatthavicintakanti attanau ca paraisañ‍ca atthaṃ hitaṃ vicintaituṃ samatthaṃ, alaṃ vā pariyattaṃ atthassa vicintakaṃ, kilaisaviddhaṃsanasamatthaṃ atthadassinaṃ vā, sabbamaitaṃ attanau antimabhavikatāya thairau vadati| pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāti rūpādayau pañ‍ca kāmakauṭṭhāsā| laukaiti taisaṃ pavattiṭṭhānadassanaṃ| sammauhāti sammauhanimittaṃ ayaunisaumanasikārahaitu| sammauhāti vā sammauhanā sammauhakarā| pātayiṃsūti dhīrabhāvatau pātaisuṃ, laukatau vā uttaritukāmaṃ maṃ laukai pātayiṃsūti atthau|

pakkhandauti anupaviṭṭhau| māravisayaiti kilaisavisayai kilaisamārassa pavattiṭṭhānai, tassa vasaṃ gatauti adhippāyau| daivaputtamārassa vā issariyaṭṭhānai taṃ anupavisitvā ṭhitau| daḷhasal‍lasamappitauti daḷhaṃ thiraṃ, daḷhaina vā sal‍laina samappitau, rāgasal‍laina hadayaṃ āhac‍ca viddhau| asakkhiṃ mac‍curājassa, ahaṃ pāsā pamuc‍citunti aggamaggasaṇḍāsaina rāgādisal‍laṃ anavasaisatau uddharantauyaiva rāgabandhanasaṅkhātā  mac‍curājassa pāsā ahaṃ parimuc‍cituṃ asakkhiṃ, tatau attānaṃ pamaucaisiṃ|

tatau aiva ca sabbai kāmā pahīnā mai, bhavā sabbai padālitāti vatthārammaṇādibhaidaina anaikabhaidabhin‍nā  sabbai kilaisakāmā ariyamaggaina samucchaidavasaina mayā pahīnā| kilaisakāmaisu hi pahīnaisu vatthukāmāpi pahīnā aiva haunti| tathā kāmabhavakammabhavādayau bhavā sabbai maggañāṇāsinā padālitā viddhaṃsitā| kammabhavaisu hi padālitaisu upapattibhavā padālitā aiva haunti| aivaṃ kammabhavānaṃ padālitattā aiva vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavauti| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| idamaiva ca thairassa añ‍ñābyākaraṇaṃ ahausi|

uttarapālatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

13. abhibhūtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

suṇātha  ñātayau sabbaiti āyasmatau abhibhūtatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni upacinantau vaissabhussa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau tādisaina kalyāṇamittasan‍nissayaina sāsanai abhippasan‍nau ahausi| sau satthari parinibbutai tassa dhātuṃ gahaituṃ mahājanai ussāhaṃ karauntai sayaṃ sabbapaṭhamaṃ gandhaudakaina citakaṃ nibbāpaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai vaiṭhapuranagarai rājakulai nibbattitvā abhibhūtauti laddhanāmau pitu ac‍cayaina raj‍jaṃ kāraiti| tasmiñ‍ca samayai bhagavā janapadacārikaṃ carantau anupubbaina taṃ nagaraṃ pāpuṇi| tatau sau rājā ‘‘bhagavā kira mama nagaraṃ anuppattau’’ti sutvā satthu santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā dutiyadivasai mahādānaṃ pavattaisi| bhagavā bhuttāvī tassa rañ‍ñau ajjhāsayānurūpaṃ anumaudanaṃ karauntauyaiva vitthāratau dhammaṃ daisaisi| sau dhammaṃ sutvā laddhappasādau raj‍jaṃ pahāya pabbajitvā arahattaṃ sacchākāsi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.11-15) –

‘‘dayhamānai  sarīramhi, vaissabhussa mahaisinau|

gandhaudakaṃ gahaitvāna, citaṃ nibbāpayiṃ ahaṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, citaṃ nibbāpayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, gandhaudakassidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā vimuttisukhaina viharantai tasmiṃ tassa ñātakā amac‍cā pārisaj‍jā nāgarā jānapadāti sabbai samāgantvā, ‘‘bhantai, kasmā tvaṃ amhai anāthai katvā pabbajitau’’ti paridaiviṃsu| thairau tai ñātipamukhai manussai paridaivantai disvā taisaṃ attanau pabbaj‍jakāraṇavibhāvanamukhaina dhammaṃ kathaintau –

255.

 ‘‘suṇātha  ñātayau sabbai, yāvantaittha samāgatā|

dhammaṃ vau daisayissāmi, dukkhā jāti punappunaṃ||

256.

 ‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

257.

 ‘‘yau  imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, appamattau vihassati|

pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti|| – tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha suṇāthāti nisāmaitha, idāni mayā vuc‍camānaṃ auhitasautā sautadvārānusāraina upadhāraithāti atthau| ñātayauti ñātī pamukhai katvā taisaṃ sabbaisaṃ ālapanaṃ, taināha ‘‘sabbai yāvantaittha samāgatā’’ti, yāvantau yattakā aittha samāgamai, aitissaṃ vā mama pabbaj‍jāya samāgatāti atthau|

idāni yaṃ sandhāya ‘‘suṇāthā’’ti savanāṇattikavacanaṃ kataṃ, taṃ ‘‘dhammaṃ vau daisayissāmī’’ti paṭijānitvā ‘‘dukkhā jāti punappuna’’ntiādinā daisaituṃ ārabhi| tattha dukkhā jāti punappunanti jāti nāmaisā gabbhauk‍kantimūlakādibhaidassa jarādibhaidassa ca anaikavihitassa dukkhassa adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau dukkhā| sā punappunaṃ pavattamānā ativiya dukkhā|

tassā  pana jātiyā samatik‍kamanatthaṃ ussāhau karaṇīyauti dassaintau āha ‘‘ārambhathā’’tiādi| tattha ārambhathāti ārambhadhātusaṅkhātaṃ vīriyaṃ karautha| nik‍kamathāti kausaj‍japakkhatau nikkhantattā nik‍kamadhātusaṅkhātaṃ taduttariṃ vīriyaṃ karautha| yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanaiti yasmā sīlasaṃvarau indriyaisu guttadvāratā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā satisampajañ‍ñanti imaisu dhammaisu patiṭṭhitānaṃ jāgariyānuyaugavasaina ārambhanik‍kamadhātuyau sampaj‍janti, tasmā tathābhūtā samathavipassanāsaṅkhātai adhisīlasikkhādisaṅkhātai vā bhagavatau sāsanai yuttappayuttā hautha| dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarauti aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jantā ca taidhātuissarassa mac‍curājassa vasaṃ sattai naitīti tassa saināsaṅkhātaṃ abalaṃ dubbalaṃ yathā nāma thāmabalūpapan‍nau kuñ‍jarau naḷaihi kataṃ agāraṃ khaṇainaiva viddhaṃsaiti, aivamaiva kilaisagaṇaṃ dhunātha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaithāti atthau|

aivaṃ pana buddhasāsanai ussāhaṃ karauntassa aikaṃsikau jātidukkhassa samatik‍kamauti dassaintau ‘‘yau imasmi’’ntiādinā tatiyaṃ gāthamāha| taṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyamaiva|

abhibhūtatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

14. gautamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

saṃsaranti  āyasmatau gautamatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayaṃ kira purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai puñ‍ñāni karauntau sikhimhi bhagavati parinibbutai tassa citakaṃ daivamanussaisu pūjaintaisu aṭṭhahi campakapupphaihi citakaṃ pūjaisi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sakyarājakulai nibbattitvā gautamauti gauttavasainaiva abhilakkhitanāmau vayappattau satthu ñātisamāgamai paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitvā vipassanāya kammaṃ karauntau chaḷabhiñ‍ñau ahausi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.47.6-10) –

‘‘jhāyamānassa  bhagavatau, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

aṭṭha campakapupphāni, citakaṃ abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘aikatiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

chaḷabhiñ‍ñau pana hutvā vimuttisukhaina viharantau aikadivasaṃ ñātakaihi ‘‘kasmā, bhantai, amhai pahāya pabbajitau’’ti puṭṭhau saṃsārai attanā anubhūtadukkhañ‍caiva idāni adhigataṃ nibbānasukhañ‍ca pakāsaintau –

258.

 ‘‘saṃsarañhi nirayaṃ agacchissaṃ, paitalaukamagamaṃ punappunaṃ|

dukkhamamhipi tiracchānayauniyaṃ, naikadhā hi vusitaṃ ciraṃ mayā||

259.

 ‘‘mānusaupi ca bhavaubhirādhitau, saggakāyamagamaṃ sakiṃ sakiṃ|

rūpadhātusu arūpadhātusu, naivasañ‍ñisu asañ‍ñisuṭṭhitaṃ||

260.

 ‘‘sambhavā suviditā asārakā, saṅkhatā pacalitā sadairitā|

taṃ viditvā mahamahattasambhavaṃ, santimaiva satimā samajjhaga’’nti|| –

tīhi gāthāhi taisaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi|

tattha saṃsaranti anādimati saṃsārai saṃsarantau kammakilaisaihi pañ‍casu gatīsu cavanupapātavasaina aparāparaṃ saṃsarantauti atthau| hīti nipātamattaṃ| nirayaṃ agacchissanti sañ‍jīvādikaṃ aṭṭhavidhaṃ mahānirayaṃ, kuk‍kuḷādikaṃ sauḷasavidhaṃ ussadanirayañ‍ca paṭisandhivasaina upagacchiṃ| ‘‘punappuna’’nti idaṃ idhāpi ānaitabbaṃ| paitalaukanti paittivisayaṃ, khuppipāsādibhaidaṃ paitattabhāvanti  atthau| agamanti paṭisandhivasaina upagacchiṃ upapaj‍jiṃ| punappunanti aparāparaṃ| dukkhamamhipīti añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ tikhiṇakasāpataudābhighātādidukkhaihi dussahāyapi| liṅgavipal‍lāsaina haitaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘dukkhamamhipī’’ti| tiracchānayauniyanti migapakkhiādibhaidāya tiracchānayauniyaṃ| naikadhā hīti auṭṭhagauṇagadrabhādivasaina caiva kākabalākakulalādivasaina ca anaikappakāraṃ anaikavārañ‍ca ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ mayā vusitaṃ nic‍caṃ utrastamānasatādivasaina dukkhaṃ anubhūtaṃ| tiracchānayauniyaṃ nibbattasattau mahāmūḷhatāya cirataraṃ tatthaiva aparāparaṃ parivattatīti dassanatthaṃ idha ‘‘cira’’nti vuttaṃ|

mānusaupi ca bhavaubhirādhitauti manussattabhāvaupi mayā tādisaina kusalakammunā samavāyaina abhirādhitau sādhitau adhigatau| kāṇakacchapaupamasuttamaittha (ma॰ ni॰ 3.252; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1117) udāharitabbaṃ| saggakāyamagamaṃ sakiṃ sakinti saggagatisaṅkhātaṃ kāmāvacaradaivakāyaṃ sakiṃ sakiṃ kadāci kadāci upapaj‍janavasaina agacchiṃ| rūpadhātusūti puthuj‍janabhavaggapariyausānaisu rūpabhavaisu arūpadhātusūti arūpabhavaisu| naivasañ‍ñisu asañ‍ñisuṭṭhitanti rūpārūpadhātūsu ca na kaivalaṃ sañ‍ñīsu aiva, atha khau naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīsu asañ‍ñīsu ca upapaj‍ja ṭhitaṃ mayāti ānaitvā yaujaitabbaṃ| naivasañ‍ñiggahaṇaina haittha naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñībhavau gahitau| yadipimai dvai bhavā rūpārūpadhātuggahaṇainaiva gayhanti, yai pana itau bāhirakā tattha nic‍casañ‍ñinau bhavavimaukkhasañ‍ñinau ca, taisaṃ tassā sañ‍ñāya micchābhāvadassanatthaṃ visuṃ gahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aivaṃ dvīhi gāthāhi bhavamūlassa anupacchin‍nattā anādimati saṃsārai attanau vaṭṭadukkhānubhavaṃ dassaitvā idāni tadupacchaidaina vivaṭṭasukhānubhavaṃ dassaintau ‘‘sambhavā’’tiādinā tatiyaṃ gāthamāha| tattha sambhavāti bhavā| kāmabhavādayau aiva hi haitupac‍cayasamavāyaina bhavantīti idha sambhavāti vuttā| suviditāti vipassanāpañ‍ñāsahitāya maggapañ‍ñāya suṭṭhu viditā| asārakātiādi taisaṃ viditākāradassanaṃ| tattha asārakāti nic‍casārādisārarahitā| saṅkhatāti samaic‍ca sambhuyya pac‍cayaihi  katā| pacalitāti saṅkhatattā aiva uppādajarādīhi pakāratau calitā anavaṭṭhitā| sadairitāti sadā sabbakālaṃ bhaṅgaina airitā, ittarā bhaṅgagāminau pabhaṅgunauti atthau| taṃ viditvā  mahamattasambhavanti taṃ yathāvuttaṃ saṅkhatasabhāvaṃ attasambhavaṃ attani sambhūtaṃ attāyattaṃ issarādivasaina aparāyattaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayavasaina ahaṃ viditvā tappaṭipakkhabhūtaṃ santimaiva nibbānamaiva maggapañ‍ñāsatiyā satimā  hutvā samajjhagaṃ adhigacchiṃ ariyamaggabhāvanāya anuppattauti| aivaṃ thairau ñātakānaṃ dhammadaisanāmukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi|

gautamatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

15. hāritatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

yau pubbai karaṇīyānīti āyasmatau hāritatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari parinibbutai tassa citakapūjāya kayiramānāya gandhaina pūjaṃ akāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai sāvatthiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā hāritauti laddhanāmau vayappattau jātimānaṃ nissāya añ‍ñai vasalavādaina samudācarati| sau bhikkhūnaṃ santikaṃ gantvā dhammaṃ sutvā paṭiladdhasaddhau pabbajitaupi ciraparicitattā vasalasamudācāraṃ na vissaj‍ji| athaikadivasaṃ satthu santikai dhammaṃ sutvā sañ‍jātasaṃvaigau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā attanau cittappavattiṃ upaparikkhantau mānuddhac‍caviggahitataṃ disvā taṃ pahāya vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.46.63-67) –

‘‘citāsu kurumānāsu, nānāgandhai samāhaṭai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, gandhamuṭṭhimapūjayiṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, citakaṃ yamapūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahā  pana hutvā vimuttisukhaṃ anubhavantau ‘‘yau pubbai karaṇīyānī’’tiādinā tīhi gāthāhi bhikkhūnaṃ auvādadānamukhaina añ‍ñaṃ byākāsi| tāsaṃ atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

hāritatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

16. vimalatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā

pāpamittaiti  āyasmatau vimalatthairassa gāthā| kā uppatti? ayampi purimabuddhaisu katādhikārau tattha tattha bhavai vivaṭṭūpanissayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ upacinantau padumuttarassa bhagavatau kālai kulagaihai nibbattitvā viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau satthari parinibbutai sādhukīḷanadivasaisu vītivattaisu satthu sarīraṃ gahaitvā upāsakaisu jhāpanaṭṭhānaṃ gacchantaisu satthu guṇai āvaj‍jitvā pasan‍namānasau sumanapupphaihi pūjamakāsi| sau taina puñ‍ñakammaina daivamanussaisu saṃsarantau imasmiṃ buddhuppādai bārāṇasiyaṃ brāhmaṇakulai nibbattitvā vimalauti laddhanāmau vayappattau saumamittatthairaṃ nissāya sāsanai pabbajitvā tainaiva ussāhitau vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaitvā nacirassaiva arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ apadānai (apa॰ thaira 2.46.58-62) –

‘‘nīharantai sarīramhi, vaj‍jamānāsu bhairisu|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, paṭṭipupphamapūjayiṃ||

‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daihapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsana’’nti||

arahattaṃ pana patvā attanau sahāyassa bhikkhussa auvādaṃ daintau –

264.

 ‘‘pāpamittai vivaj‍jaitvā, bhajaiyyuttamapuggalaṃ|

auvādai cassa tiṭṭhaiyya, patthaintau acalaṃ sukhaṃ||

265.

 ‘‘parittaṃ  dārumāruyha, yathā sīdai mahaṇṇavai|

aivaṃ kusītamāgamma, sādhujīvīpi sīdati|

tasmā taṃ parivaj‍jaiyya, kusītaṃ hīnavīriyaṃ||

266.

 ‘‘pavivittaihi ariyaihi, pahitattaihi jhāyibhi|

nic‍caṃ āraddhavīriyaihi, paṇḍitaihi sahāvasai’’ti|| –

tissau gāthā abhāsi|

tattha pāpamittaiti akalyāṇamittai asappurisai hīnavīriyai| vivaj‍jaitvāti taṃ abhajanavasaina dūratau vaj‍jaitvā| bhajaiyyuttamapuggalanti sappurisaṃ paṇḍitaṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ auvādānusāsanīgahaṇavasaina  saivaiyya| auvādai cassa tiṭṭhaiyyāti assa kalyāṇamittassa auvādai anusiṭṭhiyaṃ yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍janavasaina tiṭṭhaiyya| patthaintauti ākaṅkhantau| acalaṃ sukhanti nibbānasukhaṃ phalasukhañ‍ca| tampi hi akuppabhāvatau ‘‘acala’’nti vuc‍cati| saisaṃ vuttatthamaiva|

vimalatthairagāthāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

tikanipātavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

paṭhamau bhāgau niṭṭhitau|
